Motivation: 建立二维SW II系统二次对称代数与Laguerre型合流Heun代数之间的明确联系，为超可积系统与特殊函数代数结构之间的关系提供新的理解。

Method: 通过分析系统在Cartesian坐标和抛物坐标下的分离性，识别出Cartesian分离算子Y为Laguerre型算子，抛物积分W=L₂为其代数Heun伙伴，并计算交换子[Y,W]得到Z，进而推导出Y、W、Z之间的闭合关系式，其中H为中心元。

Result: 得到了闭合的交换关系：[Y,Z]=16ω²W-2bY，[W,Z]=6Y²-4HY+2bW+8ω²(1-c²)，其中H为中心元。这直接给出了Laguerre–Heun代数的超可积实现。

Conclusion: 二维SW II系统的二次对称代数等价于Laguerre型合流Heun代数，为该代数结构提供了一个具体的超可积物理实现。

Abstract: We identify the quadratic symmetry algebra of the two-dimensional Smorodinsky--Winternitz II system with a Laguerre-type confluent Heun algebra. The system is separable in Cartesian and parabolic coordinates. The complementary Cartesian separation operator \[
  Y=\partial_y^2-ω^2y^2+\frac{1/4-c^2}{y^2} \] is of Laguerre type, while the parabolic integral \(W=L_2\) is its algebraic Heun partner. With \(Z=[Y,W]\), the defining relations are \[
  [Y,Z]=16ω^2W-2bY,\qquad
  [W,Z]=6Y^2-4HY+2bW+8ω^2(1-c^2), \] where \(H\) is central. This gives a direct superintegrable realization of the Laguerre--Heun algebra.

</details>


### [110] [A first-order formulation for axisymmetric Willmore surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01001)
*Z. C. Tu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了轴对称Willmore曲面的首次积分降阶方程，将二阶问题转化为一阶常微分方程，并基于此提供了分类框架，以球面和Clifford环面为例验证了公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将轴对称Willmore曲面从二阶方程降为一阶，简化求解过程并建立统一的分类方案。

Method: 通过组合两个独立首次积分，推导出关于$\Psi=\sin\psi$和径向坐标$\rho$的一阶常微分方程（含积分常数$C_1,C_2$）。

Result: 得到了一个含两个积分常数的一阶ODE，可分类所有旋转对称Willmore曲面。

Conclusion: 该首次积分形式有效简化了问题，球面和Clifford环面验证了公式的正确性。

Abstract: We show that axisymmetric Willmore surfaces admit a first-order formulation obtained by combining two independent first integrals. If $ρ$ denotes the distance from the axis of revolution and $Ψ=\sinψ$, where $ψ$ is the tangent angle of the generating curve, then the profile satisfies \begin{equation*} \frac{\left[Ψ(ρΨ'-Ψ)^2+2(ρΨ'-Ψ)+2C_1ρ\right]^2}{1-Ψ^2} +\left[(ρΨ'-Ψ)^2-2\right]^2=C_2, \end{equation*} where $C_1$ and $C_2$ are constants of integration and the prime denotes differentiation with respect to $ρ$. This equation reduces the axisymmetric Willmore equation to a first-order ordinary differential equation and provides a convenient classification scheme for Willmore surfaces of revolution. The sphere and the Clifford torus are discussed as elementary checks of the formulation.

</details>


### [111] [On Jean-Marie Souriau's geometric quantization of the relativistic electron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01121)
*Géry de Saxcé*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Souriau《动力系统结构》中关于几何量子化的章节，给出了其中未证明的关键定理的完整证明，并将其应用于自旋1/2相对论性粒子，导出了狄拉克方程、概率流守恒、自旋流守恒，并利用Kaluza-Klein理论系统构建了C、P、T对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Souriau著作中几何量子化章节的重要结果和公式缺乏证明，难以验证，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 回顾余伴随轨道方法及其在自旋相对论性粒子中的应用；陈述并证明两个关键定理，用于装备预量子流形的辛和接触结构；应用Kaluza-Klein理论系统构建C、P、T对称性。

Result: 导出了狄拉克方程、概率流守恒；提出了自旋流守恒恒等式；给出了比经典表述更便捷、可读性更强的C、P、T对称性构造。

Conclusion: 本文为Souriau几何量子化框架提供了严格的证明基础，并在自旋1/2粒子上成功验证，同时提出了更清晰的对称性构造方案。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to revisit, in Souriau's book "Structure of Dynamical Systems", the chapter devoted to the geometric quantization where the justifications of important results and formulae are not given and are difficult to prove. After recalling the coadjoint orbit method and its application to the relativistic particle with spin, we state and prove two keystone theorems that allow to equip its prequantum manifold with the symplectic and contact structures. We apply them to the relativistic particle with spin 1/2, leading to the Dirac equation and the conservation of the probability current. We propose also an identity of conservation of the spin current and, invoking the Kaluza-Klein theory, a systematic construction of the symmetries of charge conjugation, parity transformation and time reversal which seems to us more convenient and readable than that of the classical presentations.

</details>


### [112] [The Schwinger-Dyson equations for random fuzzy geometries coupled to matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01343)
*Jeremy Gamble,Masoud Khalkhali,Nathan Pagliaroli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了来自(0,1)型随机模糊几何与费米子或玻色子耦合的Schwinger-Dyson方程和矩阵积分的鞍点方程。利用复分析技术从鞍点方程推导出Schwinger-Dyson方程，对于任意势能项均可迭代求解。在玻色子和费米子高斯模型中，严格推导了用椭圆积分表示的自由能和一阶矩公式，其中玻色子情形与Hoppe模型和三色模型密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解(0,1)型随机模糊几何与费米子或玻色子耦合的矩阵模型的解析性质，特别是其Schwinger-Dyson方程和鞍点方程的求解方法。

Method: 采用复分析技术从鞍点方程导出Schwinger-Dyson方程，对任意势能项进行迭代求解；对高斯模型（玻色子和费米子）使用椭圆积分严格推导自由能和一阶矩公式。

Result: 成功推导并求解了Schwinger-Dyson方程；得到了高斯模型下玻色子和费米子情形的自由能和一阶矩的椭圆积分表达式；揭示了玻色子情形与Hoppe模型和三色模型的联系。

Conclusion: 本文为(0,1)型随机模糊几何的矩阵模型提供了系统的解析求解框架，其与Hoppe模型和三色模型的关联为进一步研究模糊几何的物理性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this work we study the Schwinger-Dyson equations and saddle point equations of matrix integrals that come from type $(0,1)$ random fuzzy geometries coupled to fermions or bosons. Such random fuzzy geometries are bi-tracial Hermitian matrix ensembles with a determinant contribution in the integrand. We derive the Schwinger-Dyson equations using complex analytic techniques from the saddle point equation. For arbitrary potentials with either bosonic or fermionic contributions, their Schwinger-Dyson equations can be solved iteratively. For both the Gaussian models with either one boson or fermion we rigorously derive the formula for the free energy and first moment in terms of elliptic integrals. In the bosonic case this solution is closely related to the Hoppe model and the three-colour model.

</details>


### [113] [A review on the kinetic theory of oscillator chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01358)
*Pierre Germain,Joonhyun La,Angeliki Menegaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了一维非线性振子链的动理论，涵盖从微观到介观再到宏观的极限推导（动理学波方程与流体力学极限），讨论了与FPUT悖论及傅里叶定律推导的联系，并提出了开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理一维非线性振子链的动理论发展，特别是从微观动力学到介观动理学波方程、再到宏观流体力学极限的推导过程，并将其与FPUT悖论和傅里叶定律推导这两个数学物理经典问题联系起来。

Method: 采用动理论方法，通过非线性振子链的动力学分析，推导动理学波方程（微观到介观）以及流体力学极限（介观到宏观）。文中提供了详细的推导说明（非严格）以及现有数学理论的严格证明综述。

Result: 呈现了动理学波方程和流体力学极限的推导框架，阐述了与FPUT悖论及傅里叶定律推导的关联，并整理了数学理论的最新进展。

Conclusion: 一维非线性振子链的动理论为理解能量输运和热化过程提供了重要框架，但仍有许多开放问题待解决，文章对未来研究方向提出了建议。

Abstract: We review the kinetic theory of one-dimensional nonlinear oscillator chains, of which the most famous example is the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou equation. We provide detailed, though not rigorous, accounts of the microscopic to mesoscopic, and mesoscopic to macroscopic limits: derivation of the kinetic wave equation and hydrodynamic limit. We also present the state of the art of the mathematical theory, including proofs. We discuss the connection to two famous problems of Mathematical Physics: the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou paradox, and the derivation of Fourier's law. Finally, many open problems and possible directions for future research are proposed.

</details>


### [114] [Exact spectrum of the XX spin chain with constrained non-diagonal boundary fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01918)
*Changqing Liu,Xiaotian Xu,Xin Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究了具有约束非对角边界场的XX自旋链的精确谱，通过分析Bethe Ansatz方程，发现Bethe根具有确定奇偶性且位于单值函数零点。基于数值观测分析了基态和第一激发态的Bethe根构型，证明基本激发由一对Bethe根的协同变化表征，并给出了热力学极限下基态能量的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有约束非对角边界场的XX自旋链的精确谱，这类系统因边界场非对角而难以通过传统Bethe Ansatz直接求解，具有重要的理论挑战性。

Method: 采用Bethe Ansatz方法求解，分析Bethe根的位置与奇偶性约束，结合数值观测研究根构型，推导热力学极限下的基态能量解析表达式。

Result: 发现Bethe根具有确定奇偶性且全部位于单值函数零点；基态与第一激发态的Bethe根构型存在特定模式；基本激发表现为一对Bethe根的协同变化；获得了热力学极限下基态能量的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 成功解析了约束非对角边界XX自旋链的精确谱，揭示了Bethe根构型与激发态的关联，为可积边界问题提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the exact spectrum of the XX spin chain with constrained non-diagonal boundary fields, which can be analyzed by solving the associated Bethe Ansatz equations. In these equations, the number of Bethe roots has a definite parity, and all Bethe roots are located at the zeros of a unary function. We investigate the possible positions of the Bethe roots. Based on numerical observations, we analyze the Bethe root configurations for the ground state and the first excited state. Our results show that elementary excitations are characterized by the cooperative change of a pair of Bethe roots. Furthermore, we obtain an analytical expression for the ground state energy in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [115] [Painlevé XXXIV Asymptotics for the Focusing mKdV Equation with Finite-Genus Background and Discrete Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01977)
*Ruihong Ma,Engui Fan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究聚焦修正Korteweg-de Vries(mKdV)方程在有限亏格代数几何拟周期初值下的Cauchy问题，通过Deift-Zhou非线性最速下降法，推导了临界区域（复稳相点与有限亏格分支割线端点重合）的长时渐近行为，利用Painlevé XXXIV局部参数化解快碰撞，并纳入离散谱（呼吸子），得到误差阶为O(t^{-1/2})的一致展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解聚焦mKdV方程在临界区域的长时渐近行为，特别是当复稳相点与有限亏格代数几何背景的分支割线端点重合时，解的结构及其与呼吸子相互作用的机制。

Method: 采用Deift-Zhou非线性最速下降法处理关联的Riemann-Hilbert问题；在临界区域使用Painlevé XXXIV局部参数化解快稳相点与分支割线端点的碰撞；将离散谱（呼吸子）纳入渐近分析。

Result: 得到了临界区域中长时渐近展开式，误差阶为O(t^{-1/2})；首项由有限亏格代数几何背景和呼吸子组成，呼吸子参数被背景解缓慢调制。

Conclusion: 成功建立了聚焦mKdV方程在临界区域的长时渐近行为，揭示了呼吸子与有限亏格背景的耦合机制，验证了Painlevé XXXIV参数化在这类问题中的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate the Cauchy problem for the focusing modified Korteweg--de Vries (mKdV) equation with finite-genus algebro-geometric quasi-periodic initial data. By applying the nonlinear steepest-descent method of Deift--Zhou to the associated Riemann--Hilbert (RH) problem, we derive the long-time asymptotics of the solution in the critical regime where complex stationary phase points coalesce with the endpoints of the finite-genus branch cuts. The collision is resolved via a local Painlevé XXXIV parametrix, and the discrete spectrum (breathers) is incorporated into the analysis. The resulting expansion is valid uniformly up to an error of order $\mathcal{O}(t^{-1/2})$. In this critical region, the leading-order term comprises the finite-genus algebro-geometric background together with breathers, whose parameters are slowly modulated by the background solution.

</details>


### [116] [Higher-Rank Orthogonal Twists, APS Boundary Conditions, and $O(2)$-Equivariant Spectral Flow on a Warped Cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02186)
*Taro Kimura,Sanchita Sharma*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限扭曲圆柱上Dirac算子的O(2)-等变谱流，通过将扭曲丛复化并对角化正交扭转，将Dirac方程分解为标量Fourier模径向方程，得到块状交叉贡献构成的RO(O(2))-值谱流公式，并讨论了秩三情况下的端点η/APS指标解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究O(2)-等变谱流，建立其块状公式，并揭示普通整数值谱流如何丢失表示论信息，从而在更高的对称性背景下精细刻画谱流现象。

Method: 采用有限扭曲圆柱上的Dirac算子，配备容许的正则化APS边界条件；通过对实高阶正交扭曲丛复化并对角化正交扭转，将Dirac方程分解为标量Fourier模径向方程，分离出运动旋转块与静止中性块；再通过共轭与反射配对的块重组，构建RO(O(2))-值谱流公式。

Result: 在标准自伴Fredholm、端点可逆性、正规交叉假设以及固定中性扇区约定的条件下，获得了明确的块状RO(O(2))-值谱流公式，该公式通过组合分离块的局部交叉贡献得到，并展示了维数映射RO(O(2))→Z如何丢失表示论信息。

Conclusion: 所得RO(O(2))-值谱流公式是对普通整数值谱流的一种细化，揭示了更高对称性下谱流的精细结构；在秩三情况下，讨论了固定中性扇区的作用及对应的端点η/APS指标解释。

Abstract: We study $O(2)$-equivariant spectral flow for Dirac operators on a finite warped cylinder equipped with fixed admissible regularized APS boundary conditions. The twisting bundle is a real higher-rank orthogonal bundle, and reflection symmetry is implemented by a fiber involution. After complexifying the twisting bundle and diagonalizing the orthogonal twist, the Dirac equation decomposes into a scalar Fourier-mode radial equation, with moving rotating blocks and stationary neutral blocks. After regrouping conjugate and reflection-paired blocks, the crossing contributions define real $RO(O(2))$-classes. Consequently, we obtain an explicit blockwise formula for the $RO(O(2))$-valued spectral flow of the resulting regularized APS family. Under the standard self-adjoint Fredholm, endpoint-invertibility, and regular-crossing hypotheses, together with a fixed neutral-sector convention, this formula is obtained by assembling the local crossing contributions of the separated blocks. It refines ordinary integer-valued spectral flow and shows explicitly how the dimension map $RO(O(2))\to\mathbb Z$ loses representation-theoretic information. We also discuss the rank-three case, including the role of the fixed neutral sector, and the corresponding endpoint $η$/APS index interpretation.

</details>


### [117] [Exponential thermalisation of viscous fluids on negatively curved manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02286)
*Samuel L. Braunstein,Zhi-Wei Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在具有严格负 Ricci 曲率的紧致黎曼流形上，建立了包含热涨落的随机 Navier-Stokes 方程，证明了其谱截断系统的唯一平稳分布为 Gibbs 测度，且收敛到平衡的指数速率由变形拉普拉斯的谱隙决定，该谱隙在负曲率背景下与区域体积无关，而平坦空间中热化速率在无限体积极限下趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性不可压 Navier-Stokes 方程在物理上是不完备的：有限温度下的粘性流体必然存在热涨落，其形式由涨落-耗散定理决定。本文旨在为负曲率流形上的粘性流体提供严格的统计力学基础，并揭示几何对热平衡动力学的控制作用。

Method: 基于运动学选择的变形拉普拉斯算子，在严格负 Ricci 曲率的紧致黎曼流形上建立随机 Navier-Stokes 方程；利用拓扑（Poincaré 引理）论证从粘性算子唯一推导出噪声的涨落-耗散关系；对谱截断系统进行数学分析，证明平稳分布与收敛速率。

Result: 谱截断系统的唯一平稳分布为 Gibbs 测度（模振幅高斯分布）；收敛到平衡的指数速率至少为 $2νλ_\Def$；变形拉普拉斯的谱隙满足 $λ_\Def \geq κ^2$（当 $\Ric \leq -κ^2 g$ 时），且与区域体积无关；平衡速度-速度相关函数随测地距离指数衰减（平坦空间中为代数衰减）。

Conclusion: 负曲率几何不仅控制确定性动力学，还控制热平衡的趋近过程，为负曲率流形上粘性流体的统计力学提供了严格基础，揭示了流形几何对热化速率和关联函数的关键影响。

Abstract: The deterministic incompressible Navier-Stokes equations are physically incomplete: any viscous fluid at finite temperature must exhibit thermal fluctuations whose form is dictated by the fluctuation-dissipation relation. We formulate the stochastic Navier-Stokes equations with the kinematically selected deformation Laplacian on compact Riemannian manifolds with strictly negative Ricci curvature. The fluctuation-dissipation relation, derived from a topological (Poincaré lemma) argument, uniquely determines the noise from the viscous operator. For the spectrally truncated system, we prove that the unique stationary distribution is the Gibbs measure (Gaussian in the mode amplitudes, because the nonlinear convective terms preserve energy), and that convergence to equilibrium is exponentially fast with rate at least $2νλ_\Def$, where $ν$ is the kinematic viscosity and $λ_\Def$ is the spectral gap of the deformation Laplacian. The spectral gap satisfies $λ_\Def \geq κ^2$ when $\Ric \leq -κ^2 g$, and is independent of the volume of the domain. On flat space, the analogous thermalisation rate vanishes in the infinite-volume limit. The equilibrium velocity-velocity correlation function decays exponentially in geodesic distance, in contrast to the algebraic decay on flat space. These results provide a rigorous statistical-mechanical foundation for viscous fluids on negatively curved manifolds and illustrate how the geometry of the domain controls not only the deterministic dynamics but also the approach to thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [118] [There Are No Dirac Mass Terms with de Sitter Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02477)
*Craig McRae*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在 de Sitter 群的旋量表示中，满足必要性质的宇称算符 $mathcal{P}$ 和时间反演算符 $mathcal{T}$ 必然导致费米子自耦合为奇宇称，从而排除了任何 de Sitter 对称的自旋-1/2 费米子理论中的标准 Dirac 质量项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Lorentz 正交群的表示论中，宇称算符 $mathcal{P}$ 和时间反演算符 $mathcal{T}$ 分别应实现为线性算符和反线性算符（Wigner, 1932）。作者希望将这一结论推广到 de Sitter 群的情形，并检验其对费米子质量项的影响。

Method: 作者在 de Sitter 群的旋量表示中，严格考察满足 Wigner 所提出的必要性质的 $mathcal{P}$ 和 $mathcal{T}$ 算符，并分析这些算符对费米子自耦合项（特别是质量项）的宇称约束。

Result: 在 de Sitter 群框架下，满足必要条件的 $mathcal{P}$ 和 $mathcal{T}$ 算符会导致自旋-1/2 费米子的自耦合项必然是奇宇称的，这使得标准 Dirac 质量项（通常为偶宇称）在 de Sitter 对称的理论中无法存在。

Conclusion: 任何保持 de Sitter 对称性的自旋-1/2 费米子理论都无法包含标准的 Dirac 质量项，这对于 de Sitter 时空中的量子场论和基本粒子物理具有重要约束意义。

Abstract: In the representation theory of Lorentzian orthogonal groups there are well known arguments as to why the parity operator $\mathcal{P}$ and the time reversal operator $\mathcal{T}$ should be realized as linear and anti-linear operators respectively (Wigner 1932). In this paper it is shown that the only operators satisfying the requisite properties for the spinor representation of the de Sitter group lead to fermion self-couplings which are necessarily parity odd, ruling out standard Dirac mass terms from any de Sitter symmetric theory of spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ fermions.

</details>
