### [119] [The Brøndsted-Rockafellar theorem in geodesic spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00283)
*Alberto Domínguez Corella,Alejandro Villegas-Acuña*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在一般测地度量空间中给出了Brøndsted-Rockafellar定理的构造性版本，并应用于Caristi定理的构造性形式以及度量斜率误差界与泛函全局增长之间的量化关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Brøndsted-Rockafellar定理通常依赖非构造性方法（如选择公理），本文旨在将其推广到更一般的测地度量空间，并给出构造性证明，以便在非线性分析和优化中获得可计算的应用。

Method: 采用构造性分析的方法，在一般测地度量空间中构建Brøndsted-Rockafellar定理的构造性版本，并利用该结果推导Caristi定理的构造性形式，同时建立度量斜率误差界与泛函全局增长之间的量化关系。

Result: 成功给出了Brøndsted-Rockafellar定理在一般测地度量空间中的构造性版本，并得到了Caristi定理的构造性形式，以及度量斜率误差界与泛函全局增长之间的定量关系。

Conclusion: 本文工作为非线性分析和变分分析中的构造性方法提供了理论基础，特别是在测地度量空间中的优化问题和非光滑分析中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We present a constructive version of the Brøndsted-Rockafellar theorem in general geodesic metric spaces. Applications include a constructive form of the Caristi theorem and quantitative relations between metric slope error bounds and the global growth of functionals.

</details>


### [120] [Clustering-enhanced adaptive Benders decomposition for energy systems planning optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00388)
*Jun Wen Law,Dharik S. Mallapragada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出聚类增强的Benders分解方法，通过聚类相似子问题来生成聚合割或识别代表性子问题，以解决高分辨率能源系统容量扩展模型中主问题因累积Benders割而成为计算瓶颈、以及子问题并行化不充分的问题。在电力行业CEM上的实验表明，自适应聚合割在弱时间耦合下优于固定分组和基准多割方法，代表性子问题方法在并行资源有限时表现更优，而最优策略取决于耦合强度和计算瓶颈位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率能源系统容量扩展模型（CEM）通常导致大规模MILP问题。Benders分解虽然可扩展，但子问题生成的大量Benders割使主问题成为计算瓶颈；同时子问题并行化不充分时也会产生瓶颈。现有方法缺乏有效解决这两大瓶颈的手段。

Method: 提出聚类增强的Benders分解方法：(1) 聚合割方法——基于双变量的自适应分组和基于外生时间序列的固定分组，将相似子问题的Benders割聚合成更少割；(2) 代表性子问题方法——识别代表性子问题以高频求解。在电力系统CEM上评估不同系统规模、子问题时间长度、CO2政策强度、计算资源和随机场景下的表现。

Result: 自适应聚合割在弱时间耦合下优于固定分组和基准多割方法，在较大系统且子问题时间跨度较短时增益最大；强时间耦合（如年度CO2限制）下基准多割方法最优。代表性子问题法在并行资源有限、子问题求解占主导时优于基准。整体最优策略取决于耦合强度和计算瓶颈位置。

Conclusion: 聚类增强的Benders分解能有效缓解主问题和子问题的计算瓶颈，但最佳策略需根据问题的时间耦合强度和计算资源分布来选择，不存在普适最优方案。

Abstract: High-resolution energy system capacity expansion models (CEMs) for energy transition planning often result in large-scale mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulations. Benders decomposition (BD) offers a scalable solution approach by iteratively solving a master problem (MP) for investment decisions and multiple subproblems (SPs) for operational decisions. However, accumulated Benders cuts generated by the SPs can make MP solution a major computational bottleneck. Incomplete SP parallelization can also introduce further bottlenecks when SPs exceed available CPUs. We develop clustering-enhanced BD methods to address these challenges, by using clustering to group similar SPs for: a) aggregated Benders cut construction and b) identification of representative SPs to be solved most frequently. For grouped-cuts, we examine two adaptive formulations based on dual variables and a fixed-grouping formulation based on exogenous time-series inputs. We evaluate these methods in an electricity-sector CEM across varying system sizes, temporal SP lengths, inter-SP coupling strengths represented by CO2 policy, computational resources, and stochastic settings. Relative to a benchmark regularized multi-cut formulation, adaptive grouped cuts outperform fixed grouping and provide substantial benefits under weak inter-temporal coupling. The largest gains occur in larger systems with shorter SP horizons, where the MP accounts for a greater share of runtime. Their effectiveness declines under strong inter-temporal coupling, such as annual CO2 emissions limits, where the benchmark multi-cut performs best. The representative-SP method outperforms the benchmark under limited parallelization when SP solution dominates runtime. Overall, the preferred BD strategy depends on inter-SP coupling strength and whether computational burden lies in the MP or the SPs.

</details>


### [121] [Constructive interpolation and generalization rates for neural ODEs: a control perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00469)
*Antonio Álvarez-López,Lorenzo Liverani,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从控制论视角研究神经ODE（SA-NODE）的监督回归，证明了其能实现精确插值及“同步单元可控性”（SCC），进而得到与直方图和最近邻估计器相当的泛化风险界，并揭示了显式时间依赖的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从控制论视角推导神经ODE的显式种群风险界，理解其泛化能力的理论机制。

Method: 构造性证明SA-NODE的精确插值和SCC性质；利用SCC将插值提升为定量泛化（模拟分段常数非参数估计器）；对比分析自主与非自主NODE的结构差异。

Result: SA-NODE的风险界恢复直方图和最近邻估计器的收敛速率；实验验证SA-NODE达到竞争性测试误差；自主NODE因结构障碍无法实现SCC。

Conclusion: 显式时间依赖对实现SCC和良好泛化至关重要，SA-NODE是学习的最小有效架构。

Abstract: We study supervised regression with neural ODEs (NODEs) from a control-theoretic perspective to derive explicit population-risk bounds. We focus on a widely used class of non-autonomous models with constant parameters and explicit time dependence, which we call semi-autonomous NODEs (SA-NODEs). We constructively prove that SA-NODEs are capable of \emph{exact} interpolation of admissible finite datasets, and even satisfy a stronger property that we call \emph{simultaneous cell controllability} (SCC): their flows can map prescribed disjoint cells into arbitrarily small target balls. This property is the mechanism that upgrades interpolation into quantitative generalization, by allowing SA-NODEs to emulate piecewise-constant nonparametric estimators. Consequently, our risk bounds recover the rates of histogram and nearest-neighbor estimators, provided the network width satisfies a conservative scaling with the sample size. Numerical experiments show that trained SA-NODEs achieve competitive -- often lower -- test errors than these baselines. Finally, we show that the explicit time dependence is essential. Although two-layer autonomous NODEs can interpolate geometrically nondegenerate datasets, structural obstructions prevent them from achieving SCC. These limitations, further confirmed numerically, support the view that SA-NODEs provide a minimal effective architecture for learning.

</details>


### [122] [Verifiable Criteria and Properties for Interval $B_π^{R^I}$-Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00475)
*Li Ye,Yisheng Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将 $B_π^{R}$-张量推广到区间设置，引入区间 $B_π^{R^I}$-张量，给出了两种可验证的判定准则，建立了与区间 $P$-张量、正定区间张量和区间 $Z$-张量的联系，并讨论了其在多项式优化和区间张量互补问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 $B_π^{R}$-张量概念自然地推广到区间张量框架下，以处理具有不确定性的张量问题。

Method: 提出了两种实用的可验证判定准则：(1) 基于端点不等式的准则；(2) 构造显式向量 $π$ 的准则。并通过建立与区间 $P$-张量、正定区间张量、区间 $Z$-张量之间的理论联系来刻画该类张量的性质。

Result: 成功给出了区间 $B_π^{R^I}$-张量的两种可验证判定条件，并揭示了其与区间 $P$-张量、正定区间张量及区间 $Z$-张量之间的内在关联。

Conclusion: 该工作为区间张量理论提供了新工具，在多项式优化和区间张量互补问题中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces interval $B_π^{R^I}$-tensors as a natural extension of $B_π^{R}$-tensors to the interval setting. We provide two practical verifiable criteria for an interval tensor to be an interval $B_π^{R^I}$-tensor, one based on endpoint inequalities and another constructing an explicit vector $π$. Connections with interval $P$-tensors, positive definite interval tensors, and interval $Z$-tensors are established. Applications in polynomial optimization and interval tensor complementarity problems are briefly discussed.

</details>


### [123] [In-Expectation Convergence of Stochastic Gradient Methods under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00520)
*Zijian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了重尾噪声下的随机优化问题，证明了标准SMD、ASMD在凸优化中以及SGD、SGDM在非凸优化中均能在期望意义上收敛，无需修改算法或施加有界域等限制性假设，并提出了新的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明重尾噪声（p∈(1,2)）下许多随机梯度方法被认为不收敛，但近期发现SGD在凸有界域问题中可以收敛。受此启发，本文旨在系统性地研究标准随机梯度方法在重尾噪声下的收敛性质，并建立更通用的分析框架。

Method: 本文为SMD和ASMD（凸优化）以及SGD和SGDM（非凸优化）建立了新的期望收敛性分析，不依赖算法修改或有界域等限制性假设，提出了一个优雅且强大的重尾随机优化分析框架。

Result: 证明了标准SMD和ASMD在凸优化中、标准SGD和SGDM在非凸优化中，在重尾噪声下均能在期望意义上收敛，且结果不要求有界域等假设。

Conclusion: 本文为理解重尾噪声下的一阶随机梯度方法开辟了新路径，表明经典方法在没有修改的情况下也能在更宽松的条件下收敛，为后续理论分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Many stochastic gradient methods are believed not to converge when the noise in stochastic gradients has only a finite $p$-th moment for $p\in\left(1,2\right)$, a setting known as the heavy-tailed noise assumption. However, some recent studies have found that Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\textsf{SGD}$), without any modification to its update rule, can surprisingly converge in expectation for convex problems with bounded domains, highlighting the potential of classical stochastic gradient methods. Inspired by this recent progress, we provide a comprehensive study of stochastic optimization under heavy-tailed noise and establish new in-expectation convergence results for Stochastic Mirror Descent ($\textsf{SMD}$) and Accelerated Stochastic Mirror Descent ($\textsf{ASMD}$) in convex optimization, and for $\textsf{SGD}$ and Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum ($\textsf{SGDM}$) in nonconvex optimization. Notably, our results not only hold without algorithmic changes but also avoid restrictive assumptions, such as bounded domains, imposed in prior work. More importantly, our analysis provides a new, elegant, and powerful framework for studying heavy-tailed stochastic optimization, opening a new route to understanding first-order stochastic gradient methods.

</details>


### [124] [Minimal intersection radius for $n$ growing, non-homogeneous ellipsoids in $\mathbb{R}^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01548)
*Barbara Giunti,Sean Hill,Felix X. -F. Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了非齐次椭球体的最小相交半径（MIR）问题，提出了基于凸优化的几何方法、二阶锥规划方法以及LP型问题表述，并通过实验对比了不同求解器的性能，证明了MIR与最小包围椭球问题不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意维度空间中，计算非齐次、可增长椭球体之间的最小相交半径是一个具有理论意义和实际应用价值的问题，现有文献中缺乏系统的计算方法，且与最小包围椭球问题的关系尚不明确。

Method: 1) 提出基于凸优化的几何方法用于计算MIR；2) 提出基于二阶锥规划（SOCP）的辅助方法；3) 将MIR表述为LP型问题，并将凸优化计算结果作为验证证书；4) 使用不同凸求解器进行基准测试，对比有无LP设置下的性能。

Result: 实现了多种MIR计算方法，并在不同凸求解器上进行了基准测试，比较了LP设置与非LP设置下的计算性能。

Conclusion: MIR问题与最小包围椭球问题在一般情况下不等价，需要独立的求解方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we compute the Minimal Intersection Radius (MIR) of growing, non-homogeneous ellipsoids in arbitrary ambient dimension. We provide a geometric method to find the MIR using techniques from convex optimization, a secondary method using second-order cone programs, and show that the MIR can be phrased as an LP-type problem, where the computation from convex optimization acts as a certificate. We implement these methods and benchmark them using different convex solvers, and with or without the LP setting. We also provide a comparison with similar but different problems that appeared in the literature, and show that finding the MIR is, in general, not equivalent to finding the minimal enclosing ellipsoid.

</details>


### [125] [Stability results for regularized least-squares problems via generalized Hessian expressions and monotone generalized equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00526)
*Leo Smulansky,Tim Hoheisel,Tran T. A. Nghia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究凸正则化最小二乘问题解映射的扰动与稳定性。通过建立基于极大单调算子和光滑扰动的广义方程隐函数定理，给出解映射的局部Lipschitz连续性、方向可微性与半光滑性条件。在C²-锥可约性假设下，通过子空间刻画凸函数广义Hessian的核，将复杂二阶条件转化为易处理的一阶条件。应用于加权多面体支撑函数正则化器和分段线性二次罚函数，统一并推广了LASSO型模型和单调广义方程的Lipschitz稳定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正则化最小二乘问题的稳定性分析缺乏统一框架，传统二阶条件计算复杂。作者希望通过建立广义方程隐函数定理和简化二阶条件的刻画，提供一种能够统一处理多种正则化器（如LASSO、加权多面体支撑函数、分段线性二次罚函数）的稳定性分析工具。

Method: 1. 建立针对极大单调算子与光滑扰动的广义方程隐函数定理；2. 在C²-锥可约性假设下，通过子空间刻画凸函数广义Hessian的核，将二阶条件转化为一阶条件；3. 将理论应用于加权多面体支撑函数正则化器和分段线性二次罚函数等具体问题。

Result: 得到了解映射局部Lipschitz连续性、方向可微性与半光滑性的充分条件；建立了广义Hessian核与子空间的等价关系；为多类正则化最小二乘问题提供了统一的稳定性分析框架。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了LASSO型模型和单调广义方程领域的多个近期Lipschitz稳定性结果，为凸正则化问题的理论分析提供了更简洁有效的工具。

Abstract: We study perturbation and stability properties of solution mappings associated with convex regularized least-squares problems. We first establish an implicit function theorem for generalized equations governed by maximally monotone operators and smooth perturbations, yielding conditions for local Lipschitz continuity, directional differentiability, and semismoothness of solution mappings. We then characterize the kernel of generalized Hessians of convex functions through the subspace parallel to the subdifferential under $C^2$-cone reducibility assumptions on the conjugate of the regularizer, thereby replacing difficult second-order objects by tractable first-order conditions. As applications, we derive stability results for broad classes of regularized least-squares problems, including weighted polyhedral support-function regularizers and piecewise linear-quadratic penalties. Our framework unifies and extends several recent results on Lipschitz stability for LASSO-type models and monotone generalized equations.

</details>


### [126] [A path-following framework on fiber bundle for variational inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00778)
*Hongbo Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于有限维变分不等式问题的路径跟踪框架，通过将一般VI约简为单纯形上的光滑VI并在不动点丛上建模，实现了无需单调性假设的全局线性收敛，在14400个随机生成的VI实例（最高800维）上均成功收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分不等式是许多经典问题的基本数学框架，但现有方法通常需要单调性等强假设，缺乏对一般VI（任意连续函数、紧凸域）的高效且保证收敛的算法。

Method: 1) 将一般VI近似约简为单纯形上的光滑VI；2) 在名为"不动点丛"的纤维丛上重新表述该光滑VI；3) 利用该几何结构系统地整合起始点选择、路径跟踪和奇异性规避。核心创新在于不动点丛的几何视角。

Result: 对非奇异解保证全局线性收敛；对奇异解，到固定精度前为线性减少，之后随精度要求提高变为次线性收敛。在14400个随机VI实例（最高800维）中全部成功，迭代次数随维度增长温和。

Conclusion: 该路径跟踪框架无需单调性等假设，即可为大规模一般VI提供全局收敛保证与高效数值表现，在理论和实践上均有显著优势。

Abstract: Variational inequality (VI) is a fundamental mathematical framework for many classical problems. We present a path-following framework for finite-dimensional VIs with arbitrary continuous functions and compact convex domains. The approach first approximately reduces a general VI to a smooth VI on simplex. Its key innovation is to formulate the smooth VI on simplex on a fiber bundle called the fixed-point bundle. Exploiting this geometric structure, we systematically integrate starting point selection, path-following, and singularity avoidance. Without any assumptions such as monotonicity, the algorithm guarantees global linear convergence to nonsingular solutions. For singular solutions, it retains global linear reduction up to a fixed precision, after which convergence becomes sublinear as the required precision increases. In numerical experiments on 14400 randomly generated VIs of up to 800 dimensions, the algorithm succeeds in every instance, and iteration number increases only mildly with the dimension.

</details>


### [127] [Coupled Routing and Charge Schedule Optimization of Electrified Delivery Truck Fleets: Feasibility Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00792)
*Yared Tadesse Wendimagegnehu,Beshah Ayalew,Andrej Ivanco,Habtamu Hailemichael*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了城市配送车队电动化中的路径规划与充电调度联合优化问题，通过构建基于数据驱动的能耗模型，分析了电池容量、成本、电价等因素对车队电动化总成本的影响，发现存在最优电池容量，且在电池成本下降和优惠电价条件下电动化具备可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动化卡车车队有潜力提升能效并减少货运碳排放，但当前电池技术的续航限制和高昂资本成本给物流公司带来成本可行性挑战，因此需要探索可行的电动化路径。

Method: 首先，基于大量行驶工况的详细动力总成仿真，采用数据驱动方法构建了Class 7-8电动和柴油卡车的改进能耗模型。然后，在不同电动化渗透率下，分析电池容量、成本及电价对车队日均摊销总成本的影响，并考虑 depot 处快充可用性。

Result: 当前电池技术能量密度下，存在一个最优电池容量，这是由增大电池容量对成本、寿命、重量和能耗的矛盾效应决定的。同时，在电池成本下降趋势和 depot 优惠电价（如可再生能源微电网）条件下，即使在低渗透率水平，车队电动化也可具备经济可行性。

Conclusion: 电池成本下降和优惠电价是推动车队电动化经济可行的关键因素，且最优电池容量的存在为实际部署提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Electrifying truck fleets has the potential to improve energy efficiency and reduce carbon emissions from the freight transportation sector. However, the range limitations and substantial capital costs with current battery technologies imposes constraints that challenge the overall cost feasibility of electrifying fleets for logistics companies. In this paper, we investigate the coupled routing and charge scheduling optimization of a delivery fleet serving a large urban area as one approach to discovering feasible pathways. To this end, we first build an improved energy consumption model for a Class 7-8 electric and diesel truck using a data-driven approach of generating energy consumption data from detailed powertrain simulations on numerous drive cycles. We then conduct several analyses on the impact of battery pack capacity, cost, and electricity prices on the amortized daily total cost of fleet electrification at different penetration levels, considering availability of fast charging at the depot. Findings indicate that at typical energy density of current battery technology, there is an optimal battery pack capacity that results from the contradicting effects of increasing pack capacity on cost, life span, weight and energy consumption. It is also observed that with currently improving trends in battery pack costs and availability of reduced electricity prices at the depot, such as with renewable microgrids, fleet electrification can become viable even at low levels of penetration.

</details>


### [128] [Frank-Wolfe with Moreau Envelope Smoothing for Nonsmooth Nonconvex Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00853)
*Antonio Silveti-Falls,Cesare Molinari,Zev Woodstock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了FRAMES算法（Frank-Wolfe with Moreau Envelope Smoothing），用于求解非光滑非凸约束优化问题。该算法通过Moreau包络进行迭代平滑，随后每步执行一次Frank-Wolfe迭代。问题模板涵盖多凸约束集的分割问题以及带有非光滑弱凸正则化项（如MCP或SCAD惩罚）的问题。在多种温和假设下（包括不一致问题情形），证明了收敛性及收敛速率。此外，揭示了非光滑目标问题的Frank-Wolfe间隙与平滑代理问题的Frank-Wolfe间隙之间的新关系，指出先前分析的次优性。在矩阵分解问题和多凸约束集上的非凸二次分割问题上进行了数值实验，实证验证了分析改进的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑非凸约束优化问题在机器学习和统计学习中有广泛应用，但现有方法在处理此类问题时存在理论分析不完善或收敛速率不理想的问题。特别是对带有弱凸正则化项（如MCP、SCAD）的问题以及不一致问题（可能是不可行或不一致约束集合的情形）缺乏系统的收敛性分析。同时，先前关于Frank-Wolfe间隙用于非光滑问题的分析存在次优性，需要更精确的理论刻画。

Method: 提出FRAMES算法，将Moreau包络平滑技术与Frank-Wolfe方法相结合：每轮迭代先对非光滑目标函数构造Moreau包络（迭代平滑），然后对平滑后的代理问题执行一次Frank-Wolfe步。算法设计使得平滑参数随迭代动态调整。

Result: 在多种温和假设下（包括不一致问题情形）证明了FRAMES算法的收敛性并给出了收敛速率；建立了非光滑目标Frank-Wolfe间隙与平滑代理问题Frank-Wolfe间隙之间的新关系，揭示了先前分析存在次优性；在矩阵分解和非凸二次分割数值实验中验证了理论改进。

Conclusion: FRAMES算法为求解一类广泛的非光滑非凸约束优化问题提供了统一的理论框架，在更弱的假设下（包括不一致问题）实现了可证明的收敛保证，同时通过新的间隙关系改进了先前的理论分析。数值实验证实了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: We present and analyze Frank-Wolfe with Moreau Envelope Smoothing (FRAMES) for solving nonsmooth nonconvex constrained optimization problems, taking advantage of iterative smoothing via the Moreau envelope followed by one Frank-Wolfe step per iteration. The problem template we consider encompasses splitting problems with multiple convex constraint sets as well as problems with nonsmooth weakly convex regularizers like the MCP or SCAD penalties. We prove convergence, with rates, for both of these cases under a variety of mild assumptions, including inconsistent problems. Additionally, we highlight a new relationship between the Frank-Wolfe gap for a problem with nonsmooth objective and the Frank-Wolfe gap for a smoothed surrogate problem, demonstrating suboptimality of prior analyses. Numerical experiments are performed for matrix factorization problems and nonconvex quadratic splitting over multiple convex constraint sets, where the improvements in analysis are empirically observed.

</details>


### [129] [Constrained Neural Parameterization for Optimization in Function Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00855)
*Michael Hintermüller,Jianfeng Ning*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种约束神经参数化方法，通过构建光滑的神经网络参数化将约束优化问题转化为无约束优化问题，避免了罚参数或拉格朗日乘子，适用于函数空间中的多类约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束优化方法（如罚函数法、拉格朗日乘子法）在处理函数空间中的约束时，需要引入额外参数或乘子，导致优化困难。本文旨在构造一种无需这些辅助手段、可直接进行梯度优化的光滑无约束转换方法。

Method: 构建光滑的神经网络参数化，使其像集完全落在可行集内且渐近稠密。具体包括：针对Hilbert空间中多面体约束集的几何构造、针对逐点约束的光滑神经架构、以及针对可分离结构PDE约束的精确简化神经公式。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性，表明该方法能够成功求解各类约束优化问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束神经参数化方案为函数空间中的约束优化问题提供了一种高效、可微的无约束转化途径，避免了传统方法的罚参数或拉格朗日乘子，具有良好的实用前景。

Abstract: We propose constrained neural parameterization schemes for several classes of constraints arising in optimization problems in function spaces. This is achieved by constructing smooth neural parameterizations whose image lies entirely in the admissible set while remaining asymptotically dense. In this way, the original constrained optimization problem is transformed into a smooth unconstrained problem in parameter space, enabling efficient gradient-based optimization without penalty parameters or Lagrange multipliers. We develop geometric constructions for polyhedral constraint sets in Hilbert spaces, propose smooth neural architectures for some pointwise constraints, and introduce an exact reduced neural formulation for PDE constraints that admit a separable structure. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [130] [Tiny Recursive Models for Solving the J2-Perturbed Lambert Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00895)
*Minduli Wijayatunga,Roberto Armellin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出TRM-PL模型，一种基于Tiny Recursive Models的递归神经网络求解器，用于J2摄动下的Lambert问题。该模型通过重复应用紧凑推理模块在双层潜在层级中迭代细化出发速度，统一了初值生成与迭代修正过程。在三种测试场景（单圈LEO、多圈LEO、多圈Jovian转移）中，仅使用位置监督的细化方法最可靠，中值终端位置误差从21.7km降至0.027km（单圈LEO），从340.9km降至0.31km（多圈LEO），仅需2.3M参数。对Jovian场景，一次牛顿校正后中值误差可达0.063km。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典摄动Lambert求解器依赖于同伦或延续方法等手工设计的路径从开普勒解过渡到摄动解，计算复杂度高且难以端到端学习。本文旨在设计一种可学习的递归细化解法，将初值生成与迭代修正统一于单一可微分架构，以提高求解效率与精度。

Method: 基于Tiny Recursive Models (TRM)构建TRM-PL模型。采用权重共享架构，紧凑推理模块在双层潜在层级中反复应用，通过模拟J2轨迹并计算跟踪误差来迭代修正候选出发速度。支持端到端可微训练，比较三种训练范式：联合学习Lambert解与J2修正、使用目标位置和J2修正速度监督细化、仅使用目标位置监督细化。

Result: 在单圈LEO转移中，中值终端位置误差从21.7km降至0.027km；多圈LEO转移中从340.9km降至0.31km；Jovian多圈转移中，经一次牛顿校正迭代后中值误差达0.063km。全部场景使用相同2.3M参数架构，细化方法表现最可靠。

Conclusion: TRM-PL模型证明了递归神经网络可有效替代经典摄动Lambert求解器中的同伦/延续方案，通过端到端学习实现高精度求解。紧凑的模型规模（2.3M参数）使其适合嵌入式部署，为星上自主轨道计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a fast, recursive neural solver for the J2-perturbed Lambert problem based on Tiny Recursive Models (TRM), termed the TRM-Perturbed Lambert (TRM-PL) model. TRM is a weight-shared architecture whose effective capacity emerges from iteration depth rather than parameter count: a compact reasoning module is applied repeatedly within a two-level latent hierarchy, refining a candidate departure velocity by simulating the J2 trajectory and correcting it from the resulting tracking error. This unifies initial-guess generation and iterative correction in a single, end-to-end differentiable architecture. The recursive refinement loop is a learned alternative to the homotopy and continuation schemes of classical perturbed-Lambert solvers: rather than following a hand-designed path from the Keplerian to the perturbed solution, the network learns its own sequence of corrections. We evaluate TRM-PL on three test cases of increasing difficulty: single-revolution low-Earth-orbit (LEO) transfers, multi-revolution LEO transfers, and multi-revolution Jovian transfers. Three training paradigms are compared: jointly learning the Lambert solution and the J2 correction; refining the Lambert initial velocity with target-position and J2-corrected velocity supervision; and refining it with target-position supervision alone. Across all cases, the refinement-only approaches are the most reliable. The position-supervised variant reduces the median terminal-position error from 21.7 km to 0.027 km on single-revolution LEO, from 340.9 km to 0.31 km on multi-revolution LEO, all with the same 2.3M-parameter architecture. A single Newton corrector iteration on the TRM-PL output tightens the Jovian median to 0.063 km, yielding compact models accurate enough for embedded deployment.

</details>


### [131] [When Shortages Lead to Export Restrictions: A Computational Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00896)
*Martha L. Sabogal De La Pava,Emily L. Tucker,Yongjia Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对出口禁令这一系统性风险，提出了一种将常规中断风险（质量相关产能失效和自然灾害）与出口禁令关联的供应链设计模型。该模型采用两阶段随机混合整数规划，并设计了三种求解方法（交替整数L型切割、交替析取切割、交替双线性切割），其中交替整数L型切割方法在近连续第二阶段场景中表现最佳。通过肿瘤药物案例研究，验证了将系统性诱导风险纳入供应链设计的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制药行业因救命药物的关键性，对出口禁令尤为敏感。现有研究缺乏将常规中断风险与出口禁令这一系统性、诱导性风险关联起来的供应链设计模型。

Method: 构建两阶段随机混合整数规划模型，第一阶段为供应链设计决策，第二阶段在系统性设定下包含一个二元变量和连续变量。针对该结构提出三种求解方法：交替整数L型切割法、交替析取切割法和交替双线性切割法。

Result: 在近连续的第二阶段场景中，交替整数L型切割方法表现最优。通过肿瘤药物案例研究，量化了将系统性诱导风险纳入供应链设计的价值。

Conclusion: 将出口禁令这类系统性诱导风险纳入供应链设计能够显著提升制药供应链的韧性，且交替整数L型切割法为解决此类问题提供了高效的计算工具。

Abstract: Globalization has enabled companies to leverage cost efficiencies; however, it has increased exposure to disruption risks that threaten supply stability. Among these, export bans have emerged as a systemic challenge, often arising as a secondary effect of conventional supply capacity disruptions. The pharmaceutical industry is particularly vulnerable to bans because of the criticality of keeping life-saving medications available, yet such disruptions also jeopardize patient health globally as well as challenge company operations. This paper proposes a supply chain design model that incorporates the relationship between conventional disruption risks (quality-related capacity failures and natural disasters) and export bans that may be induced when the drug is projected to be in short supply. The model is a two-stage stochastic mixed integer program, where the systemic setting yields a second-stage problem with one binary variable and continuous variables. The structure motivates the study of three tailored solution methods: the Alternating Integer L-shaped Cut, Alternating Disjunctive Cut, and Alternating Bilinear Cut methods. In the nearly-continuous second stage context, the Alternating Integer L-shaped cuts perform best. Using an oncology drug case study, we study effects of systemic, induced risk and quantify the value of incorporating disruptions into supply chain design.

</details>


### [132] [Nonsmooth High-Order Averaging Theory with Application to Extremum Seeking Optimization and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00969)
*Hesham Abdelfattah,Sameh A. Eisa,Peter Stechlinski*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑系统提出了一种高阶平均理论和方法，利用广义导数理论和字典序微分理论，首次实现了非光滑二阶平均，并推广到极值搜索控制系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均理论主要针对光滑系统，缺乏适用于非光滑系统的高阶平均工具，限制了非光滑动力系统的分析与控制设计。

Method: 基于广义导数理论构建非光滑近恒等变换；利用字典序微分理论的锐利微积分规则，推导出非光滑一阶和二阶平均的闭式公式；将方法推广到非光滑控制仿射极值搜索系统。

Result: 首次提出非光滑二阶平均理论，该方法在系统光滑时自动退化为经典光滑平均结果，无需额外检查光滑性条件；数值仿真验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了非光滑系统高阶平均理论的空白，为含非光滑向量场或目标函数的极值搜索等控制系统提供了稳定性分析工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a higher-order averaging theory and method for a wide range of nonsmooth systems that are generally characterized by the classical averaging canonical form. Utilizing tools from generalized derivatives theory, we provide a nonsmooth near-identity transformation analogous to the one in smooth averaging theory. Additionally, we exploit sharp calculus rules from lexicographic differentiation theory to provide a closed formula for nonsmooth first-order averaging, and for the first time in the literature, nonsmooth second-order averaging. In fact, our approach recovers the smooth averaging results, without needing to check, if the system under consideration is smooth. Equipped with a nonsmooth second-order averaging theory, we generalize literature results and introduce a class of control-affine extremum seeking systems that tolerate nonsmoothness in the vector fields and/or the objective function by analyzing its stability based on a closed formula analogous to first-order Lie bracket approximations available in the smooth literature. We provide numerical simulation results involving complicated nonsmooth functions to demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [133] [Exact-Penalty Prox-Linear Methods for Bilevel Optimization with $\ell_1$ Lower-Level Gradient Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01055)
*Yutong Zheng,Jiani Li,Qingna Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ℓ1范数精确罚函数的两层优化问题重构方法，并设计了精确罚近线性(EPPL)算法，在简单两层设定下进一步提出了对偶谱投影梯度法，数值实验证明了该方法在精度上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两层优化因下层最优性条件的隐式和集值性质导致求解困难，现有方法难以高效处理非光滑罚问题并保证收敛性。

Method: 采用ℓ1范数下层梯度精确罚函数重构原问题；提出精确罚近线性(EPPL)方法求解非光滑罚问题并建立驻点收敛保证；针对简单两层情形，将子问题等价转化为盒约束二次规划，进而提出对偶谱投影梯度法并证明内层对偶迭代收敛。

Result: 在最小范数最小二乘两层模型上，该方法能高效降低上下层间隙至高精度；与多种现有方法相比，在测试实例上取得了最佳最终解精度。

Conclusion: 基于ℓ1范数精确罚函数的两层优化求解框架理论上具有精确罚性质和收敛保证，实践中能够达到高精度解，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is a fundamental framework for hierarchical decision-making, but its solution is challenging due to the implicit and typically set-valued nature of the lower-level optimality condition. In this paper,
  we study bilevel optimization problems through an exact-penalty reformulation based on the $\ell_1$-norm of the lower-level gradient. Under suitable regularity assumptions,
  we show that this penalty defines a distance-bound function and yields an exact penalty property for sufficiently large penalty parameters.
  To solve the resulting nonsmooth penalized problem, we propose an exact-penalty prox-linear (EPPL) method and establish a stationarity-oriented convergence guarantee. We further specialize the method to the simple bilevel setting, where the subproblem admits an explicit dual reformulation as a box-constrained quadratic program. This structure leads to a dual spectral projected gradient method with closed-form primal recovery, for which convergence of the inner dual iterates is proved.
  Numerical experiments on a minimum-norm least-squares bilevel model show that the proposed method is effective in reducing both the lower-level and upper-level gaps to high accuracy.
  Compared with several existing methods, the proposed approach attains the best final solution accuracy on the tested instance.

</details>


### [134] [Relaxed Control with Entropy Regularization for Itô Stochastic Systems with Input Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01058)
*Ruixue Li,Xun Li,Zhaorong Zhang,Juanjuan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带输入延迟的无限时域经典随机最优控制问题，在熵正则化松弛控制框架下，将问题重构为熵正则化形式，推导出服从高斯分布的最优控制器，并证明当探索权重趋于零时，最优高斯控制分布收敛到最优狄拉克测度，最后通过数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机最优控制问题在无限时域和输入延迟条件下存在求解困难，现有方法对探索与利用的平衡处理不足。作者希望引入熵正则化松弛控制框架，在保证控制性能的同时实现更好的探索行为。

Method: 构建松弛系统并引入熵正则化项，将原始最优控制问题转化为熵正则化形式，从而推导出服从高斯分布的最优控制器。

Result: 获得了服从高斯分布的最优控制器，并证明了当探索权重趋于零时，该高斯分布收敛到最优狄拉克测度（确定性控制）。

Conclusion: 所提出的熵正则化松弛控制框架能有效处理带输入延迟的无限时域随机最优控制问题，具有理论上的收敛保证和良好的数值表现。

Abstract: This paper investigates the infinite-horizon classical stochastic optimal control problem with input delay under an entropy-regularized relaxed control framework. In particular, by constructing a relaxed system and introducing an entropy regularization term, we reformulate the classical optimal control problem into an entropy regularized formulation, and derive the optimal controller that follows a Gaussian distribution. Furthermore, we show that the optimal Gaussian control distribution converges to the optimal Dirac measure as the exploration weight tends to zero. Numerical simulation is provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [135] [A Semi-analytic Method for Rapid Coverage Analysis of a Satellite Using Piecewise Ellipse Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01100)
*Byeong-Un Jo,Jinsung Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分段椭圆模型的半解析方法，通过引入ELAN参数和分段椭圆近似，在不进行重复时域可见性搜索的情况下，高效计算卫星对地覆盖的访问时间和重访时间，在绝大多数情况下误差低于0.1%且大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卫星覆盖分析依赖密集数值计算评估访问和重访时间，在长期性能评估和大规模星座分析中计算成本极高，需要一种更高效的覆盖分析方法。

Method: 提出ELAN（历元升交点经度）参数，利用一个节点周期内地迹形状不变性，通过ELAN确定目标可见性；识别ELAN可行范围排除不可见节点周期；将可见性边界关系转化为MELAN并用分段椭圆模型进行解析可逆近似，直接评估进出时间；利用ELAN周期性演化高效计算访问和重访时间。

Result: 在多种轨道构型和目标纬度下的数值案例中，该方法在绝大多数情况下误差低于0.1%，同时显著降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: 所提出的半解析方法能够高效、准确地进行卫星覆盖分析，适用于长期性能评估和大规模星座场景，为卫星覆盖快速计算提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Satellite coverage analysis commonly relies on intensive numerical computations to evaluate access and revisit times, resulting in high computational costs, particularly in long-term performance assessment and large-scale constellation analysis involving numerous satellites. To address this issue, this paper presents a semi-analytic method for rapid coverage analysis for a satellite using piecewise ellipse models. We introduce the epoch longitude of the ascending node (ELAN), defined as the longitude of the ascending node at the beginning of each nodal period. Since the shape of the ground track over one nodal period remains invariant, target visibility within the period is uniquely determined by ELAN. By identifying the feasible range of ELAN, the proposed method excludes invisible nodal periods, thereby substantially reducing search space. Within the feasible range, we transform the visibility-boundary relationship into a modified ELAN (MELAN) and approximate it using analytically invertible piecewise ellipse models, enabling direct evaluation of the entry and exit times without repeated time-domain visibility searches. By exploiting the periodic evolution of ELAN, the proposed method efficiently computes access and revisit times over a given time horizon. Numerical case studies over a wide range of orbital configurations and target latitudes show that the proposed method achieves sub-0.1% error in most cases while significantly reducing computational effort.

</details>


### [136] [Time-Optimal Collision Avoidance Via a Greedy Polynomial Backward Sweep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01169)
*Zeno Pavanello,Frank De Veld,Roberto Armellin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贪心时间最优后向扫描方法，用于确定低推力卫星在碰撞规避中的最晚机动开始时间，通过逆向传播机动并采用微分代数高效计算，在保持机载适用运行时间的同时实现了接近最优控制基准的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低推力卫星的碰撞规避不仅需要确定如何机动，还需要确定最晚可以在何时开始机动仍能保证安全，现有方法对此问题的求解效率或最优性存在不足。

Method: 从标称最接近时刻开始，逆向迭代传播机动，每一步选择局部最小化危险指标的推力方向；利用微分代数高效传播状态灵敏度并在线更新最接近时刻。

Result: 在大型交会数据集上对两种安全指标（最小距离和碰撞概率）进行测试，方法准确，与最优控制基准相比最优性损失很小，且运行时间适合机载实现。

Conclusion: 提出的GTO后向扫描方法能有效确定最晚机动开始时间，兼顾了求解质量与计算效率，具备机载部署潜力。

Abstract: Spacecraft collision avoidance for low-thrust satellites often requires determining not only how to maneuver, but also how late a maneuver can begin while still ensuring safety. This paper presents a greedy time-optimal (GTO) backward-sweep method to find the latest maneuver initiation time. The method starts from the nominal time of closest approach and iteratively propagates the maneuver backward in time, selecting at each step the thrust direction that locally minimizes the chosen danger metric. Differential algebra is used to efficiently propagate state sensitivities and update the time of closest approach online. The method is tested on a large dataset of conjunctions, using both miss distance and probability of collision as safety metrics. The approach achieves accurate results and only a small loss of optimality relative to an optimal-control benchmark, while retaining runtimes suitable for on-board implementation.

</details>


### [137] [Non-convergence Analysis of Probabilistic Direct Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01320)
*Cunxin Huang,Zaikun Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对概率直接搜索法（一种随机无导数优化方法）提出了非收敛理论，证明在凸优化问题中，若轮询方向满足概率上升条件（与现有收敛理论中的亚鞅条件相反），则非收敛概率为正，并给出了下界。这表明亚鞅类假设对收敛性是本质必要的，而非分析技术的产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究现有概率直接搜索法收敛理论中的亚鞅类假设是否本质必要，即该假设究竟是算法收敛的固有要求，还是仅仅由于分析技术限制导致的人为条件。

Method: 通过构造并分析两个控制迭代点到初始点距离的随机级数，估计这些级数保持在一定界限以下的概率，从而证明非收敛概率的正性并建立下界。

Result: 对于凸优化问题，若轮询方向满足概率上升条件（与亚鞅收敛条件相反），则非收敛概率为正且存在下界。在典型实现中（每次迭代独立均匀地从单位球面抽取固定数量的随机方向），若方向数低于收敛理论阈值，算法不是全局收敛的。

Conclusion: 确认了亚鞅类假设对于概率直接搜索法的收敛性是本质必要的，而非分析技术的产物。

Abstract: We present a non-convergence theory for probabilistic direct search, a randomized derivative-free optimization method, where non-convergence means the failure to produce iterates that achieve stationarity asymptotically. The motivation is to understand whether the submartingale-like assumption in the existing convergence theory is essential or merely an artifact of the analysis techniques. For convex objectives, we prove that the probability of non-convergence is positive, provided that the polling directions satisfy a probabilistic ascent condition that is essentially the opposite of the submartingale-like convergence condition. Furthermore, we establish a lower bound for the non-convergence probability. For the typical implementation of this method, where each iteration draws a fixed number of random polling directions independently and uniformly from the unit sphere, our theory implies that the method is not globally convergent if the number of directions is below the threshold specified in the convergence theory, and the submartingale-like assumption is confirmed to be essential for convergence. Our theory is obtained by examining two random series that control the distance from any iterate to the starting point and estimating the probability for these series to stay below certain bounds.

</details>


### [138] [Global Convergence of a Line-Search Filter Differential Dynamic Programming Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01487)
*Ming Xu,Iman Shames*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了FilterDDP算法的全局收敛性质，该算法将离散时间DDP扩展到处理状态和控制上的非线性约束，采用线搜索过滤过程进行步长接受，并通过反向递归和正向模拟计算试验点。证明了在约束最优控制问题子集中，该反向-正向过程满足与牛顿步相同的性质，从而实现了全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的离散时间DDP算法扩展到能够处理状态和控制上的非线性约束，并建立其全局收敛性保证。

Method: FilterDDP采用线搜索过滤过程进行步长接受，通过反向递归和正向模拟计算试验点，替代了一般非线性规划中的阻尼牛顿步。收敛性分析借鉴了Wächter和Biegler的线搜索过滤方法框架。

Result: 证明了在约束最优控制问题的子集中，FilterDDP的反向-正向过程满足与牛顿步相同的性质，从而建立了算法的全局收敛性。

Conclusion: FilterDDP算法在约束最优控制问题子集上具备全局收敛性，为DDP类方法处理约束问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this article, we establish the global convergence properties of the FilterDDP algorithm, which extends the discrete-time differential dynamic programming (DDP) algorithm of Mayne and Jacobson [\emph{International Journal of Control}, 3, (1966), pp. 85-95] to handle nonlinear constraints over states and controls, in addition to the dynamics. FilterDDP adopts a line-search filter procedure for step acceptance. However, instead of a damped Newton step applied in the general nonlinear programming setting, the computation of a trial point involves applying a backward recursion and a forward simulation. We establish the global convergence of FilterDDP by showing that for a subset of constrained optimal control problems, the this backward-forward procedure satisfies the same properties as a Newton step for the purpose of establishing global convergence of a line-search filter method, following the analysis of Wächter and Biegler [\emph{SIAM Journal on Optimization}, 16 (2005), pp. 1-31].

</details>


### [139] [Inexactly Smooth Performance Estimation and New Optimized Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01505)
*Aaron Zoll,Benjamin Grimmer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类广义的“非精确光滑”凸函数提出统一模型，涵盖了L-光滑、M-Lipschitz、Hölder光滑函数及其组合，并建立了必要且充分（模通用常数）的插值定理，从而能够通过性能估计问题（PEP）分析一阶方法，并扩展了Drori和Taylor的构造性算法设计方法。基于此，得到了$(β,0)$-Hölder光滑问题的精确极小极大最优方法、$(β,p)$-Hölder光滑凸最小化的最佳已知收敛保证方法，以及适用于任何非精确光滑凸问题的新型通用快速回溯方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸优化理论中对函数光滑性的刻画（如L-光滑、Lipschitz、Hölder光滑）各自独立，缺乏一个统一的框架来同时涵盖这些情形及其组合，从而限制了算法设计与分析的通用性。

Method: 1) 提出“非精确光滑”凸函数的统一模型；2) 建立该函数类上的插值定理（必要且充分，模通用常数）；3) 利用插值定理将一阶方法分析转化为性能估计问题（PEP）；4) 扩展Drori和Taylor的构造性方法进行算法设计。

Result: 1) 对$(β,0)$-Hölder光滑问题得到精确极小极大最优方法；2) 对$(β,p)$-Hölder光滑凸最小化得到最佳已知收敛保证（模常数意义下）；3) 提出适用于任何非精确光滑凸问题的通用快速回溯方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的非精确光滑凸函数统一框架及其插值定理为分析和设计一阶优化方法提供了通用工具，能够导出最优或接近最优的算法，展示了该框架的理论价值和实用性。

Abstract: We consider a general class of ``inexactly smooth'' convex functions, providing a universal model capturing as special cases $L$-smooth, $M$-Lipschitz, and Hölder smooth functions, and any combination thereof. Such functions possess a calculus closely following that of smooth functions. Our main results provide inexactly smooth functions with interpolation theorems that are necessary and sufficient up to modest universal constants. These enable analysis of first-order methods for any inexactly smooth convex problem class via solving convex Performance Estimation Problems (PEPs). Further, these enable the extension of Drori and Taylor's constructive approach to algorithm design. From this, we derive an exactly minimax optimal method for $(β,0)$-Hölder smooth problems, methods with the best-known convergence guarantees up to constants for any $(β,p)$-Hölder smooth convex minimization, and a new universal fast backtracking method for any inexactly smooth convex problem.

</details>


### [140] [An arc-search BFGS algorithm for unconstrained nonlinear optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01506)
*Yaguang Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弧搜索BFGS算法，在保持鲁棒BFGS方法全局收敛和超线性收敛特性的前提下，进一步提高计算效率，并通过数值实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典BFGS算法在非凸优化问题中可能无法可靠收敛，现有鲁棒BFGS方法虽已实现全局收敛和超线性收敛，但计算效率仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出弧搜索BFGS算法，在鲁棒BFGS方法的基础上引入弧搜索策略，同时对凸和非凸优化问题进行优化。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提算法在计算效率上优于现有最优算法，同时保留了全局收敛性和超线性收敛速度。

Conclusion: 弧搜索BFGS算法在保持鲁棒BFGS方法理想收敛性质的同时，进一步提升了计算效率，适用于凸和非凸非线性优化问题。

Abstract: The classical BFGS algorithm performs excellently for convex optimization problems. However, for non-convex problems, the classical BFGS method may fail to converge reliably. To overcome this limitation, researchers have developed modified BFGS methods that are applicable to both convex and non-convex optimization problems. Among these methods, a robust BFGS algorithm has been shown to achieve global convergence and fast local convergence, with a superlinear convergence rate, for both convex and non-convex nonlinear optimization problems under mild assumptions. In this paper, we propose an arc-search BFGS algorithm that aims to further improve the computational efficiency of the robust BFGS method while preserving its desirable convergence properties. Numerical experiments are carried out, and performance comparisons between the proposed algorithm and state-of-the-art algorithms are reported to demonstrate the advantages of the arc-search BFGS algorithm.

</details>


### [141] [Inexact Proximal Point and Tseng Algorithms with Nonsummable Errors to Solve Monotone Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01536)
*Ba Khiet Le,Boris S. Mordukhovich,Michel A. Thera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次在文献中证明了非精确近端点算法(IPPA)和非精确Tseng算法(ITA)在Hilbert空间中处理不可和误差时的收敛性，通过Tikhonov正则化、单调算子的压缩性质以及R-连续性理论实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于非精确近端点算法和非精确Tseng算法的收敛性分析通常要求误差可和(summmable)，但在实际应用中误差往往不可和(nonsummable)，因此需要建立更一般的收敛性理论。

Method: 采用Tikhonov正则化技术、单调算子的压缩性质以及近年发展的R-连续性理论来分析算法在不可和误差下的收敛行为。

Result: 首次证明了IPPA和ITA在Hilbert空间中处理不可和误差时的收敛性，且所提技术可推广至其他涉及不可和误差的非精确算法分析中。

Conclusion: 本文为带不可和误差的非精确优化算法提供了新的收敛性分析框架，可拓展应用于多种重要的非精确算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish, for the first time in the literature, the convergence of the practical versions of the Inexact Proximal Point Algorithm (IPPA) and the Inexact Tseng Algorithm (ITA) for computing approximate solutions to monotone inclusions in Hilbert spaces under the the presence of nonsummable errors. Our ap- proach relies on Tikhonov regularization, the contraction property of the associated monotone operators, and the recently developed R-continuity theory. The proposed techniques and results can be extended to analyze various important inexact algorithms in optimization-related problems with nonsummable errors.

</details>


### [142] [MINTS: Minimalist Thompson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01655)
*Kaizheng Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种极简贝叶斯框架，仅对最优值位置设置先验，通过轮廓似然消除 nuisance 参数，并实例化为 MINTS 算法。在均值约束的多臂赌博机问题上，MINTS 实现了近最优非渐近遗憾保证和锐利几乎必然渐近遗憾刻画，在无结构设定下达到经典 Lai-Robbins 常数，且自动适应单峰结构达到仅由最优臂邻域决定的锐利常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯范式在序贯决策中虽有原则性工具，但其依赖对所有参数的概率建模，难以融入复杂结构约束。本文旨在克服这一局限，提出一种仅对最优值位置建模的极简方法。

Method: 提出极简贝叶斯框架：仅对最优值位置设置先验，利用轮廓似然消除 nuisance 参数，得到广义后验。基于此框架实例化 MINTS（MINimalist Thompson Sampling）算法。

Result: 在均值约束的多臂赌博机中，MINTS 达到近最优非渐近遗憾上界；在几乎必然渐近遗憾方面，无结构设定下达到经典 Lai-Robbins 常数，在单峰结构下自动适应，达到仅由最优臂相邻臂决定的锐利常数。

Conclusion: 所提极简贝叶斯框架有效消除了 nuisance 参数建模负担，同时自然容纳结构约束；MINTS 算法在理论和实证上均展现出理想的遗憾性能与自适应能力。

Abstract: The Bayesian paradigm offers principled tools for sequential decision-making under uncertainty, but its reliance on a probabilistic model for all parameters can hinder the incorporation of complex structural constraints. We introduce a minimalist Bayesian framework that places a prior only on the location of the optimum, while eliminating nuisance parameters through profile likelihood. This yields a generalized posterior that naturally accommodates structural constraints. As a direct instantiation, we develop MINimalist Thompson Sampling (MINTS). For multi-armed bandits with mean constraints, we establish near-optimal non-asymptotic regret guarantees and sharp almost-sure asymptotic regret characterizations. In particular, MINTS attains the classical Lai--Robbins constant in the unstructured setting and automatically adapts to unimodal structure, achieving the sharp constant determined only by the immediate neighbors of the optimal arm.

</details>


### [143] [A Unified Variational Design of Predictive Mirror Descent in Convex Games under Stochastic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01729)
*Yunian Pan,Tao Li,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一个带有辅助记忆状态的随机镜像微分博弈，为预测性镜像方法提供了变分统一框架，在局部镜像正则性和Bregman增长条件下建立了有限时域局部终末时间界和高概率退出估计，为稳定均衡附近的最后迭代性能提供了随机保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准镜像下降法在弱稳定机制下最后迭代行为可能失败（出现旋转或循环瞬态），而现有预测性变体多为算法级引入且各自独立分析，缺乏统一的变分构造框架。

Method: 构造一个带有辅助记忆状态的随机镜像微分博弈，其阶段成本耦合两项Fenchel项：基于预测策略的战略项和由实际反馈驱动的校正项。均衡反馈机制产生通用镜像几何下的双通道预测镜像动力学。

Result: 在局部镜像正则性、定量局部Bregman增长条件和有界布朗扩散下，建立了有限时域局部终末时间界的期望和高概率界，以及局部化邻域的退出概率估计。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了预测-记忆镜像流的统一变分构造，并为稳定均衡附近最后迭代性能提供了局部随机保证。

Abstract: Mirror descent provides a geometric framework for learning in games, but its last-iterate behavior can fail in weakly stable regimes, where the dynamics may exhibit rotational or recurrent transients. Predictive mirror methods mitigate this issue by modifying the feedback entering the mirror update, yet standard predictive variants are typically introduced algorithmically and analyzed one at a time. This letter gives a variational route to predictive feedback by constructing a stochastic mirror differential game with an auxiliary memory state. Its stage cost couples two Fenchel terms: a strategic term evaluated at a predicted profile and a corrective term driven by realized feedback. The resulting equilibrium feedback induces two-channel predictive mirror dynamics in general mirror geometry. Under local mirror regularity, a quantitative local Bregman growth condition, and bounded Brownian diffusion, we establish finite-horizon local terminal-time bounds in expectation and with high probability, together with an exit-probability estimate for the localization neighborhood. The result provides a unified variational construction of the induced predictive-memory mirror flow together with a local stochastic certificate for last-iterate performance near stable equilibria.

</details>


### [144] [An Improved Lower Bound for the Three-Dimensional Blaschke--Lebesgue Problem from Spectral and Dual Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01754)
*Akatsuki Nishioka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对三维Blaschke--Lebesgue问题（即Meissner猜想），通过支撑函数、球谐函数谱估计和Bochner公式，将常宽凸体体积的下界从Chakerian的约0.364916d³提升至(4π/33)d³≈0.380799d³，并揭示了该下界与拉格朗日对偶问题的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维Blaschke--Lebesgue问题（Meissner猜想）悬而未决，已有的Chakerian下界（约0.364916d³）与猜想的最优体Meissner四面体体积（约0.419860d³）之间存在较大差距，需要更紧的理论下界。

Method: 采用支撑函数表述常宽凸体，利用球谐函数进行谱分析，结合Bochner公式推导体积下界；并将该下界解释为相关凹二次极值问题的拉格朗日对偶界。

Result: 建立了三维常宽凸体体积的新下界(4π/33)d³≈0.380799d³，优于Chakerian的0.364916d³，但尚未达到Meissner四面体的0.419860d³。

Conclusion: 新下界作为拉格朗日对偶界，为进一步提升下界提供了可能的优化路径，但Meissner猜想仍未完全解决。

Abstract: The Blaschke--Lebesgue problem asks for convex bodies of minimum volume among all convex bodies of prescribed constant width. In the plane, the minimizer is the Reuleaux triangle, whereas the corresponding three-dimensional problem remains open and is also known as Meissner's conjecture. In this paper, we establish the lower bound $(4π/33)d^3 \simeq 0.380799\,d^3$ for the volume of any three-dimensional convex body of constant width d. This improves upon Chakerian's lower bound, approximately $0.364916\,d^3$, although it remains below the volume of the conjectured minimizers, Meissner's tetrahedra, whose volume is approximately $0.419860\,d^3$. The proof is based on a support-function formulation, spectral estimates via spherical harmonics, and Bochner's formula. We also show that the resulting lower bound can be interpreted as a Lagrange dual bound for the associated concave quadratic minimization problem. This dual viewpoint suggests possible routes toward sharper lower bounds.

</details>


### [145] [Accelerating Min-Max Optimization via Power-Law Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01764)
*Yue Wu,Weiqiang Zheng,Yang Cai,Haipeng Luo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了外梯度法（EG）在无约束双线性极小极大优化中的收敛性。提出了一种确定性动态步长调度，将EG的最后迭代收敛率从 Θ(T^{-1/2}) 提升至 O(T^{-2/3+ε})，并通过允许不同步长进一步优化至近乎最优的 O(T^{-1+ε})。该调度遵循幂律分布，且可拓展至其他方法如乐观梯度法（OG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EG方法在固定步长下最后迭代收敛率为 Θ(T^{-1/2})，远慢于通过锚定等机制可达到的最优 O(T^{-1}) 率。受动态步长能显著加速梯度下降的启发，本文探究动态步长是否同样能加速EG的最后迭代收敛。

Method: 将步长调度转化为优化问题，求解得到遵循幂律分布（离散化）的确定性动态步长调度。分别研究了相同步长和不同步长（外推步与更新步）两种设置，并证明率为紧。

Result: 相同步长下收敛率提升至 O(T^{-2/3+ε}) 且紧；不同步长下进一步达到近乎最优的 O(T^{-1+ε})。该调度与分析可拓展至OG等其他方法。

Conclusion: 动态步长可显著加速EG方法的最后迭代收敛，且步长调度的幂律分布设计具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We revisit the convergence guarantees of the Extragradient (EG) method for unconstrained biaffine min-max optimization. It is known that EG with a fixed stepsize achieves a $Θ(T^{-1/2})$ last-iterate convergence rate, which is slower than the optimal $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1})$ rate attainable by incorporating additional mechanisms such as anchoring. Motivated by recent advances showing that dynamic stepsizes alone can significantly accelerate gradient descent, we ask whether dynamic stepsizes can similarly accelerate the last-iterate convergence of EG.
  We present the first positive result in this direction. Specifically, we provide a deterministic dynamic stepsize schedule that accelerates the convergence rate of EG to $\mathcal{O}(T^{-2/3+\varepsilon})$ for any $\varepsilon > 0$. We also show that this rate is tight when the extrapolation and update steps of EG use the same stepsize. We then show that allowing different stepsizes for the extrapolation and update steps further improves the convergence rate to the near-optimal $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1+\varepsilon})$. Our analysis reduces stepsize scheduling to an optimization problem, whose solution leads to a stepsize schedule that follows (a discretization of) a power-law distribution. Our proposed stepsize schedules and analysis extend to other methods, such as Optimistic Gradient (OG), and suggest broader applicability to general min-max optimization problems.

</details>


### [146] [Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization with a Polyak-type Step size: A Theory-Grounded Scheduler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01827)
*Dimitris Oikonomou,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对SAM优化器对学习率敏感的问题，基于随机Polyak步长方法，为SAM风格更新推导了Polyak调度器，提出了确定性和随机环境下的自适应算法，并证明了强凸目标的线性收敛性和凸目标的O(1/T)收敛率，实验表明性能与精心调参的SAM相当或更优，且大幅减少调参需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM及其变体对学习率选择敏感，通常需要大量超参数调优或预定义调度器，这增加了使用成本。本文受随机Polyak步长在SGD中有效性的启发，希望为SAM设计自适应的Polyak调度器，以减少对学习率调优的依赖。

Method: 本文基于随机Polyak步长方法，为SAM风格的更新推导了Polyak调度器，分别设计了确定性和随机两种环境下的自适应算法。在理论上证明了强凸目标的线性收敛率和凸目标的O(1/T)收敛率（确定性情形），以及随机情形下达到最优邻域的收敛保证。

Result: 所提出的Polyak调度器在数值实验中达到了与经过仔细调参的SAM基线相当或更优的性能，同时显著减少了对学习率调优的需求。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Polyak步长方法适配到SAM优化器中，提出了自适应学习率调度方案，既保持了SAM的良好泛化性能，又大幅降低了学习率调优成本，为SAM系列优化器的实用化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) has established itself as a powerful and widely adopted optimizer for training machine learning models. By explicitly minimizing the sharpness of the loss landscape, SAM often improves generalization while delivering strong empirical performance. However, SAM and its variants, like most training algorithms, are sensitive to the choice of learning rate, which is typically selected through extensive hyperparameter tuning or predefined schedulers. In this work, motivated by recent advances on the effectiveness of stochastic Polyak step sizes for Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD), we derive Polyak schedulers tailored to SAM-style updates, yielding novel adaptive algorithms in both deterministic and stochastic settings. In the smooth setting, we prove linear convergence for strongly convex objectives and an $\mathcal{O}(1/T)$ convergence rate for convex objectives in the deterministic case. In the stochastic setting, we establish analogous convergence guarantees up to a neighborhood of the optimum. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed Polyak schedulers achieve performance comparable to or better than carefully tuned SAM baselines, while substantially reducing the need for learning-rate tuning.

</details>


### [147] [Second-Order Optimality Conditions for Sparse Differentiable Optimization Problems via Limiting Second-Order Subdifferentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01998)
*Luu Thi Thu Huyen,Liguo Jiao,Nguyen Van Tuyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类目标函数和约束函数均为Fréchet可微且具有局部Lipschitz连续梯度映射的稀疏优化问题。利用Lagrange函数的极限（Mordukhovich）二阶次微分，建立了局部最优解的新二阶必要和充分最优性条件。这些结果在温和假设下推导得出，推广了文献中的多个现有结果。此外，将理论发展应用于稀疏多目标优化问题，导出了有效解的二阶充分最优性条件，并通过实例验证了所提结果的适用性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏优化问题的最优性条件研究尚不充分，尤其是在二阶条件方面。本文旨在利用Mordukhovich二阶次微分这一工具，在较温和假设下建立更一般的二阶必要和充分最优性条件，填补理论空白，并推广到稀疏多目标优化问题。

Method: 利用极限（Mordukhovich）二阶次微分分析Lagrange函数，推导二阶必要和充分最优性条件，并将理论结果应用于稀疏多目标优化问题以导出有效解的二阶充分条件。

Result: 建立了新的二阶必要和充分最优性条件，推广了现有文献结果；针对稀疏多目标优化问题导出了有效解的二阶充分最优性条件；通过示例验证了理论结果的适用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的二阶最优性条件在温和假设下有效，且能推广至多目标情形，为稀疏优化理论提供了重要扩展。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a class of sparse optimization problems in which both the objective and constraint functions are Fréchet differentiable and possess locally Lipschitz continuous gradient mappings. More precisely, by utilizing the limiting (Mordukhovich) second-order subdifferential of the associated Lagrangian function, we establish new second-order necessary and sufficient optimality conditions for local optimal solutions. The obtained results are derived under mild assumptions and extend several existing results in the literature. In addition, we apply our theoretical developments to sparse multiobjective optimization problems and derive second-order sufficient optimality conditions for efficient solutions. Several examples are also presented to demonstrate the applicability and effectiveness of the proposed results.

</details>


### [148] [A first-optimize-then-reduce framework for optimal control with the shifted proper orthogonal decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02064)
*Tobias Breiten,Shubhaditya Burela,Philipp Schulze*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文探索了将移位本征正交分解（sPOD）非线性模型降阶技术应用于最优控制问题，采用先优化后降阶（FOTR）框架，以1D线性对流方程约束的线性二次型最优控制问题为例，比较了sPOD与标准POD的计算性能，旨在克服输运主导问题中构建高效ROM的Kolmogorov障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输运主导问题中构建高效降阶模型（ROM）存在Kolmogorov障碍，阻碍了ROM作为PDE约束最优控制问题的计算廉价替代模型；由于最优控制问题需要多次求解完整PDE，使用ROM替代可加速整体求解过程。

Method: 采用先优化后降阶（FOTR）框架：先构造完整PDE问题的最优性系统，再用降阶模型近似该最优性系统；具体将移位本征正交分解（sPOD）非线性模型降阶技术应用于线性二次型最优控制问题，并与标准POD进行对比。

Result: 以1D线性对流方程约束的线性二次型最优控制问题为例，比较了sPOD方法与标准POD方法的计算性能。

Conclusion: sPOD方法有望在输运主导问题中克服Kolmogorov障碍，为最优控制问题提供更高效的ROM替代方案，但其具体性能优势有待文中实验结果进一步验证。

Abstract: Overcoming the Kolmogorov barrier for constructing efficient reduced-order models (ROMs) for transport-dominated problems remains a challenge. This has impeded their use as computationally cheap surrogates for partial differential equations (PDEs) associated with optimal control problems. Since such problems require multiple computations of the full PDE, employing their reduced-order surrogates instead could speed up the overall optimal control problem. Motivated by this idea, in this paper we explore the use of a nonlinear model-order reduction technique, namely, the shifted proper orthogonal decomposition (sPOD) in an optimal control context. In doing so, we explore the framework of first-optimize-then-reduce (FOTR) where an optimality system for the full PDE problem is constructed first, followed by approximating the optimality system with reduced-order models. We consider this framework for a linear quadratic optimal control problem constrained by a 1D linear advection equation and compare the computational performance of the sPOD method against the use of the standard POD.

</details>


### [149] [Decision-calibrated prediction sets for robust power system operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02081)
*Akylas Stratigakos,Honglin Wen,Elina Spyrou,Pierre Pinson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出决策校准预测集作为鲁棒优化的不确定集，通过对下游决策可靠性进行校准而非传统预测覆盖校准，在电力系统储备调度中实现更精确的约束满足控制与更低运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准数据驱动方法校准不确定集以达到预测覆盖，但这会产生不必要的大集合和高昂运行成本。需要一种新的校准方法，直接针对下游决策的可靠性而非预测覆盖来定义不确定性集。

Method: 1) 将决策校准预测集学习为基于范数的得分函数的子水平集，由部分输入凸神经网络表示；2) 受共形风险控制启发，校准得分阈值参数以控制不确定集体积，从而控制下游运行约束的期望违反率。

Result: 决策校准集将约束满足目标偏差控制在约3个百分点以内，而标准覆盖校准系统性地超出目标11个百分点以上，导致更大集合和更高运行成本。

Conclusion: 决策校准预测集在保持凸性和可处理性的同时，能更精准地控制下游决策的可靠性，优于传统覆盖校准方法。

Abstract: Robust optimization offers a tractable approach to balance operating costs and reliability in power systems dominated by weather-dependent renewable uncertainty, but its performance depends critically on the uncertainty set. Standard data-driven approaches often calibrate uncertainty sets to attain predictive coverage, which can produce unnecessarily large sets and costly operating decisions. In contrast, we introduce decision-calibrated prediction sets and embed them as uncertainty sets in robust optimization problems; these are conditional multivariate prediction sets where calibration is defined in terms of the reliability of downstream decisions, rather than in terms of the coverage. First, we learn these conditional prediction sets as sub-level sets of norm-based score functions represented by partially input-convex neural networks, capturing contextual information and multivariate dependence while preserving convexity and tractability in downstream robust formulations. Second, inspired by conformal risk control, we calibrate a score-threshold parameter that sets the volume of the uncertainty set, thereby controlling the expected violations of downstream operational constraints. We apply our approach to 15-minute-ahead reserve scheduling with network-constrained deliverability, which we formulate as a robust DC optimal power flow problem with affine recourse. Numerical experiments show that decision-calibrated sets attain prescribed constraint-satisfaction targets within about three percentage points, whereas standard coverage-based calibration systematically exceeds these targets by more than eleven percentage points, leading to larger sets and higher operating costs.

</details>


### [150] [Switched Event-Triggered Adaptive Control of Reaction-Diffusion PDE-ODE with Neural Operator Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02114)
*Hongpeng Yuan,Ji Wang,Mamadou Diagne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类反应-扩散PDE-ODE级联系统，提出了一种基于切换事件触发机制的自适应边界控制方法。通过两步反步变换推导连续时间控制律，并设计了新型动态事件触发控制策略，结合自适应更新律处理系统不确定性。同时，采用深度神经算子（DeepONet）近似反步核函数，避免在线求解核PDE。Lyapunov分析证明了闭环系统的L²全局渐近调节性能，数值仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对PDE-ODE级联系统的反步控制主要假设系统参数已知，且事件触发控制研究中缺乏对参数不确定性和复杂级联结构的综合处理。本文旨在解决反应-扩散PDE-ODE级联系统中同时存在ODE系统矩阵、输入矩阵不确定性以及PDE空间变化反应系数不确定性的自适应事件触发边界控制问题。

Method: (1) 构建两步反步变换推导连续时间控制律；(2) 提出基于切换事件触发机制的新型动态事件触发控制策略，确保全局指数稳定性并排除Zeno行为；(3) 开发自适应更新律处理系统不确定性，生成时变增益核；(4) 采用DeepONet将估计参数到核函数的映射进行离线学习与在线近似，避免实时求解核PDE。

Result: Lyapunov分析证明了闭环系统在事件触发机制、参数自适应和核近似误差共同影响下的L²全局渐近调节性能；数值仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文贡献包括：(i) 开发了针对反应-扩散PDE-ODE级联系统的自适应DeepONet框架；(ii) 将现有自适应事件触发控制从单一反应-扩散PDE扩展到具有更复杂不确定性的级联系统；(iii) 将具有全局指数稳定性的切换动态事件触发控制推广到PDE-ODE级联系统。

Abstract: This paper develops a switched event-triggered adaptive boundary control for a class of reaction-diffusion PDE-ODE cascade systems, where the system and input matrices in the ODE as well as the spatially-varying reaction coefficient in the PDE are uncertain. A two-step backstepping transformation is constructed to derive the continuous-time control law. Then a novel dynamic event-triggered control strategy for the PDE-ODE cascade is proposed based on a switched event-triggering mechanism, ensuring global exponential stability of the closed-loop system in place of the exponential convergence commonly achieved with backstepping-based classical dynamic ETC, while inherently excluding Zeno behavior. To address the uncertainties in the PDE-ODE cascade, adaptive update laws are developed, leading to time-varying gain kernels that are adaptively scheduled through the event-triggered control mechanism. Furthermore,to facilitate efficient real-time implementation, deep neural operators (DeepONet) are employed to approximate the backstepping kernels as mappings from the estimated parameters to kernel functions, thereby eliminating the need to repeatedly solve kernel PDEs online. Through a Lyapunov analysis that incorporates the effects of the event-triggering mechanism, parameter adaptation, and kernel approximation errors, we prove the $L^2$ global asymptotic regulation of the resulting closed-loop system. In summary, the key contributions of the paper are threefold: (i) developing an adaptive DeepONet-based framework for reaction-diffusion PDE-ODE cascade systems; (ii) extending the existing adaptive event-triggered control design for reaction-diffusion PDEs to the case with more complex uncertainties; and (iii) generalizing switched dynamic ETC with global exponential stability to PDE-ODE cascades. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [151] [Retraction based regression methods on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02116)
*Estefanía Loayza-Romero,Benedikt Sibum,Kathrin Welker*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回缩（retraction）而非黎曼指数映射的测地线回归框架，将回归模型推广到更一般的流形设置，并通过对p-范数球的应用验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测地线回归依赖黎曼指数映射与黎曼距离，但指数映射在许多流形上计算代价高昂或难以获得闭式解，限制了其应用范围。本文旨在构建一个更灵活的回归框架，使其适用于指数映射不易计算的情形。

Method: 1) 用回缩映射（retraction）替代黎曼指数映射，用回缩距离替代黎曼距离来构建回归模型；2) 在切丛上构造由底层流形回缩诱导的回缩映射，将优化问题定义在切丛的子集上；3) 以(n-1)维p-范数球为例，采用归一化回缩定义回归问题，并使用黎曼最速下降法求解。

Result: 成功建立了基于回缩的测地线回归一般框架，并在p-范数球上实现了该框架的实例化，通过黎曼最速下降法求解了对应的优化问题。

Conclusion: 该框架比传统基于指数映射的测地线回归更具灵活性，可推广到指数映射计算困难的流形场景，为流形值数据分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Geodesic regression generalizes classical regression models to manifold-valued data by replacing affine models in Euclidean spaces with geodesic models on Riemannian manifolds. In this paper, we set up a framework for regression based on retractions instead of the Riemannian exponential map and its corresponding retraction-based distance. The associated optimization problem is posed on a subset of the tangent bundle which is why we additionally construct retractions on the tangent bundle induced by retractions on the underlying manifold. Our approach yields a more flexible formulation which is applicable beyond settings where the exponential map can be computed efficiently. As a proof of concept, we apply the developed framework to the (n-1)-dimensional p-norm sphere using the retraction by normalization to define the regression problem. The resulting optimization problem is solved using the Riemannian steepest descent method.

</details>


### [152] [Investment and Operational Planning for electricity markets with massive entry of renewable energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02165)
*Nicolás Hernández-Santibáñez,Alejandro Jofré,Matías Vera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究电力批发市场中独立系统运营商（ISO）在满足供需平衡和容量约束条件下的连续时间联合运营与投资成本最小化问题。通过日前问题得到最优运营的显式形式，将投资问题重构为带状态约束的随机控制问题，利用指向内条件将问题的值函数刻画为受约束HJB方程的唯一粘性解。在容量-需求过程特定动态下，讨论了HJB刻画所需的预算约束假设，并通过智利三节点市场仿真分析了短、中、长期规划及高可再生能源渗透率转型路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场中ISO需同时最小化运营与投资成本，现有方法多分别处理运营与投资决策，缺乏统一连续时间框架下的联合优化分析，且投资问题中的状态约束在随机控制框架下刻画不足。

Method: 采用连续时间随机控制框架，首先通过日前问题推导最优运营的显式解；然后利用该解将投资问题转化为带状态约束的随机控制问题；应用指向内条件（Pointing Inward Condition）将值函数刻画为受约束HJB方程的粘性解；最后在特定容量-需求过程动态下进行数值仿真。

Result: 成功将投资问题转化为带状态约束的随机控制问题，并证明了其值函数为受约束HJB方程的唯一粘性解；在智利三节点市场的仿真中展示了短、中、长期不同规划情景下的最优投资与运营策略，以及向高可再生能源系统转型的路径。

Conclusion: 本文提出的连续时间联合优化框架能够统一处理电力市场中的运营与投资决策，通过粘性解方法有效处理了投资问题中的状态约束，仿真结果验证了模型在可再生能源转型规划中的实用性。

Abstract: In wholesale electricity markets, electricity producers and the independent system operator (ISO) play a central role. The ISO is responsible for minimizing production costs while satisfying supply-demand balance and capacity constraints. In this paper, we study a continuous-time problem in which the ISO seeks to minimize the joint cost of operation and investment in an electricity network. The problem is formulated in terms of operational and investment control variables. We analyze the hierarchy between these controls and use the so-called Day-Ahead Problem to find an explicit form of the optimal operation. This allows us to reformulate the investment problem as a stochastic control problem with state constraints. We extend the results of state-constrained stochastic control to fit our setting. In particular, we use a version of the Pointing Inward Condition to fully characterize the value of the problem as the unique viscosity solution of a constrained HJB equation. We then assign a specific dynamic to the capacity-demand process and discuss how the assumptions for the HJB characterization result in a budget constraint for the planning. Finally, we run simulations for a three-node setting that resembles the Chilean market. We analyze short-, medium-, and long-term planning scenarios and discuss how to transition toward a system with high penetration of renewable energy.

</details>


### [153] [Relax and Follow: L0-Path Computation with L0-Bregman Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02176)
*Mhamed Essafri,Luca Calatroni,Emmanuel Soubies*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 L0PathBrex 方法，利用 L0 Bregman 松弛（B-rex）来估计 L0 正则化问题的解路径，通过动态构建局部极小值候选集并利用热启动策略，在稀疏最小二乘和逻辑回归任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L0 正则化问题具有非凸、不连续的优化特性，现有方法难以高效地估计其完整的解路径。作者希望利用 B-rex 的连续重构特性来克服这一挑战。

Method: 基于 L0 Bregman 松弛（B-rex）构造连续目标函数的重新表述；动态构建局部极小值候选集合；利用热启动策略识别正则化参数区间，使每个极小值在该松弛下保持有效。

Result: 在稀疏最小二乘和逻辑回归任务上，L0PathBrex 在合成与真实数据集上全面优于现有基线方法；此外还研究了 B-rex 选择对稀疏泊松回归中路径质量的影响。

Conclusion: L0PathBrex 是一种有效的 L0 解路径估计方法，能系统性地超越现有方法，B-rex 函数族的选择对路径质量有显著影响。

Abstract: This work introduces L0PathBrex, a novel method for estimating the solution path of L0-regularized problems through the use of L0 Bregman relaxations (B-rex). Recently introduced and analyzed in the literature, these relaxations provide continuous reformulations of the original objective, are applicable to possibly non-quadratic data fidelity terms, and depend on a family of functions designed to preserve the global minimizers while eliminating part of the undesirable local minima. Given any numerical solver for the relaxation, the proposed approach dynamically constructs a collection of local minimizers that are candidates for the L0-solution path. It exploits warm-start strategies and identifies ranges of the regularization parameter for which each minimizer remains valid under the corresponding relaxation. Experiments on sparse least-squares and logistic regression problems demonstrate that L0PathBrex systematically outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across both synthetic and real-world datasets in terms of various evaluation metrics; additionally, the study investigates how the choice of the B-rex affects the quality of the estimated path in the sparse Poisson regression setting.

</details>


### [154] [Benchmarking Realistic Synthetic Instances Against a Large-Scale District Heating Network: A Multi-Objective Optimization Study for Berlin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02195)
*Annika Buchholz,Stephanie Riedmüller,Matthew Passage,Janina Zittel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于区域供热系统多目标优化的大规模合成实例生成方法，基于柏林真实网络构建，提供开源基准测试数据，并验证了合成实例在复现真实优化特性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常针对单一真实网络，限制了优化算法的可比性和可迁移性。为建立可控、可复现的基准测试环境，需要能够保留真实系统关键特征的合成实例。

Method: 通过透明可复现的流程生成大规模合成实例，以JSON格式提供网络拓扑，以MPS格式提供混合整数规划模型。基于柏林区域供热网络（西欧最复杂）构建了三目标混合整数模型，时间分辨率为4小时，规划期长达25年。

Result: 通过计算实验对比了合成实例与真实柏林数据，揭示了合成实例复现真实优化特性的条件，并分析了导致模型计算挑战的关键特征。

Conclusion: 精心设计的合成实例能够支持鲁棒的基准测试实践，有助于对有意义的评估大规模区域供热系统的（多目标）优化方法。

Abstract: Decarbonizing urban energy systems requires optimization approaches capable of handling the operational complexity of large-scale district heating networks. However, existing studies typically focus on a single real-world network, limiting comparability and the transferability of insights. To address this, realistic synthetic instances provide controlled, reproducible environments for testing optimization algorithms independent of specific case studies while preserving key structural and temporal characteristics of real systems. Such instances enable systematic benchmarking, methodological development, and comparative studies across algorithms and modeling choices. In this work, we generate a suite of large-scale synthetic instances for multi-objective optimization of district heating systems. The instances are openly available as network topologies in JSON format and as mixed-integer programs (MPS files) for benchmarking. They are constructed via a transparent procedure that allows reproduction, extension, and transfer to other network-based problems. We apply the method to Berlins district heating network, the most complex in Western Europe, formulating a tri-objective mixed-integer model for unit commitment over up to 25 years with 4-hour temporal resolution. A computational study provides a detailed comparison between the synthetic instances and the real-world Berlin data, showing under which conditions the generated instances reproduce realistic optimization characteristics. Furthermore, we investigate which features make the resulting models computationally challenging. The findings highlight how well-designed synthetic instances can support robust benchmarking practices and enable meaningful assessment of (multi-objective) optimization methods for large-scale district heating systems.

</details>


### [155] [A novel L-shaped refinement chain cuts method for two-stage stochastic programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02469)
*Mike Hewitt,Francesca Maggioni,Andrea Spinelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出L型细化链割方法，将情景细化链融入经典L型分解框架中，通过分组求解子问题而非单个情景，推广了经典的多割点和单割点L型公式，并建立了理论收敛性，在两个阶段随机固定费用多商品网络设计问题上的实验证明了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统L型方法在处理大规模两阶段随机规划时，需要为每个情景求解子问题，计算成本高昂。本文旨在通过引入情景细化链分组策略，降低计算复杂度同时保证收敛性。

Method: 将完整情景集按细化链分层划分为子组，每层为每个子组求解一个子问题而非每个情景；建立相邻细化层之间Benders割的关系，设计基于迭代细化的求解算法。

Result: 在均值-风险公式下的两阶段随机固定费用多商品网络设计问题的基准实例上，该框架表现出良好的性能，适用于大规模风险厌恶随机优化问题。

Conclusion: 所提方法推广了经典多割点和单割点L型方法，在保持理论收敛性的前提下，通过情景分组显著提升了计算效率，尤其适用于大规模风险厌恶随机优化场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces the L-shaped refinement chain cuts method, a novel approach for solving two-stage stochastic programs. The proposed method integrates the refinement chain of scenarios within the classical L-shaped decomposition framework. In the proposed approach, the full scenario set is partitioned into subgroups at each level of the refinement chain, and one subproblem is solved for each subgroup rather than for each individual scenario as in the classical L-shaped method. The proposed framework generalizes both the classical multi-cut and single-cut L-shaped formulations. Theoretical convergence properties to the optimal solution of the original two-stage stochastic program are established for every refinement level. In addition, the relationships between consecutive refinement levels are characterized in terms of Benders cuts, leading to the development of an iterative refinement-based solution algorithm across consecutive levels of the refinement chain. The effectiveness of the proposed method is evaluated on a two-stage stochastic fixed-charge multicommodity network design problem under a mean-risk formulation. Computational experiments on benchmark instances demonstrate the promising performance of the proposed framework and highlight its applicability to large-scale risk-averse stochastic optimization problems.

</details>


### [156] [A No-Regret Framework for Adaptive Incentive Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02529)
*Georgios Vasileiou,Lantian Zhang,Silun Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无遗憾自适应激励设计（RAID）框架，用于连续动作空间和私有代理成本的非线性博弈。该框架通过切换激励策略（交替探索和利用）实现几乎必然的$O(t^{-0.5})$参数估计率和$O(t^{0.5}\log t)$平方社会成本遗憾，并进一步扩展到内生噪声响应模型，使用重复采样估计器保持相同的收敛和遗憾率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激励设计研究中央权威如何通过支付、补贴或税收影响策略性主体，使个体目标与集体福利对齐。现有方法通常假设已知代理偏好或限制于线性/离散模型，本文旨在解决非线性连续博弈中在线学习未知代理偏好同时设计激励以实现社会最优的问题。

Method: 1. 构建RAID问题并设计最小二乘估计器，仅需递减激励即可实现强一致性；2. 提出切换激励策略，交替使用探测（探索）和基于估计（利用）的激励；3. 针对内生噪声响应模型，采用重复采样估计器解决标准最小二乘的偏差问题，并设计相应的切换策略。

Result: 所提策略几乎必然地实现了$O(t^{-0.5})$的参数估计率和$O(t^{0.5}\log t)$的平方社会成本遗憾。数值实验验证了方法的有效性和预测的收敛速率。

Conclusion: RAID框架成功解决了非线性连续博弈中在线激励设计与学习的联合问题，且对内生噪声模型也具备鲁棒性，为实际社会经济系统中的自适应激励机制设计提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Incentive design studies how a central authority can influence strategic agents through payments, subsidies, or taxes, so that individual objectives align with collective welfare. This paper introduces a No-Regret Adaptive Incentive Design (RAID) framework for nonlinear games with continuous action spaces and private agent costs. In this framework, the authority (planner) designs incentives that regulate the Nash equilibrium toward a socially optimal action profile, while simultaneously learning agents' unknown preferences from repeated strategic responses. We formulate the RAID problem and construct a least-squares estimator whose strong consistency requires only diminishing excitation. Leveraging this weak excitation requirement, we propose a switching incentive policy that alternates between probing (exploration) and estimate-based (exploitation) incentives. The resulting policy achieves an $O(t^{-0.5})$ parameter estimation rate and accumulates $O(t^{0.5}\log t)$ squared social-cost regret, almost surely. We further extend the framework to an endogenous-noise response model, where standard least-squares estimation is biased due to an error-in-variables correlation between the noise and agent responses. We utilize a repeated-sampling estimator and corresponding switching policy that retain the same almost-sure convergence and regret rates. Numerical experiments validate the effectiveness and predicted convergence rates of the method.

</details>
