### [157] [Comparisons of triple-differential cross sections for quasielastic-like $ν_μ$-hydrocarbon interactions using $\langle E_ν\rangle \sim$ 3~GeV versus $\sim$ 6~GeV beams in MINERvA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00745)
*D. Ruterbories,S. Akhter,Z. Ahmad Dar,M. Sajjad Athar,M. Betancourt,S. Boyd,H. da Motta,J. Felix,L. Fields,R. Fine,A. M. Gago,H. Gallagher,P. K. Gaur,S. M. Gilligan,R. Gran,E. Granados,D. A. Harris,A. L. Hart,A. Klustová,M. Kordosky,D. Last,Z. Lin,A. Lozano,S. Manly,W. A. Mann,C. Mauger,K. S. McFarland,M. Mehmood,O. Moreno,J. G. Morfín,J. K. Nelson,C. Nguyen,V. Paolone,G. N. Perdue,C. Pernas,M. A. Ramírez,R. D. Ransome,N. Roy,H. Schellman,C. J. Solano Salinas,N. H. Vaughan,A. V. Waldron,L. Zazueta*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文比较了同一测量在两个不同能谱的中微子束流（峰值分别约3 GeV和6 GeV）中的结果，发现数据与预测存在差异，表明对准弹性事件中质子和带电π介子的末态相互作用存在高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡测量广泛依赖中微子-核子电荷电流准弹性类散射，但该过程受多种核效应（单核子敲出、多核子过程、非弹性散射及末态重吸收等）影响，导致自由中子散射偏离。现有模型对这些核介质效应的处理存在不确定性，直接影响振荡实验中的中微子能量重建精度。

Method: 在同一探测器中分别使用两种宽波段中微子束流（峰值~3 GeV和~6 GeV）进行测量，对比μ子和质子运动学下的微分截面，通过不同束流能区的系统差异来检验核介质引发的偏离效应。

Result: 数据与模型预测存在显著差异，具体体现为模型对质子末态相互作用和带电π介子末态相互作用的高估。

Conclusion: 现有中微子相互作用模型在描述准弹性类事件中的核效应方面存在不足，尤其对质子与π介子在核内末态相互作用的处理需要改进，以提升振荡实验中中微子能量重建的可靠性。

Abstract: Neutrino charged-current quasielastic-like scattering, a reaction category extensively used in neutrino oscillation measurements, receives contributions from single nucleon knockout processes, multinucleon processes, and inelastic scattering with subsequent rescattering or absorption in the nucleus to produce only nucleons in the final state. In this article, comparisons are presented of the same measurement in two different wideband neutrino beams: one beam peaks near 3 GeV with few neutrinos above 6 GeV; the other peaks near 6 GeV with few neutrinos above 10 GeV. Comparisons of differential cross sections in muon and proton kinematics for these two exposures probe deviations from free-neutron scattering that arise from the processes involving the nuclear medium, and provide a test of neutrino interaction models used to infer neutrino energies in oscillation experiments. Discrepancies are observed between the data and predictions that point to overestimates of the final state interactions of both protons and charged pions in quasielastic-like events.

</details>


### [158] [Search for single production of a vector-like B' quark decaying to a top quark and a W boson in the single-lepton final state in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01423)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验利用138 fb⁻²的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，通过单轻子末态搜寻窄宽度矢量类B'夸克的单产生（衰变至tW），使用神经网络标记大半径喷注、神经自回归流网络模拟本底，在0.8-1.23 TeV质量区间排除单重态B'夸克（Γ/m=5%），并给出耦合系数限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找超出标准模型的新物理信号，具体针对矢量类B'夸克的单产生过程，探索其与电弱玻色子的耦合性质。

Method: 基于CMS探测器2016-2018年收集的138 fb⁻²数据，选择单轻子末态（电子或缪子），结合丢失横动量、大半径喷注和小半径喷注重建B'夸克候选者。采用神经网络标记器识别大半径喷注的起源粒子，利用神经自回归流网络从数据中建模主要本底贡献。

Result: 在0.8至1.23 TeV质量范围内排除单重态B'夸克（Γ/m_B' = 5%），给出单B'夸克伴生t夸克产生的截面限制及B'夸克与电弱玻色子耦合因子的上限。

Conclusion: 该搜索是迄今对窄宽度B'夸克单产生最灵敏的搜寻，对B'夸克的质量、产生截面及耦合强度给出了新的实验限制。

Abstract: A search is presented for the single production of a narrow-width vector-like B' quark that decays to a t quark and a W boson, with one of the decay products yielding an electron or muon. The data were collected from 2016 to 2018 by the CMS experiment at the LHC in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-2}$. The search is performed in a single-lepton final state, where the B' quark candidate is reconstructed from an electron or muon, missing transverse momentum, one large-radius jet, and one small-radius jet if the t quark decays leptonically. The originating particles of large-radius jets are identified using a neural-network-based tagger, and the dominant background contributions are modeled from data using a neural autoregressive flow network. This search is the most sensitive to date to the single production of narrow-width B' quarks, excluding singlet B' quarks with $Γ/m_\mathrm{B'}$ = 5% for masses between 0.8 and 1.23 TeV. Limits are also placed on the production cross section of single B' quarks produced in association with t quarks, and on the coupling factor of the B' quark to electroweak bosons.

</details>


### [159] [HS3: A Descriptive, Interoperable Serialization Standard for Statistical Models in High-Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01760)
*Carsten Burgard,Oliver Schulz,Giordon Stark,Jonas Rembser,Simon Cello,Cornelius Grunwald*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了HS3（高能物理统计序列化标准），一种实现无关、人类可读、可扩展的统计模型序列化格式，支持有/无分箱似然及分层复合模型，可从ROOT/RooFit转换并作为pyhf的超集，旨在为LHC及更广科学社区提供FAIR原则下长期可用的统计模型表示与交换标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计模型表示格式（如ROOT workspaces、pyhf JSON、CMS DataCards）与特定软件栈绑定，缺乏通用交换、验证和长期保存标准，难以满足高能物理中基于似然的精确测量、有效场论拟合及跨分析组合日益增长的需求。

Method: HS3将似然表示为由命名分布、函数、数据集、域和分析方案构成的计算图，支持有分箱和无分箱似然以及分层复合模型；设计上通过向后兼容扩展支持新统计构造，推理过程和执行细节由下游框架负责；实现了从/到ROOT/RooFit的转换，并作为pyhf的超集。

Result: 给出了HS3的设计原则、结构和语义，总结了C++、Python和Julia中的现有实现，并展示了在HEPData上公开似然、跨框架验证和可重复性方面的早期应用。

Conclusion: HS3为LHC及更广领域提供了符合FAIR原则、长期可用的统计模型基础标准，旨在服务更广泛的科学社区并随时间演进适用于多种领域。

Abstract: Statistical models in high-energy physics formally encode the relationship between observed data, physics parameters of interest, and experimental and theoretical uncertainties. Likelihood-based inference is the central tool for precision measurements, effective field theory fits, and cross-analysis combinations. Consequently, there is an increasing need for machine-readable, descriptive, and portable model representations. Existing formats such as ROOT workspaces, pyhf JSON, and CMS DataCards provide valuable capabilities but remain tied to specific software stacks and offer no universal standard for exchange, validation, or long-term preservation. We introduce HS3, the High-Energy Physics Statistics Serialization Standard, an implementation-agnostic, human-readable, and extensible serialization format for statistical models. HS3 is designed such that new statistical constructs can be incorporated through backward-compatible extensions, while inference procedures and implementation-specific execution details remain the responsibility of downstream frameworks. HS3 represents likelihoods as computational graphs composed of named distributions, functions, datasets, domains, and analysis prescriptions. It supports binned and unbinned likelihoods as well as hierarchical composite models. HS3 is convertible from and to ROOT/RooFit and is a superset of pyhf. We describe the design principles, structure, and semantics of HS3 and summarize existing implementations in C++, Python, and Julia. We also present early applications to public likelihoods on HEPData, cross-framework validation, and reproducibility efforts. HS3 provides a foundation for FAIR (Findable, Accessible, Interoperable, Reusable), long-lived statistical models at the LHC and beyond. The standard is intended to serve the broader scientific community and to evolve over time for application across a wide range of domains.

</details>


### [160] [Search for a leptoquark in events with a hadronically decaying $τ$-lepton and missing transverse momentum using $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02067)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（140 fb⁻¹）中搜索产生τ轻子（强子衰变）和缺失横向动量的轻子夸克信号，未发现超出标准模型预期的超出现象，在U₁矢量轻子夸克模型下对1.5-3.0 TeV质量区间的耦合参数设置了95%置信水平上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻子夸克是超越标准模型的重要候选粒子，可同时耦合夸克和轻子，目前尚无实验证据，需通过高能对撞机数据进行直接搜索。

Method: 基于ATLAS探测器Run 2期间采集的140 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，选择包含强子衰变τ轻子和缺失横向动量的末态，同时针对共振产生和非共振t道交换两种机制进行敏感性分析，覆盖多种耦合参数空间。

Result: 未观察到显著超出标准模型背景的统计信号，在U₁矢量轻子夸克模型的基准框架下，对1.5-3.0 TeV质量范围给出了95%置信水平的耦合上限。

Conclusion: 该搜索是目前同类分析中对U₁矢量轻子夸克耦合约束最强的结果之一，为未来更高能量或精度的轻子夸克搜索提供了参考上限。

Abstract: A search for leptoquark signals is performed in final states with a hadronically decaying $τ$-lepton and missing transverse momentum, using data from proton--proton collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider during Run 2 (2015--18), corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis is designed to probe both resonant production and non-resonant $t$-channel exchange of the leptoquark, covering a wide range of coupling scenarios. No excess above the Standard Model background prediction is observed. Limits are set on the couplings in the benchmark $U_1$ vector-leptoquark model at 95% confidence level for masses between 1.5 TeV and 3.0 TeV.

</details>

### [161] [Exploring the periodicity of flight patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00128)
*Sarah M. Coleman,H. Sherry Zhang,Lydia R. Lucchesi,Saptarshi Roy*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文分析了2025年ASA数据挑战赛提供的35年美国商业航班数据，通过可视化与香农熵量化方法，发现了主要航空公司和FAA枢纽机场的航班调度“波”状周期性结构，并利用乘客登机数据集进行FAA枢纽分类，比较了美国四大航空公司的调度周期性与熵值差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与2025年JSM数据挑战赛，利用超过35年的美国商业航班数据，探索航空公司、机场和时间维度上的航班调度模式，并识别调度中的周期性结构。

Method: 对每日航班起降调度进行可视化分析，观察周期性结构（“波”）；通过计算香农熵对调度周期性进行量化；使用BTS乘客登机数据集确定FAA枢纽分类（区别于航空公司特定枢纽）。

Result: 在主要航空枢纽和大型FAA枢纽观察到明显的调度“波”状周期性结构；不同机场在调度周期性和熵值上存在显著差异；美国四大航空公司（美航、达美、联航、西南航空）之间也存在差异。

Conclusion: 航班调度存在可量化的周期性模式，香农熵可作为衡量调度规律性的有效指标，结合乘客登机数据进行FAA枢纽分类可补充传统航空公司枢纽视角。

Abstract: Each year the American Statistical Association (ASA) hosts the Annual Data Challenge Expo, which tasks participants with analyzing a given dataset and presenting their work at the Joint Statistical Meeting (JSM). The 2025 Data Challenge Expo tasked participants with analyzing over 35 years of commercial flight data from the United States Bureau of Transportation Statistics (BTS). These data provide extensive geographic coverage and operational details for the U.S. domestic aviation market. For millions of past flights, there is information about the flight's date, origin, destination, carrier, plane, departure, and arrival. In this article, we present our analysis for the 2025 JSM Data Challenge Expo. We chose to explore patterns in the daily scheduling of departures and arrivals across airlines, airports, and time. In doing so, we observed distinct scheduling ``waves'', or periodic structures at major airline hubs as well as large Federal Aviation Administration (FAA) hubs. In the remainder of this article, we detail the process of visualizing periodicity in flight scheduling as well as quantifying it through the calculation of Shannon entropy. An additional element to the 2025 Data Challenge Expo is the incorporation of a second dataset, to be decided by the participants. We detail the use of a BTS dataset with passenger enplanement (boarding) information to determine Federal Aviation Administration (FAA) hub classification (as opposed to airline-specific hubs). Furthermore, we discuss results from this visual and quantitative analysis, highlighting noticeable differences in the scheduling periodicity and entropy across airports, for the ``big four'' or four largest carriers, in U.S. aviation: American Airlines, Delta Air Lines, United Airlines, and Southwest Airlines.

</details>


### [162] [Bayesian Inference of Nonlinear Malaria Dynamics in Ghana via an Ensemble Markov Chain Monte Carlo Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00783)
*T. Ansah-Narh,Y. Asare Afrane,J. Bremang Tandoh*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个贝叶斯非线性推断框架，整合三次基线与阻尼振荡核，利用仿射不变集成马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样器，对加纳2014-2023年疟疾住院数据进行建模。框架在有限数据下实现高精度拟合（R²>0.995），揭示显著空间异质性，并生成2024-2026年概率性预测，显示疟疾病例将逐步回升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲疟疾动态的可靠量化受限于短、噪声大且空间异质的监测记录。加纳2014-2023年的卫生机构数据显示非线性且年龄特异的住院波动，但现有方法难以捕捉随机变异性或提供可信的不确定性边界。

Method: 开发了一个贝叶斯非线性推断框架，整合三次基线（cubic baseline）与阻尼振荡核（damped oscillatory kernel），通过仿射不变集成马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器进行估计。框架能够适应有限数据、建模参数不确定性，并为五岁以下儿童及五岁以上个体生成概率性预测。

Result: 模型拟合优度极高（五岁以下R²=0.9958，五岁以上R²=0.9956），残差误差低于2%，后验分布混合良好确认收敛。地区级分析显示显著空间异质性，变异系数从城市中心库马西的<0.07到边缘地区姆波霍尔和东比亚的>3.3。2024-2026年预测显示病例逐步回升：五岁以下从137,000增至149,000例，五岁以上从348,000增至375,000例，不确定性随时间扩大。

Conclusion: 通过生成概率性预测，该贝叶斯框架为预测疟疾波动、加强加纳国家疟疾控制策略中基于数据的决策提供了原则性工具。

Abstract: Reliable quantification of malaria dynamics in sub-Saharan Africa is hindered by short, noisy, and spatially heterogeneous surveillance records. In Ghana, health-facility data from 2014 to 2023 reveal non-linear and age-specific fluctuations in hospital admissions, yet existing approaches struggle to capture stochastic variability or provide credible uncertainty bounds. This study develops a Bayesian nonlinear inference framework that integrates a cubic baseline with a damped oscillatory kernel, estimated via an affine-invariant ensemble Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampler. The framework accommodates limited data, models parameter uncertainty, and generates probabilistic forecasts for children under five years and individuals aged five years or more. Results show strong empirical adequacy ($R^2 = 0.9958$ for $<5$ years; $R^2 = 0.9956$ for $\geq 5$ years) with residual errors below $2\%$ and well-mixed posteriors confirming convergence. District-level analysis reveals pronounced spatial heterogeneity, with coefficients of variation ranging from $<0.07$ in urban centres such as Kumasi to $>3.3$ in peripheral districts such as Mpohor and Bia East. Forecasts for 2024-2026 indicate a gradual resurgence: from 137,000 to 149,000 cases among children under five years and from 348,000 to 375,000 cases among older individuals, with uncertainty widening over time. By producing probabilistic forecasts, this Bayesian framework provides a principled tool for anticipating malaria fluctuations and strengthening data-driven decision-making in Ghana's national malaria control strategy.

</details>


### [163] [Hybrid Probabilistic Forecasting of Under-Five Malaria Admissions in Ghana: A Gaussian Process Regression with Holt-Winters Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00834)
*T. Ansah-Narh,Y. Asare Afrane,J. Bremang Tandoh*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出了一种将高斯过程回归(GPR)与Holt-Winters指数平滑相结合的混合框架，用于加纳地区五岁以下儿童疟疾入院人数的概率预测。在十年区级数据验证下，混合模型R²达0.9906，显著优于单一Holt-Winters模型(0.8213)，并成功预测2024-2028年月均入院人数约8,000-12,200例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲地区的疟疾预测面临强季节性、报告不确定性和非平稳传播动力学等挑战，传统模型可靠性不足。加纳区级疟疾监测需要概率严谨且数据有限下鲁棒的预测框架。

Method: 提出GPR与Holt-Winters指数平滑的混合框架：GPR捕捉非线性行为与预测不确定性，Holt-Winters稳定长程预测并保留季节结构。使用十年(2014-2023)区级数据，采用滚动原点扩展窗口验证进行评估。

Result: 混合模型R²=0.9906，对比Holt-Winters单独模型的0.8213；94.2%的残差在±2σ范围内。2024-2028年预测月均入院人数约8,000-12,200例。时空分析显示北部高负担地区虽绝对波动大但相对模式稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架为流行地区疟疾早期预警和运营规划提供了可扩展的概率方法，支持加纳国家疟疾控制战略。

Abstract: Accurate malaria forecasting remains a major challenge in sub-Saharan Africa, where strong seasonality, reporting uncertainty, and non-stationary transmission dynamics reduce the reliability of conventional models. In Ghana, district-level malaria surveillance requires forecasting frameworks that are probabilistically rigorous and robust under limited data. This study proposes a hybrid framework integrating Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) with Holt-Winters exponential smoothing for modelling monthly under-five malaria admissions. GPR captures non-linear behaviour and predictive uncertainty, while Holt-Winters stabilises long-horizon forecasts and preserves seasonal structure. Using ten years of district-level data (2014-2023), performance was evaluated via rolling-origin expanding-window validation. The hybrid model achieved $R^2 = 0.9906$ versus $0.8213$ for Holt-Winters alone, with $94.2\%$ of residuals within $\pm 2σ$ bounds. Forecasts for 2024-2028 project average monthly admissions from approximately 8{,}000 to 12{,}200 cases. Spatio-temporal analysis revealed pronounced ecological heterogeneity: northern high-burden districts exhibited stable relative patterns despite large absolute fluctuations. The framework provides a scalable probabilistic approach for malaria early warning and operational planning in endemic settings, supporting Ghana's national malaria control strategy.

</details>


### [164] [Markovianity-Based Conditioning Depth Diagnostics for Hidden Confounding in Observational Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01214)
*S. A. Adedayo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究时间序列因果发现中，随着条件集深度增加，因果图是否趋于稳定这一现象。作者提出图不稳定性统计量，用于判断因果推断在何种条件下是可靠的。实验表明，c-GC变体在区分马尔可夫与非马尔可夫系统方面表现最好，而该方法不恢复潜在图，仅作为模型检查工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列因果发现中，若条件集未能充分表征系统状态（如遗漏相关历史或未观测过程），残差依赖会表现为虚假因果链接。现有方法缺乏对因果图稳定性的系统诊断工具。

Method: 提出基于条件深度网格的图不稳定性统计量，通过观察因果图随条件深度增加的变化来诊断模型稳定性。在合成数据（含马尔可夫与非马尔可夫系统）和真实钙成像数据上进行实验，比较c-GC和PCMCI等多种方法。

Result: 合成数据中，c-GC变体对马尔可夫与非马尔可夫系统的区分最清晰，PCMCI变体趋势较弱。真实数据中，推断连接度随条件深度先急剧下降后趋于平稳。该方法无法恢复潜在图，也无法清晰区分潜在混杂、滞后阶数误设、非平稳性和测量误差。

Conclusion: 该方法定位为实用且适度的模型检查工具，用于判断因果声称何时是稳定的、何时应谨慎对待。

Abstract: Reliable causal discovery in time series depends on whether the conditioning set adequately represents the system state. If relevant history or unobserved processes are omitted, residual dependence can appear as direct causal links. We study this failure mode on promnient constraint-based causal discovery methods through a simple premise: how much does the inferred graph change as conditioning depth increases? When the observed process is described approximately by a finite-order Markovian representation, inferred graphs should stabilize once sufficient past observations are observed. Hidden confounding and other hidden-memory mechanisms should remain sensitive to depth when the observed state is incomplete. We formalise this behavior with graph instability statistics computed over the conditioning-depth grid. The empirical study covers synthetic systems with known ground truth and calcium imaging recordings with unknown causal structure. In simulations, both Markovian and non-Markovian systems relatively upheld our premise. With known ground truth, we evaluate recovery using confusion matrix metrics; while in real data without ground truth, we use descriptive graph instability summaries. Across synthetic Markovian and hidden memory systems, c-GC variants give the clearest separation, while PCMCI variants show weaker compatible trends. In real data, inferred connectivity drops sharply with conditioning depths and then levels off. This method, however, does not recover latent graphs, nor does it clearly separate latent confounding from lag-order misspecification, non-stationarity, measurement error. Its contribution is more modest and practical: and explicit model-checking tool for deciding when causal claims are stable and when they should be treated caustiosly.

</details>


### [165] [Model complexity in econometrics - a combinatorial analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01489)
*Vahidin Jeleskovic*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对回归模型和VAR模型在规范选择中被忽视的状态数量问题，提出了一种组合框架来计算模型可能的状态数，并呼吁学界关注这一理论缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在确定模型最优状态数量（即系统可假设的状态数）方面存在显著空白，而AIC/BIC等准则虽能平衡拟合度与复杂度，却未触及状态空间的组合可能性问题。

Method: 提出一个组合框架，将模型开发划分为四个不同阶段，每个阶段提供一系列规格选择，从而对所有可能状态进行全面的组合计算。

Result: 该方法能够系统性地枚举和计算计量经济学模型中所有可能的状态配置，揭示了传统模型选择准则未覆盖的理论维度。

Conclusion: 需要建立理论复杂性准则来阐明基本局限性并提出新的优化目标，以提升计量经济模型的精度和适用性。

Abstract: Regression models and Vector Autoregressive Models (VARs) play crucial roles in econometrics by allowing the analysis of multiple variables simultaneously. Despite their utility, these models face challenges like underfitting and overfitting, especially when determining the optimal model specification, which can lead to significant computational costs. To address these challenges, econometricians often rely on widely adopted model selection criteria such as the Akaike Information Criterion (AIC) and Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC). These criteria help balance model complexity and goodness of fit, aiding in the selection of the most suitable model specification for the given data. Nonetheless, there is a notable gap in existing research concerning the correct specification of these models, particularly in determining the optimal number of states a system can assume. Addressing this gap, we introduce a combinatorial framework designed to calculate the potential number of states in such econometric models. Our approach involves delineating four distinct stages in model development, each offering a range of specifications. This method enables a comprehensive combinatorial calculation of all possible states. The aim of this paper is to highlight this overlooked aspect of model specification and to spark a constructive dialogue within the empirical research community. By doing so, we hope to inspire further research that enhances the precision and applicability of econometric models. A theoretical complexity criterion is necessary to elucidate fundamental limitations and propose new objectives to pursue.

</details>


### [166] [Mapping the Storm: Geospatial Impacts of Severe Weather on LEO Network Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01724)
*Sina Ehsani,Bhanu Pallakonda,Pragyana K. Mishra*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用超过87万终端小时的分钟级遥测数据，对Starlink低轨卫星网络在全美范围内受天气影响的性能退化进行了大规模地理空间分析。研究发现，雷暴、强雨雪等恶劣天气会导致超过55%的受影响终端出现显著性能下降，服务中断可持续数分钟至数小时，并提出了天气感知网络规划与弹性通信系统设计的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星星座（如Starlink）在提供全球宽带连接方面日益重要，但其可靠性和性能对环境动态（尤其是具有强时空变异性的局地天气现象）高度敏感。目前缺乏大规模实证研究来量化天气对LEO卫星网络性能的影响。

Method: 收集了来自1,292个Starlink终端的超过87万终端小时的分钟级遥测数据，结合高分辨率局地天气观测数据，采用空间连接技术和时间对齐相关性分析，评估了多种气象条件下的延迟、丢包率和信号质量等关键性能指标。

Result: 恶劣天气（如雷暴、强雨雪）对网络性能有显著影响，超过55%的受影响终端经历了实质性性能退化；分钟级时间连续性分析显示退化可导致持续数分钟到数小时的服务中断或完全故障。

Conclusion: 本文是第一项将LEO卫星互联网性能与精细时空天气数据相关联的大规模实证研究，为地理空间预测建模、天气感知网络规划和弹性卫星通信系统设计提供了可行的见解，并提出了将天气推断的性能变异性纳入未来地理空间规划和SLA预测工具的框架。

Abstract: LEO satellite constellations, led by deployments such as Starlink, are playing an increasingly pivotal role in enabling global broadband connectivity. However, the reliability and performance of these space-based networks are highly sensitive to environmental dynamics, particularly localized weather phenomena that exhibit strong spatio-temporal variability. In this study, we present a continental-scale geospatial analysis of weather-induced performance degradation in the Starlink LEO network, with a focus on the contiguous United States. Leveraging a unique dataset comprising more than 870,000 terminal hours of minute-level telemetry from 1,292 Starlink terminals, we integrate high-resolution localized weather observations to quantify the impact of various meteorological conditions. We evaluated key performance indicators (KPIs)-including ping latency, ping drop rate, and signal quality-using spatial join techniques and time-aligned correlation with classified weather events. Our analysis reveals that severe weather events, such as thunderstorms with heavy rain or snow, have a pronounced effect on network performance. In particular, more than 55% affected terminals experienced substantial degradation. Temporal continuity analysis at the minute level shows that such degradation can lead to sustained impairments or full service outages lasting from several minutes to multiple hours.This work contributes to the first large-scale empirical study linking LEO satellite Internet performance with fine-grained weather data in both space and time. Our findings offer actionable insights for geospatial predictive modeling, weather-aware network provisioning, and resilient satellite communication system design. We also propose a framework for incorporating weather-inferred performance variability into future geospatial planning and service-level forecasting tools for LEO-based Internet systems.

</details>


### [167] [Bandwidth selection with a frequency-domain version of the AIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02295)
*Erhard Reschenhofer*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文论证了在非参数谱密度估计中，使用最简单的频域AIC自动选择带宽，能够取得与标准参数方法（AR模型+AIC）相当的结果，旨在推动客观自动方法取代主观视觉方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非参数谱密度估计缺乏标准的自动方法，实践中仍广泛使用主观视觉方法确定平滑参数，而参数方法（AR模型+AIC）已成为简洁可靠的标准。作者希望推动客观自动方法的应用，减少主观性。

Method: 采用最简单的频域版本AIC作为带宽自动选择准则，将其应用于非参数谱密度估计中，并与标准参数方法（AR模型+阶数选择的AIC）进行对比。使用真实时间序列和合成时间序列（具有不同复杂度的谱密度）进行评估。

Result: 使用频域AIC自动选择带宽的非参数方法，在估计效果上与标准的参数方法（AR模型+AIC）表现相当，无论是真实数据还是合成数据。

Conclusion: 最简单的频域AIC可以作为一种有效的自动带宽选择方法用于非参数谱密度估计，其结果可媲美参数方法，值得在实践中推广使用。

Abstract: When it comes to estimating an unknown spectral density as simply and reliably as possible, parametric spectral density estimation using AR models and order selection via AIC is the method of choice. In contrast, no standard method has yet emerged for automatic nonparametric spectral density estimation, and there seems to be little willingness to weigh the advantages and disadvantages of different risk functions and the various methods for estimating them on a case-by-case basis, particularly because it is unclear whether the effort is even worthwhile without concrete prior information about the unknown spectral density. As a result, subjective visual methods are still widely used in practice to determine the appropriate smoothing parameter for a nonparametric estimation. This article aims to encourage the increased use of objective automatic methods by presenting evidence that using what is arguably the simplest and most straightforward frequency-domain version of the AIC for the automatic determination of an appropriate bandwidth enables results that are comparable to those obtained using the standard parametric approach. This evidence is based on both real-world time series and synthetic time series with spectral densities of varying complexity.

</details>


### [168] [AI and physics-based weather forecasting: A comparative study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02508)
*Mátyás Kocsis,Sándor Baran*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了ECMWF基于AI的预报系统（AIFS）与传统物理模型IFS在2025年7月至11月期间全球9000多个站点10米风速中期集合预报的表现。原始IFS预报显著优于原始AIFS；后处理（EMOS和QR）大幅缩小两者差距，但IFS在短预报时效仍保持优势；AIFS计算速度快10倍、能耗低约1000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型在气象预报领域兴起，ECMWF开发了AIFS集合预报模型并于2025年7月投入业务化运行。需要系统评估这一新型AI模型与作为黄金标准的传统物理模型IFS在预报性能上的差异，尤其是在后处理校正条件下的对比。

Method: 选取2025年7月至11月期间全球9000多个地面观测站的10米风速中期集合预报数据，分别对IFS和AIFS的原始预报及后处理预报进行对比评估。后处理采用参数化集合模型输出统计（EMOS）和非参数化分位数回归（QR）两种方法。

Result: 原始IFS集合预报在所有预报时效上均显著优于原始AIFS；后处理显著提升了两个模型的预报技巧；EMOS在多数验证指标上优于QR，尤其在短预报时效；后处理大幅缩小了IFS与AIFS之间的技巧差异，但在短预报时效IFS仍显著优于AIFS。

Conclusion: 尽管AIFS在计算速度和能耗上具有巨大优势（快10倍、能耗低约1000倍），但其原始预报精度仍不及传统IFS模型。后处理能有效缩小两者差距，但IFS在短预报时效仍保持领先。AIFS作为业务化AI预报模型的先驱，已在效率和精度之间展现出可行的发展方向。

Abstract: In the last few years, AI-based models have become the centre of attention in weather forecasting due to their increasing accuracy and efficiency. Pioneering among weather services, ECMWF has developed its Artificial Intelligence Forecasting System (AIFS) model, which was first to provide data-driven ensemble forecasts in June 2024. Since July 2025, the AIFS ensemble model has been operational and runs in parallel with ECMWF's physics-based Integrated Forecasting System (IFS), which is considered the gold standard in weather prediction. The new AIFS model can generate forecasts ten times faster than the classical numerical weather prediction model, while consuming approximately a thousand times less energy. We present the results of our systematic assessment of the performance of the IFS and AIFS models by comparing the accuracy of raw and post-processed medium-range 10-m wind-speed ensemble forecasts generated operationally by the two models for the period between July and November 2025 for more than 9000 synoptic observation stations across the globe. The post-processed case involves the parametric ensemble model output statistics (EMOS) as well as the non-parametric quantile regression (QR) approach to correct any systematic inaccuracies in the raw forecasts. The predictive performance of raw IFS ensemble forecasts proves to be substantially superior to the skill of the raw AIFS predictions for all investigated forecast horizons. As expected, post-processing significantly improves the skill of both IFS and AIFS predictions, and, across most verification metrics, EMOS is superior to QR, especially for short lead times. Compared to the raw ensemble, the differences in skill between the matching IFS and AIFS predictions are substantially decreased by post-processing and are mostly significant at short lead times, when the IFS forecasts outperform their AIFS counterparts.

</details>


### [169] [Probabilistic storyline attribution using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02550)
*Frieder Loer,Maybritt Schillinger,Sebastian Sippel*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出使用分布自编码器（DAEs）生成基于故事线的气候反事实，用于归因极端天气事件。DAEs能够建模大气环流状态和全球变暖水平条件下欧洲温度场的全分布，从而计算条件概率比。以2003年欧洲热浪为例，模拟了2028年和2053年的反事实情景，显示条件强度分别增至30.3°C和32.1°C，条件概率比分别为2.1和3.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候归因中，故事线方法需要将观测事件在不同气候强迫状态间转移，但物理模型无法直接实现。需要一种能够生成环流条件约束下不同气候状态反事实的方法。

Method: 使用分布自编码器（DAEs）对大气环流状态和全球平均变暖水平条件下的欧洲温度场全分布进行建模，训练于完全耦合气候模型模拟，并在不同事实和故事线反事实模拟中评估。

Result: 以2003年欧洲热浪为例，生成2028年和2053年反事实，条件强度从29.3°C分别增至30.3°C和32.1°C，条件概率比分别为2.1和3.2。

Conclusion: DAEs是一种生成气候反事实的多功能方法，能够导出有意义的条件概率比，为故事线归因提供了优势。

Abstract: A fundamental goal in climate attribution is to estimate how forced climate change contributes to observed extreme weather events. The storyline attribution method compares an observed weather event, conditional on its atmospheric dynamic state (i.e., atmospheric circulation), in the current, 'factual' climate to an event with very similar circulation conditions in a hypothetical, 'counterfactual' climate. However, physical climate models cannot directly transfer these storyline counterfactuals across different climate forcing states. Statistical and machine learning techniques may overcome this limitation; yet, emulating circulation-conditional extreme events under different climate states is challenging. Here, we demonstrate distributional autoencoders (DAEs) as a versatile method for generating climate counterfactuals. They model the full distribution of spatially resolved European temperature fields conditional on the atmospheric circulation state and the mean global warming level. These distributions allow for deriving meaningful conditional probability ratios, which is a particular advantage of the DAE-based storyline approach. We train DAEs on fully coupled climate model simulations and we evaluate the modelled distributions across different factual and storyline-based counterfactual climate model simulations. In an illustrative case study, we revisit the 2003 European heatwave and we generate counterfactuals for a hypothetical `2003-like European heatwave' using ERA5 circulation, which we hypothesize to occur a quarter century (2028) and a half century (2053) after 2003. The conditional intensity would increase from 29.3 °C in 2003, to 30.3 °C and 32.1 °C in 2028 and 2053, respectively and conditional probability ratios would be 2.1 and 3.2 when compared to 2003.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [170] [Error bounds for approximate posteriors from likelihood-informed reduced-order models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00274)
*Han Cheng Lie,Jakob Scheffels,Elisabeth Ullmann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对线性高斯贝叶斯逆问题，分析了一种基于似然信息子空间的Petrov-Galerkin投影降阶模型，推导了先验预条件海森矩阵、后验协方差和后验均值的误差界，并证明当降阶模型秩等于问题固有维度时可精确恢复后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯逆问题中正问题模型计算成本高昂，而现有降阶模型的理论性质尚不明确，需系统分析其近似误差及对后验解的影响。

Method: 针对线性高斯逆问题（允许奇异先验协方差），分析基于似然信息子空间的Petrov-Galerkin投影降阶方法，推导先验预条件数据失配海森矩阵的近似误差界，并进一步给出后验协方差和均值的误差界。

Result: 推导了降阶模型近似先验预条件海森矩阵的误差界，以及后验协方差和后验均值的误差界；证明当降阶秩等于先验预条件海森矩阵的秩（固有维度）时，可精确恢复真实后验；两个结构工程数值实验验证了误差界的有效性。

Conclusion: 该降阶模型在理论上有严格的误差保证，且能在适当秩下实现后验的精确恢复，为贝叶斯逆问题的高效近似求解提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In the design of computational methods for Bayesian inverse problems, costly forward model evaluations make it difficult to sample from or compute the posterior. This motivates the need for approximate forward models that are cheaper to evaluate. We consider reduced-order forward models which exploit the lower-dimensional structure in the Bayesian inverse problem by projecting to the "likelihood-informed subspace" of the parameter space where the prior-to-posterior update is significant. However, the theoretical properties of these reduced-order forward models and their impact on the solution of the Baysian inverse problem are not always well-understood. In this work we consider linear Gaussian inverse problems with a possibly singular prior covariance matrix. We analyse a recently proposed reduced-order model which uses a Petrov-Galerkin projection to likelihood-informed subspaces that arise in optimal low-rank approximations of the posterior covariance matrix. We bound the error in the resulting approximation of the root prior-preconditioned Hessian of the data misfit. Based on this we also bound the errors of the approximate posterior covariance and mean. Our analysis shows that this reduced-order model recovers the exact posterior when the rank of the reduced-order model is equal to the "intrinsic dimension" of the inverse problem, i.e. the rank of the prior-preconditioned Hessian. Two numerical experiments from structural engineering illustrate the performance of our bounds.

</details>


### [171] [Symmetric Hermite quadrature-based balanced truncation for learning linear dynamical systems from derivative data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00298)
*Sean Reiter,Steffen W. R. Werner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对称Hermite形式的四象限平衡截断算法，通过利用全阶系统的传递函数及其导数信息构建线性降阶模型，并保持系统的状态空间对称性和渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的降阶建模是控制系统计算机辅助设计的关键组成部分，现有方法在保持系统定性性质（如对称性和稳定性）方面存在不足，需要一种能够保留这些性质的降阶算法。

Method: 提出了一种对称Hermite形式的四象限平衡截断算法，利用全阶系统传递函数及其导数的评估数据来构建线性降阶模型，通过Hermite形式确保状态空间对称性和渐近稳定性等定性性质得以保留。

Result: 该Hermite形式能够保留用于生成数据的全阶系统的定性性质，包括状态空间对称性和渐近稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的对称Hermite形式四象限平衡截断算法能有效构建保持系统关键定性性质的降阶模型，适用于数据驱动的控制系统设计。

Abstract: Data-driven reduced-order modeling is an essential component in the computer-aided design of control systems. In this work, we present a novel symmetric Hermite formulation of the quadrature-based balanced truncation algorithm that constructs linear reduced-order models from evaluations of the full-order system's transfer function and its derivative. Significantly, the Hermite formulation preserves desirable qualitative properties of the system used to generate the data, such as state-space Hermiticity and, consequently, asymptotic stability.

</details>


### [172] [Stochastic Rounding Increases Small Singular Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00312)
*Linkai Ma,Tingzhou Yu,Petros Drineas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明随机舍入（SR）的谱正则化效应不仅限于极端纵横比矩阵，而且能提升整个奇异值谱尾部的簇，而非仅最小奇异值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究仅发现SR对极端细长或扁平矩阵的最小奇异值有正则化作用，但对其在更普遍矩阵上的谱效应理解有限。

Method: 通过理论分析，从两个方向扩展：1) 证明SR正则化效应在恒定纵横比矩阵中依然存在；2) 揭示SR提升的是整个奇异值谱尾部的簇，而非仅最小奇异值。

Result: SR的正则化效应在恒定纵横比矩阵中持续存在；SR作用于整个奇异值谱尾部簇，提供更广泛的谱正则化。

Conclusion: 随机舍入是一种广义的谱正则化器，其效应超越极端纵横比，作用于奇异值谱的更大范围。

Abstract: Over the past half-dozen years, stochastic rounding (SR) has regained significant attention as a quantization scheme for low-precision floating-point arithmetic, with applications spanning numerical analysis and modern machine learning systems. Recent work has shown that SR acts as an implicit regularizer by increasing the smallest singular value of extremely tall-and-thin (or, symmetrically, short-and-fat) matrices. In this work, we substantially sharpen and extend this understanding in two directions. First, we show that the regularization effect of SR is not restricted to extreme aspect ratio regimes: it persists for matrices with constant aspect ratio. Second, we demonstrate that SR does not merely regularize the smallest singular value, but instead lifts entire clusters of singular values at the tail of the spectrum. Together, these results provide a more general characterization of stochastic rounding as a spectral regularizer, revealing that its effects extend beyond extremal aspect ratios and act on a broader portion of the singular value spectrum.

</details>


### [173] [A Lifting-Based Interface Reduction Framework for Nonlinear Transmission and Eigenvalue Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00468)
*So-Hsiang Chou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于提升（lifting）的界面降阶框架，用于非线性传输问题和特征值问题。该方法将解分解为体分量和承载界面跳跃的提升分量，将原问题简化为定义在界面上的非线性系统。通过将界面未知量限制在有限维子空间得到低维近似，提升模式预先计算并重用。数值实验表明，近似精度和谱行为主要由界面表示决定而非体网格离散，少量界面模式即可有效控制问题行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型降阶方法在处理非线性传输问题和特征值问题时，往往需要处理高维体离散，计算成本高昂。本文旨在开发一种将问题简化为界面表示的低维框架，以大幅降低计算复杂度，同时保持足够的近似精度和谱结构捕捉能力。

Method: 1) 将解分解为体分量和提升分量，提升分量承载界面跳跃，原问题简化为界面上的非线性系统；2) 界面未知量限制在有限维子空间，提升模式预计算并复用；3) 体算子保持固定，核心行为由少量界面自由度控制；4) 对特征值问题，通过参数依赖的界面矩阵近奇异性识别特征值，特征向量揭示主导界面模式。

Result: 数值实验表明：近似精度和谱行为主要由界面表示决定而非体网格离散；丰富界面空间能快速提高精度并揭示额外特征模式；而单纯加密体网格效果有限。

Conclusion: 传输问题和特征值问题实际上由少量界面模式主导，本文框架提供了一种简洁且计算高效的模型降阶视角，界面表示是控制问题行为的关键因素。

Abstract: We present a lifting-based interface reduction framework for nonlinear transmission and eigenvalue problems. The method represents the solution as a sum of a bulk component and a lifting component that carries the interface jump, thereby reducing the original problem to a nonlinear system posed on the interface.
  A low-dimensional approximation is obtained by restricting the interface unknown to a finite-dimensional subspace. The corresponding lifting modes are precomputed and reused, leading to a formulation in which the bulk operator remains fixed and the essential behavior is governed by a small number of interface degrees of freedom.
  For eigenvalue problems, the same framework yields a reduced system in which eigenvalues are identified through the near-singularity of a parameter-dependent interface matrix. The associated eigenvectors reveal dominant interface modes, providing a direct interpretation of the spectral structure.
  Numerical experiments show that both approximation accuracy and spectral behavior are determined primarily by the interface representation rather than by the bulk discretization. In particular, enriching the interface space rapidly improves accuracy and reveals additional eigenmodes, while mesh refinement alone has limited effect.
  These results indicate that transmission and eigenvalue problems are effectively governed by a small number of interface modes, offering a simple and computationally efficient perspective on model reduction.

</details>


### [174] [Deep neural network yields regularization for ill-posed inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00517)
*Qiao Zhu,Lan Wang,Ye Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度神经网络对不适定逆问题的正则化方法，提出了基于架构正则化的自适应扩张深度网络框架，并设计了两种基于差异原理的扩张算法，证明了算法的有限终止性和解的收敛性，给出了网络复杂度的渐近界，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基于架构的正则化从浅层网络扩展到深层模型，以解决不适定逆问题的正则化难题。

Method: 提出确定性框架，自适应扩大可容许网络类；设计两种基于差异原理的扩张DNN算法（分别处理参数半径已知和未知的情况）；证明自适应扩张过程的有限终止性和正则化解的收敛性；推导终端网络架构的渐近界。

Result: 证明了两种算法的有限终止性和噪声水平趋于零时正则化解的收敛性，导出了网络复杂度随噪声水平标度的渐近界，数值实验验证了理论结果和实用性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效实现了深度网络对不适定逆问题的正则化，网络架构复杂度充当了正则化机制，理论分析和实验均支持该方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies the regularization of ill-posed inverse problems by deep neural networks (DNNs). We extend architecture-based regularization from shallow networks to deep models by developing a deterministic framework in which the admissible network class is enlarged adaptively and the resulting architecture complexity acts as the regularization mechanism. We propose two discrepancy-principle-driven expanding DNN algorithms to treat the cases where an explicit parameter-radius bound is available and unavailable, respectively. For both algorithms, we prove the finite termination of the adaptive expansion procedure and the convergence of the regularized solutions as the noise level vanishes. In addition, we derive explicit asymptotic bounds on the terminal network architecture, thereby quantifying how the required network complexity scales with the noise level. Numerical experiments on several representative linear and non-linear inverse problems support the theoretical findings and illustrate the practical usefulness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [175] [Tensor gradient flow with quasi-entropy for smectic liquid crystals and discretizations keeping coupled physical constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00659)
*Jie Xu,Xiaomei Yao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文构建了描述层状液晶的浓度梯度流与2×2张量模型，通过严格凸的准熵项实现浓度与张量的耦合约束，提出边界法向导数演化方程以保证能量耗散，设计了满足分部求和的数值格式，并证明了存在唯一性、能量耗散和误差估计，数值实验观察到不同于其他层结构的缺陷构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有液晶模型在处理浓度与张量耦合约束时存在困难，且对边界法向导数的演化机制缺乏系统描述，导致无法准确模拟层状液晶的缺陷结构。

Method: 构建浓度梯度流与2×2张量模型；自由能包含熵项（耦合浓度与张量的严格凸下半连续准熵）和含二阶空间导数平方的相互作用项；额外提出浓度边界法向导数的演化方程；数值格式利用熵项保持耦合约束，并采用满足分部求和的边界法向导数离散化。

Result: 建立了格式的存在唯一性、能量耗散与误差估计；数值实验验证了高效性和鲁棒性；观察到与其他层结构不同的缺陷构型。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型和数值格式能有效描述层状液晶的浓度-张量耦合行为，捕获新颖的缺陷构型。

Abstract: A gradient flow for the concentration and a $2\times 2$ tensor is constructed to describe smectic liquid crystals. The free energy consists of the entropy term and interaction term involving squared second order spatial derivatives. The entropy term incorporates the concentration in the quasi-entropy originally proposed for the tensor only, which is a strictly convex and lower semicontinuous function imposing coupled constraints between the concentration and the tensor. An evolution equation for the boundary normal derivative of the concentration is proposed in addition to the equations for the concentration and the tensor, giving an energy dissipation system. Numerical schemes are designed with emphases on using the entropy term to keep the coupled constraints, and the discretization of the boundary normal derivatives satisfying summation by parts. Existence, uniqueness, energy dissipation and error estimates are established. Numerical results indicate the efficiency and robustness of the scheme. Configurations of defects different from other layer structures are observed.

</details>


### [176] [A piecewise constant levelset approach for semi-blind deconvolution: Application to barcode decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01156)
*Adriano De Cezaro,Eduardo Hafemann,Antonio Leitão*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对模糊线性条码解码中的半盲反卷积问题，基于分段常数水平集（PCLS）框架，提出并分析了一种基于增广拉格朗日的求解方法，建立了广义乘子的存在性和对偶间隙的缺失，提供了正则化理论依据，并给出了ADMM型迭代算法，数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模糊线性条码解码中，测量噪声会导致反问题求解不稳定，现有方法缺乏系统的理论保证，需要一种既能稳定求解又具备正则化理论基础的策略。

Method: 基于PCLS框架，采用增广拉格朗日方法处理半盲反卷积问题；理论上证明了广义乘子的存在性及对偶间隙的缺失，为正则化性质提供基础；设计了ADMM型迭代算法进行条码近似计算。

Result: 建立了增广拉格朗日泛函广义乘子的存在性，证明了对偶间隙不存在；数值实验在不同模糊方差和噪声水平下验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的增广拉格朗日方法结合ADMM算法能够有效且稳定地求解模糊条码解码反问题，具备坚实的正则化理论基础。

Abstract: We consider a semi-blind deconvolution problem arising in the decoding of blurred linear barcodes. Building on the Piecewise Constant Level Set (PCLS) framework introduced in [De\,Cezaro et al., Inv.\,Probl., 29 (2013), 015003], we propose and analyze a solution method based on augmented Lagrangians to obtain stable approximate solutions to the corresponding inverse problem with respect to noisy measurements. We establish the existence of generalized multipliers for the augmented Lagrangian functional under consideration, as well as the absence of duality gaps. These results provide the theoretical foundation required to prove regularization properties of the approximate solutions produced by the proposed strategy. Furthermore, we present an associated ADMM-type iterative scheme for the explicit computation of approximate barcodes. Numerical experiments are carried out for various variance values (responsible for the blurred effect) and several levels of noise, validating the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [177] [Residual-Weighted Randomized Jacobi: Sharpened Bounds via Residual Concentration and Asynchronous Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01232)
*Evan Coleman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究对称正定线性系统的随机定常迭代方法，其中分量以概率正比于|r_j|^ℓ被选取，该族方法在均匀随机Jacobi (ℓ→0) 与Gauss–Southwell贪婪松弛 (ℓ→∞) 之间连续插值。针对ℓ=2的核心情形，利用逆参与率(IPR) ν²(r) 改进单步收敛分析，得到预期进度被ν²因子放大的界。将分析扩展到异步情形，IPR同时控制进度系数和允许延迟窗口。实验表明IPR轨迹对并发度不敏感，但一致读执行在高并发下会破坏加权采样而弱一致读保持稳定，并提出反馈阻尼机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机定常迭代方法在均匀采样与贪婪选择之间存在空白，缺乏连续统系的理论框架。此外，异步并行环境下功率加权采样的收敛行为与线程交互效应尚未被充分理解。

Method: (1) 定义概率与|r_j|^ℓ成正比的功率加权Jacobi族，涵盖均匀随机Jacobi (ℓ=0) 到Gauss–Southwell (ℓ→∞)；(2) 针对ℓ=2，利用逆参与率ν²(r)=n‖r‖₄⁴/‖r‖₂⁴推导紧的单步收敛界；(3) 通过Avron–Druinsky–Gupta框架扩展至异步情形，导出基于epoch的收敛定理；(4) 提出反馈阻尼机制处理高并发下的不稳定性。

Result: ℓ=2时收敛界被IPR精确放大ν²倍；IPR可在O(n)时间内在线计算并作为迭代诊断指标。异步分析中IPR同时控制进度系数与允许延迟窗口。实验确认IPR轨迹对并发度不敏感；发现一致读执行在高并发下破坏功率加权采样，而弱一致读保持稳定。

Conclusion: 功率加权Jacobi族为随机定常迭代提供了统一的理论框架，IPR是联系精度与异步并行行为的关键量。实验结果揭示了弱一致读在异步功率加权采样中的优势，反馈阻尼机制有效缓解了高并发不稳定性。

Abstract: We study randomized stationary methods for symmetric positive definite linear systems in which component $j$ is selected with probability proportional to $|r_j|^\ell$. This power-weighted family interpolates continuously between uniform randomized Jacobi as $\ell \to 0$ and Gauss--Southwell greedy relaxation as $\ell \to \infty$. For the central case $\ell = 2$, we sharpen the standard one-step convergence analysis using the inverse participation ratio (IPR) $ν^2(r) = n\|r\|_4^4/\|r\|_2^4$, which equals $1$ when the residual is uniform and grows toward $n$ as it concentrates. The resulting bound amplifies the expected per-step progress by exactly $ν^2$ over the uniform-sampling baseline. The IPR can be computed online at $O(n)$ cost and doubles as a per-iteration diagnostic.
  We extend the analysis to asynchronous power-weighted Jacobi via the Avron--Druinsky--Gupta framework, obtaining an epoch-based convergence theorem in which the IPR controls both the progress coefficient and the allowed-delay window. Numerical experiments on shared-memory hardware support the sharpened bound and show the IPR trajectory is essentially concurrency-insensitive. Unexpectedly, consistent-reads execution, the easier case for the ADG analysis, destabilizes power-weighted sampling at high concurrency while inconsistent reads remain stable; the same IPR that amplifies progress amplifies a thread-collision rate that inconsistent reads appear to absorb. We propose a feedback-damping mechanism and verify two predictions about its dependence on problem size.

</details>


### [178] [Multicontinuum Generalized Multiscale Finite Element Method (MC-GMsFEM). Theory and applications to upscaling of two-phase flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01303)
*Mohammed Al Kobaisi,Dmitry Ammosov,Yalchin Efendiev,Wing Tat Leung*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多连续介质广义多尺度有限元方法（MC-GMsFEM），统一了多尺度有限元构造与多连续介质表示，能够在异质介质中同时提供精确的数值近似和物理一致的宏观方程，特别适用于两相不可混溶流动的升尺度模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典多尺度方法虽然能高效逼近细尺度解，但缺乏系统推导宏观控制方程的途径，难以建立多尺度离散化与多连续介质均匀化之间的直接联系。

Method: 基于GMsFEM框架，利用多尺度基函数的表示分离粗变量及其梯度；通过带积分约束的局部问题构造依赖于连续介质的辅助场基函数，实现粗尺度解到连续变量及其梯度的分解。

Result: 该方法在空间变化连续介质数量的异质介质中具有更大灵活性，能够自然嵌入标准有限元框架，并推导出新的宏观模型；当使用经典模型时，误差可能很大。

Conclusion: MC-GMsFEM为多物理场异质介质问题提供了一种系统性的宏观方程推导途径，兼具理论一致性与数值实现简便性。

Abstract: We develop a multicontinuum Generalized Multiscale Finite Element Method (MC-GMsFEM) for constructing coarse-scale models in heterogeneous media that simultaneously provide accurate numerical approximations and physically consistent macroscopic equations. Classical multiscale methods efficiently approximate fine-scale solutions on coarse grids using localized basis functions, but they do not offer a systematic pathway for deriving macroscopic governing equations. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a unified framework that integrates multiscale finite element constructions with multicontinuum representations. The proposed method builds on the structure of GMsFEM and exploits a representation of multiscale basis functions that separates coarse variables and their gradients. We construct continuum-dependent basis functions using auxiliary fields defined through local problems with integral constraints, ensuring that each basis function is associated with a specific continuum. This leads to a decomposition of the coarse-scale solution into continuum variables and their gradients, establishing a direct connection between multiscale discretizations and multicontinuum homogenization. Compared to existing multicontinuum approaches, the proposed framework provides greater flexibility in handling heterogeneous media with spatially varying numbers of continua and is naturally embedded within a standard finite element setting. This enables both systematic derivation of macroscopic equations and straightforward numerical implementation. We apply the proposed method to the upscaling of two-phase immiscible flow in heterogeneous porous media, where multiple interacting continua, including mobile and trapped phases, arise. With the proposed approaches, we derive new macroscopic models and show that if classical models are used, the errors can be large.

</details>


### [179] [Transpose-free linear algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01335)
*Diana Halikias,Michiel E. Hochstenbach,Alex Townsend*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了无转置矩阵计算（仅通过前向矩阵-向量乘积访问矩阵A）的理论局限性。研究表明，缺乏转置访问会导致Krylov方法中投影算子范数序列可任意变化、最小二乘等核心问题不可识别，并给出了近似任务（如秩k近似需至少n次查询）的定量下界，揭示了无转置环境在可识别性和效率上的根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子学习、逆问题和矩阵-free PDE求解器等领域需要仅通过前向矩阵-向量乘积（matvecs）访问矩阵A，而无法访问其转置AT或其运算。作者旨在系统性地理解这种无转置访问设置下算法在理论上存在哪些根本性限制，揭示其在可识别性和计算效率上的壁垒。

Method: 通过理论分析建立多个核心问题的不可识别性结果（即不同矩阵可生成完全相同的查询记录却具有根本不同的解），证明Krylov方法中Arnoldi迭代投影算子范数序列可任意变化，并推导近似任务（如秩k近似、Frobenius范数估计）的查询复杂度下界。

Result: (1) Krylov方法中Arnoldi迭代产生的投影算子范数序列可遵循任意非递减曲线；(2) 最小二乘、范数估计、列子集选择、局部最大体积等问题存在非可识别性；(3) 计算近最优秩k近似至少需要n次查询；(4) 以相对精度ε估计Frobenius范数需要Ω(ε⁻²)次查询（n足够大时），匹配Hutchinson类估计器的复杂度常数。

Conclusion: 无转置访问设置虽然部分问题仍可解，但在可识别性和计算效率上从根本上比有转置访问环境更受限制，存在严重且有时无法克服的理论障碍。

Abstract: We study the limitations of matrix-free algorithms that access a matrix $A$ only through forward matrix-vector products (matvecs) $x \mapsto Ax$, without access to the transpose $A^\top$ or its action. This setting arises naturally in operator learning, inverse problems, and matrix-free PDE solvers, where adjoint evaluations may be unavailable or prohibitively expensive. We show that the lack of transpose access creates severe and sometimes insurmountable theoretical barriers. For Krylov methods, we prove that the sequence of projected operator norms produced by Arnoldi iteration can follow any prescribed nondecreasing curve, showing that forward matvecs alone provide essentially no reliable information about the spectral norm. For several core problems, including least squares, norm estimation, column subset selection, and local maximum volume, we establish non-identifiability results; distinct matrices can generate identical forward-query transcripts while having fundamentally different solutions. We also prove quantitative lower bounds on the number of forward matvecs required for approximation tasks. In particular, any algorithm that computes a near-optimal rank-$k$ approximation must use at least $n$ queries, and estimating the Frobenius norm to relative accuracy $\eps$ requires $Ω(\eps^{-2})$ queries when $n$ is sufficiently large, matching the complexity of Hutchinson-type estimators up to constants. Although some problems remain solvable without transpose access, the transpose-free setting is fundamentally more limited in both identifiability and efficiency.

</details>


### [180] [Ferrofluids: Modeling and Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01392)
*Gonzalo A. Benavides,Ricardo H. Nochetto,Mansur Shakipov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文基于麦克斯韦方程和线性动量平衡方程，利用广义昂萨格原理推导出铁磁流体模型，并设计了收敛的Galerkin格式及其误差估计，最后通过数值实验验证了模型与方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一性原理出发推导铁磁流体的热力学一致模型，并建立可靠的数值方法以准确模拟其复杂行为。

Method: 基于麦克斯韦方程和线性动量平衡方程，结合广义昂萨格原理推导铁磁流体模型；利用离散扰动估计设计Galerkin有限元格式并分析收敛性。

Result: 推导了热力学一致的铁磁流体模型；设计了收敛的Galerkin格式并建立了误差估计；数值实验验证了模型和方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的铁磁流体模型及Galerkin数值方法具备理论上的收敛保证，且数值实验支持其适用性和准确性。

Abstract: Starting from Maxwell's and linear momentum balance equations, we derive a ferrofluid model using the generalized Onsager's principle. Guided by a discrete perturbation estimate, we design and analyze families of Galerkin schemes that converge to sufficiently regular solutions and derive error estimates. Finally, we numerically explore the model with our proposed method.

</details>


### [181] [Four-Level Overlapping Schwarz as Multigrid Coarse Solver for Incompressible Non-Newtonian Flow in Complex Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01433)
*Stephan Köhler,Oliver Rheinbach*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对复杂几何问题的四层重叠Schwarz预处理方法，通过递归应用两层GDSW粗空间构造，仅在最后一层使用直接求解器，有效解决了几何多重网格粗问题过大无法直接求解的难题。在三维不可压缩非牛顿Stokes问题上的数值实验（最高4000 MPI进程）表明该方法具有良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂几何问题中，几何多重网格的粗问题规模过大，无法使用直接求解器有效求解，亟需一种可扩展的粗问题求解策略。

Method: 采用递归方式将两层GDSW粗空间构造重复应用三次，构建四层重叠Schwarz预处理方法：第一至第三层粗问题使用重叠Schwarz方法处理，仅第四层（最粗层）使用稀疏直接求解器。该方法基于加法重叠Schwarz域分解和GDSW粗空间理论。

Result: 在三维不可压缩稳态Stokes问题（Carreau型非牛顿粘度模型，挤压模具复杂几何）上，最高在4000 MPI进程上进行了数值测试，并与两层、三层预处理方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 四层重叠Schwarz预处理方法通过递归粗化策略，将直接求解器仅用于最小规模的最粗层，有效解决了复杂几何下粗问题过大无法直接求解的瓶颈，为大规模并行计算提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: For complex geometries, the coarse problem of geometric multigrid can be too large to be solved by a direct solver. Here, we report on the use of domain decomposition applied to the multigrid coarse problem. Additive overlapping Schwarz methods are domain decomposition methods for the iterative solution of partial differential equations whose numerical and parallel scalability can be improved by the addition of coarse levels. A successful coarse space for such methods, inspired by iterative substructuring, is the generalized Dryja--Smith--Widlund (GDSW) space. A monolithic two-level overlapping Schwarz preconditioner based on a GDSW coarse space has been introduced for the solution of saddle-point problems arising from incompressible fluid problems, and has subsequently been extended to a three-level method. In the present work, for the first time, we consider a monolithic four-level overlapping Schwarz preconditioner, obtained by applying the two-level monolithic GDSW construction recursively three times, so that the second- and third-level coarse problems are themselves treated by overlapping Schwarz and only the smallest fourth-level coarse problem is solved by a sparse direct method. Numerical results are presented for a three-dimensional incompressible stationary Stokes problem with a Carreau-type non-Newtonian viscosity model posed on the complex geometry of an extrusion die, on up to $4\,000$\,MPI ranks, comparing the four-level preconditioner with its two-level and three-level counterparts in both roles. This work is part of the StroemungsRaum project, funded by the German Bundesministerium für Forschung, Technologie und Raumfahrt (BMFTR, formerly BMBF) as part of the SCALEXA program on new methods and technologies for exascale computing.

</details>


### [182] [Learning Chaotic Dynamics through Second-Order Geometric Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01596)
*Shinhoo Kang,Hai V. Nguyen,Tan Bui-Thanh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出模型约束随机雅可比匹配方法，通过隐式施加二阶一致性（Hessian匹配）来提升混沌动力系统学习的长期统计特性，在O(d²)成本下实现接近显式Hessian匹配的效果，避免了传统一阶方法在高维混沌系统中的吸引子漂移和Lyapunov指数失真问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的一阶方法（轨迹匹配和雅可比匹配）只约束向量场的值和切线结构，不约束场在切平面外的弯曲方式，导致模型在监督点局部准确但长期统计特性失真、吸引子漂移。二阶一致性虽能缓解这些问题，但显式Hessian匹配在d维系统中需O(d³)成本，高维不可行。

Method: 提出模型约束随机雅可比匹配（Randomized Jacobian Matching）：在随机扰动的输入点上比较真实和学习的雅可比矩阵。通过Taylor展开证明，随机雅可比损失的期望等于名义雅可比失配加上由噪声方差缩放的Hessian失配，从而以O(d²)成本隐式施加二阶一致性，无需构造O(d³)的Hessian张量。

Result: 在Lorenz 63上，一阶方法在极少数时间监督下产生灾难性Lyapunov指数离群值，二阶方法消除离群值并恢复正确吸引子。在耦合Lorenz 96上，外力F的分布外扫描显示：F≤16时所有方法一致，F>18时只有二阶方法能保持不变测度和Lyapunov谱。随机雅可比匹配性能与显式Hessian匹配相当，但成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: 二阶一致性对混沌系统长期统计特性的学习至关重要；随机雅可比匹配以极低的计算成本实现了二阶一致性，为高维混沌动力系统学习提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning chaotic dynamical systems from data requires more than short-term predictive accuracy: the learned model must preserve the attractor geometry and its invariant statistics. Trajectory (zero-order) and Jacobian (first-order) matching supervise the values and tangent structure of the vector field, but neither constrains how the field bends away from its tangent plane. A model can thus match values and tangents at the supervised states yet curve differently from the truth, remaining locally accurate while drifting toward spurious attractors and distorting long-time statistics. We show that enforcing second-order consistency mitigates these failures, but forming the full Hessian is prohibitive in high dimensions. We propose model-constrained randomized Jacobian matching, which compares the Jacobians of the true and learned vector fields at randomly perturbed inputs. A Taylor expansion shows that the expected randomized Jacobian loss decomposes into the nominal Jacobian mismatch plus a Hessian mismatch scaled by the noise variance, implicitly enforcing second-order consistency at $\mathcal{O}(d^2)$ cost without forming the $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$ Hessian tensor. Using only Jacobian evaluations, the method scales to high dimensions where explicit Hessian matching does not. Numerical experiments confirm that second-order methods are robust. For Lorenz~63, first-order methods produce catastrophic Lyapunov-exponent outliers under minimal temporal supervision, which second-order methods eliminate while recovering the correct attractor. For coupled Lorenz~96, an out-of-distribution forcing sweep separates the methods: all agree up to $F=16$, but beyond $F=18$ only second-order methods preserve the invariant measure and Lyapunov spectrum. On both systems, randomized Jacobian matching performs comparably to explicit Hessian matching at much lower cost.

</details>


### [183] [Stabilization-free virtual element methods based on finite element interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01614)
*Jikun Zhao,Wenhao Zhu,Bei Zhang,Shipeng Mao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限元插值策略的无稳定项虚拟单元法框架，通过构造可计算的、多项式保持且范数等价的插值算子，同时消除扩散项和反应项离散中的稳定项，并设计了两种无稳定项方案，在二维和三维中实现了保形和非保形虚拟单元的最优收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚拟单元法（VEM）需要额外的稳定项来保证刚度矩阵的稳定性，但稳定项引入了额外计算成本且参数选择敏感。本文旨在设计一种能够消除稳定项的策略，同时保持方法的收敛性和稳定性。

Method: 构造一个从虚拟单元空间到（局部）有限元空间的可计算、多项式保持且范数等价的插值算子；基于该算子设计两种无稳定项方案：方案一要求插值保持与双线性形式相关的多项式一致性，从而维持标准VEM的一致性和稳定性；方案二放宽了一致性要求，虽然不满足标准多项式一致性，但构造更简单、自由度更少，适用于非线性或变系数问题。在二维和三维中构造了保形和非保形虚拟单元的具体插值算子。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提方法的最优收敛速率。该框架可推广到其他多面体离散方法（如混合高阶法和弱Galerkin法）的设计中。

Conclusion: 提出的基于有限元插值的无稳定项VEM框架有效消除了扩散项和反应项中的稳定项，同时保持了最优收敛性；方案二在牺牲标准多项式一致性的情况下获得了更简单的构造和更广的适用性，为复杂问题（非线性、变系数）提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a new framework for designing stabilization-free virtual element methods (VEMs) based on an finite element interpolation-based strategy, where we can simultaneously eliminate the stabilization terms in the discretizations of diffusion and reaction terms. The core idea is to construct a computable, polynomial-preserving, and norm-equivalent interpolation operator from the virtual element space to a (local) finite element space. Leveraging the properties of this operator, we design two types of stabilization-free schemes. The first scheme requires the interpolation to preserve the polynomial consistency related to the bilinear forms, thereby maintaining both consistency and stability as in the standard VEM. The second scheme relaxes this consistency requirement. While it may not satisfy the standard polynomial consistency, the second scheme retains optimal convergence with simpler construction, fewer degrees of freedom and, more importantly, applicable to more complex problems such as those involving nonlinearities or variable coefficients. We construct concrete interpolation operators for both conforming and nonconforming virtual elements in two and three dimensions. These operators are then employed to realize stabilization-free schemes for conforming and nonconforming VEMs. Numerical experiments confirm the optimal convergence rates of the proposed methods. The presented framework can be extended to design stabilization-free schemes for other polytopal discretization methods, such as the hybrid high-order method and the weak Galerkin method.

</details>


### [184] [An alternating learning-based collocation method for solving inverse elliptic problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01622)
*Zhizhong Kong,Jerry Zhijian Yang,Cheng Yuan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出交替学习配点法(ALBC)用于求解椭圆逆问题，采用正弦浅层网络作为自适应基生成器，通过交替更新状态变量和未知参数将非凸联合优化分解为一系列可解的线性子问题，在理论上建立了稳定性估计并证明了收敛性，数值实验表明该方法在精度上优于标准配点法，计算成本远低于物理信息神经网络，且在20%噪声下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决椭圆逆问题中传统配点法固定基函数的局限性，以及深度学习方法收敛缓慢的问题，提出一种兼顾精度与计算效率的替代方案。

Method: 采用正弦浅层网络作为自适应基生成器，通过交替更新状态变量和未知参数，将原始非凸联合优化问题分解为一系列可解的线性子问题。

Result: 在五个基准问题上，ALBC方法精度一致优于标准配点法；性能与物理信息神经网络相当或更优，但计算成本大幅降低；在高达20%的噪声水平下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ALBC方法有效克服了传统配点法的固定基局限，避免了深度学习的慢收敛问题，是一种高效、鲁棒的椭圆逆问题求解方法。

Abstract: We propose the Alternating Learning-Based Collocation (ALBC) method for solving inverse elliptic problems. Our approach employs sinusoidal shallow networks as adaptive basis generators. By alternately updating the state variable and the unknown parameter, we decompose the original nonconvex joint optimization problem into a sequence of tractable linear subproblems. This strategy effectively overcomes the fixed-basis limitations of classical collocation methods while avoiding the slow convergence typically encountered in deep learning approaches. Theoretically, we establish stability estimates and prove the convergence of the proposed algorithm. Numerical experiments on five benchmark problems demonstrate the efficacy of ALBC, which consistently outperforms the standard collocation method in accuracy. Furthermore, it achieves performance comparable to or better than that of physics-informed neural networks at a substantially lower computational cost. Finally, the method remains robust under noise levels of up to twenty percent.

</details>


### [185] [Asymptotic Recovery in Fourier Spectral Methods for the Schrödinger Equation with Point Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01718)
*Yanjie Li,Sihong Shao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了傅里叶谱方法（FSM）求解含奇异势（如3D库仑势、1D狄拉克δ势）的薛定谔方程，证明了特征值的收敛阶为2s+2，特征函数在H¹范数下收敛阶为s+1，并发现投影特征函数存在超收敛现象（阶数s+1+b）。进一步提出渐近恢复（AR）后处理技术，将收敛阶提升至2s+2+2b和s+1+b，且计算成本与FSM自由度线性相关。数值实验验证了理论界的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有傅里叶谱方法处理奇异势（如库仑势、狄拉克δ势）时，收敛阶理论尚不完整，特别是超收敛现象未得到充分揭示和利用。本文旨在建立FSM在奇异势下的精确收敛阶理论，并开发高效的后处理技术以进一步提升精度。

Method: 1) 将Feshbach-Schur映射与精细摄动论证结合，推导FSM的收敛阶；2) 定义点奇异性的严格数学框架，建立特征函数的渐近展开（正则部分H^{s+4}+每个奇点相关的d+1个渐近函数）；3) 基于超收敛性质提出渐近恢复（AR）后处理技术（AR-FSM），以线性计算成本实现阶数提升。

Result: FSM原始收敛阶：特征值2s+2，特征函数H¹范数s+1；投影特征函数超收敛阶s+1+b（b=min{s+2-d/2-ε, s+1, 2}>0）。AR-FSM收敛阶：特征值2s+2+2b，特征函数H¹范数s+1+b。数值实验证实理论界是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 该工作为奇异势下谱方法的收敛性分析提供了完整理论框架，超收敛的发现和AR后处理技术的提出为高精度求解量子力学中的奇异势问题提供了实用工具，且计算效率优异。

Abstract: This paper studies the Fourier spectral method (FSM) for the Schrödinger equation with singular potentials $V \in H^{s}$, where $s > \max\{d/2-2,-1\}$ and $d$ denotes the spatial dimension. This setting includes a broad class of singular potentials, such as the 3D Coulomb potential and the 1D Dirac-delta potential. First, we combine the Feshbach-Schur map with a refined perturbation argument to derive sharp convergence orders for FSM, yielding order $2s+2$ for eigenvalues and order $s+1$ for eigenfunctions in the $H^1$ norm. More importantly, the $H^1$ error with respect to the projected eigenfunction converges with a higher order $s+1+b$, where $b=\min\{s+2-d/2-\varepsilon,\; s+1,\; 2\}>0$ for arbitrarily small $\varepsilon>0$, revealing a super-convergence phenomenon. Second, in the presence of potentials with isolated point singularities, we develop an asymptotic-recovery (AR) technique to post-process the FSM solutions. The resulting method, dubbed AR-FSM, fully exploits the super-convergence property and achieves convergence orders $2s+2+2b$ for eigenvalues and $s+1+b$ for eigenfunctions in the $H^1$ norm, while the AR post-processing requires only a computational cost that is linear in the number of FSM degrees of freedom. The analysis introduces a rigorous definition of point singularities and develops a foundational framework for their study. It further establishes an asymptotic expansion of eigenfunctions consisting of a regular component in $H^{s+4}$ together with $d+1$ asymptotic functions associated with each singular point. Numerical experiments confirm the sharpness of these theoretical bounds.

</details>


### [186] [The Immersed Discontinuous Galerkin Method for Elliptic Interface Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01814)
*Lin Yang,Qilong Zhai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种线性显式浸入式有限元（IFE）函数，该函数在界面单元上精确满足实际界面的界面条件，无需求解辅助问题或局部线性系统即可直接获得。尽管该IFE函数是非多项式的，但理论分析证明其具有最优逼近性质并满足必要的迹不等式，且常数与界面切割单元的方式无关。基于此IFE函数，开发了浸入式间断Galerkin数值格式，数值实验验证了IFE函数及数值方法在H¹和L²范数下均达到最优收敛率，且刚度矩阵条件数对界面位置具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浸入式有限元方法在界面单元上构造IFE函数时通常需要求解局部线性系统或辅助问题，计算成本较高。本文旨在构造一种显式的、可直接获得的IFE函数，同时保持最优逼近性质和界面条件精确满足的能力。

Method: 构造线性显式浸入式有限元函数，该函数在界面单元上精确满足实际界面条件，以显式形式直接给出无需求解辅助问题。基于该IFE函数发展浸入式间断Galerkin数值格式，并通过理论分析证明其最优逼近性质和迹不等式。

Result: 理论分析证明所构造的非多项式IFE函数具有最优逼近性质且满足必要的迹不等式，常数与界面切割元素的方式无关。数值实验验证了IFE函数及数值方法在H¹和L²范数下均达到最优收敛率，刚度矩阵条件数对界面位置具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的线性显式IFE函数在保持界面条件精确满足的同时，通过显式构造避免了求解局部线性系统，且具有最优逼近性质；基于该函数的浸入式间断Galerkin方法在收敛性和条件数鲁棒性方面表现优异。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to construction and convergence analysis of the linear explicit immersed finite element (IFE) function. For the interface elements, the proposed IFE functions precisely satisfy the interface conditions on the actual interface. The IFE functions are constructed in an explicit form and can be obtained directly without solving any auxiliary problems or local linear systems. Although the constructed IFE functions are non-polynomial, we establish rigorous theoretical analysis showing that they achieve optimal approximation properties and satisfy the essential trace inequalities. And the constants in the analysis are independent of how the interface cuts through the elements. Based on these IFE functions, an immersed discontinuous Galerkin numerical scheme is developed. Several numerical experiments are implemented to confirm that both the IFE functions and the numerical method achieve optimal convergence rates in the $H^1$ and $L^2$ norms. Furthermore, the numerical results indicate that the condition numbers of the stiffness matrices are robust with respect to the interface location.

</details>


### [187] [Variational Free Energy Pivot Selection for Pivoted Cholesky](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01821)
*Louise Schaub,Peter Zaspel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出Δ-VFE pivoted Cholesky方法，通过最大化变分自由能（VFE）的一步变化量作为选主元准则，在保持计算复杂度与随机Cholesky相当的同时，在低中秩下显著提升目标函数值和预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统pivoted Cholesky分解基于残差迹范数误差选择主元，但在许多应用中矩阵会进入非线性矩阵泛函（如高斯过程回归中的变分自由能），此时残差迹范数最小化并不直接等价于目标泛函的最优化，存在结构不匹配问题。

Method: 推导了在Cholesky一致的秩1更新下，最大化包含log-determinant、二次项和迹项的组合泛函的一步变化量的主元选择准则；该增益可分解为复杂度、数据拟合和迹贡献三部分。每次迭代从残差对角元按比例采样的s个候选主元中，通过增量Woodbury更新高效计算该准则，总复杂度为O(s n r²)。

Result: 数值实验表明，在低到中秩范围内，Δ-VFE pivoted Cholesky相比经典贪婪和随机pivoted Cholesky，在目标函数值和预测精度上均有显著提升，同时保持相当的迹范数近似质量。

Conclusion: 将矩阵分解的选主元准则与下游非线性目标函数对齐是可行的，Δ-VFE方法为面向特定目标（如高斯过程回归中的变分自由能）的低秩近似提供了理论与高效算法。

Abstract: Pivoted Cholesky factorizations construct low-rank approximations of symmetric positive definite matrices by sequentially selecting pivots from the residual diagonal. Classical greedy and randomized rules, such as randomly pivoted Cholesky, target the algebraic trace-norm error of the residual. In many applications, however, the matrix enters a nonlinear matrix functional whose value, not the trace-norm error, determines solution quality, and residual-based rules ignore this structure. We derive a pivot rule that maximizes the exact one-step change of such a functional under Cholesky-consistent rank-1 updates, for a functional combining log-determinant, quadratic, and trace terms. This functional arises as the variational free energy in Gaussian process regression, where the matrix is a kernel matrix. The resulting per-step gain admits a closed-form additive decomposition into complexity, data-fit, and trace contributions, and is used directly as a pivot-selection criterion. We refer to the resulting method as $Δ$-VFE pivoted Cholesky. At each iteration, the criterion is evaluated on a batch of $s$ candidate pivots sampled proportionally to the residual diagonal via incremental Woodbury updates, at a total cost of $\mathcal{O}(snr^2)$ for an $n\times n$ matrix and target rank $r$. This matches the asymptotic complexity of randomly pivoted Cholesky up to the batch factor $s$. Cholesky-consistent rank-1 updates yield monotonically non-decreasing functional values, and the proposed rule maximizes the per-step gain among them. Numerical experiments show improved objective values and predictive accuracy at low to moderate ranks compared to classical and randomly pivoted Cholesky, while preserving trace-norm approximation quality.

</details>


### [188] [Block Jacobi/Gauss-Seidel preconditioning for GLT sequences, and GLH sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01888)
*Carlo Garoni,Stefano Serra-Capizzano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了若{A_n}是符号为κ的GLT序列，且P_n是A_n的任意块Jacobi或块Gauss-Seidel预条件子（块数固定与n无关），则{P_n}也是符号为κ的GLT序列，并由此推广了Fasino-Tilli定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义局部Toeplitz（GLT）序列的块Jacobi和块Gauss-Seidel预条件子的谱性质，并利用该结果推广Hankel矩阵序列的零点分布定理。

Method: 理论证明GLT序列的预条件子仍为同符号GLT序列，结合GLT理论分析预条件子的效率，并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 块Jacobi/块Gauss-Seidel预条件子保持与原矩阵序列相同的GLT符号κ，预测了该类预条件对GLT序列的高效性；并将Fasino-Tilli定理从L^1函数生成的Hankel序列推广到更广泛的广义局部Hankel（GLH）序列。

Conclusion: 块Jacobi/Gauss-Seidel预条件子对于GLT序列具有显著效率，且该理论可推广至GLH序列的零点分布分析。

Abstract: The theory of generalized locally Toeplitz (GLT) sequences is an apparatus for computing the spectral and singular value distribution of sequences of matrices that possess a (possibly hidden) Toeplitz-like structure. These sequences, which are known as GLT sequences, arise in several applications, including the discretization of differential equations. Associated with any GLT sequence is a special function called symbol. In this paper, we prove that, if $\{A_n\}_n$ is a GLT sequence with symbol $κ$ and $P_n$ is any block Jacobi or block Gauss-Seidel preconditioner for $A_n$ with a fixed number of blocks independent of $n$, then $\{P_n\}_n$ is a GLT sequence with symbol $κ$, just like $\{A_n\}_n$. This result allows us to predict a remarkable efficiency of block Jacobi/Gauss-Seidel preconditioning for GLT sequences, which is in fact illustrated through numerical experiments. It also allows us to extend the Fasino-Tilli theorem on the zero distribution of Hankel matrix sequences generated by $L^1$ functions to a larger class of matrix sequences called generalized locally Hankel (GLH) sequences.

</details>


### [189] [$p$-Robust Trace Liftings for Discrete Harmonic Extensions and Boundary-Preserving $hp$ Interpolation on Tetrahedral Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02086)
*Situan Li,Weiying Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在三维四面体网格上构造了p-鲁棒的多项式迹提升算子，该算子保持次数、支撑在边界层内，且满足与网格尺寸和多项式次数无关的H^1和L^2估计。基于局部构造、四面体多项式提升、面对粘合和非奇异顶点补丁等技术，进一步获得了p-鲁棒的离散调和扩展和保持边界的hp插值算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三维四面体网格上实现p-鲁棒（即与多项式次数p无关）的多项式迹提升，以解决高精度有限元方法中边界条件的稳定处理问题。

Method: 采用局部构造策略，结合四面体多项式提升、面对粘合参数化以及非奇异顶点补丁技术，在边界层内构建次数保持的提升算子。

Result: 成功构造了满足H^1半范数和缩放边界层L^2范数一致估计（常数与网格尺寸h和多项式次数p无关）的多项式迹提升算子；并衍生出p-鲁棒的离散调和扩展和边界保持的hp插值算子。

Conclusion: 该构造为三维hp有限元方法提供了稳定的迹处理和插值工具，保证了高次数下数值方法的鲁棒性和最优收敛性。

Abstract: We construct p-robust polynomial trace liftings on three-dimensional tetrahedral meshes. The prescribed trace is a continuous piecewise polynomial function on a boundary face patch; the tetrahedra touching this patch have one common degree, while the interior degrees may be arbitrary. The lifting is degree-preserving, supported in the corresponding boundary layer, and satisfies both an H^1 estimate and a scaled boundary-layer L^2 estimate with constants independent of the mesh size and the polynomial degree.
  The construction is local and combines tetrahedral polynomial liftings, face-gluing arguments, and nonsingular vertex patches. As consequences of the construction, we obtain p-robust discrete harmonic extensions, including an H^1-seminorm-stable extension for the pure diffusion energy, and a boundary-preserving hp interpolation operator that keeps piecewise polynomial Dirichlet data exactly while retaining standard local approximation estimates.

</details>


### [190] [Trace-Preserving hp Interpolation and Polynomial Liftings on Conforming Hexahedral Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02125)
*Situan Li,Weiying Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文构建了非奇异及奇异共形六面体边界块上的p-稳健多项式提升算子，实现了H^1范数和半范数的稳定控制，并据此导出了迹保持的插值算子与离散调和拓展，在均匀边界度条件下常数p-均匀，非均匀情形仅有对数损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶有限元分析中，在共形六面体网格上需要将多项式扩展至相邻单元或边界块时，必须保持指定的边和面迹。现有方法对迹保持多项式提升的p-稳健性（即随多项式阶数增长而稳定）尚不充分，特别是在奇异边界块上需要构造稳定的提升算子。

Method: (1) 在非奇异共形六面体边界块上构造p-稳健多项式提升，建立H^1范数和半范数的稳定估计；(2) 利用该提升导出H^1半范数稳定的离散调和拓展；(3) 作为共形hp Clement插值子的边界修正，构造仅需H^1正则性的迹保持插值算子；(4) 在参考单元上开发迹保持插值和顶点支撑分解作为局部工具；(5) 将结果推广至含共形奇异边界块的网格，分析对数损失。

Result: 在均匀边界度条件下，常数是p-均匀的；在非均匀情形下仅有对数损失；在含奇异边界块时，损失仍保持为最大局部度数的多对数函数。

Conclusion: 所构造的多项式提升算子是p-稳健的，适用于非奇异和奇异共形六面体边界块，为高阶有限元分析中迹保持的插值和离散拓展提供了理论基础，且对函数仅需H^1正则性。

Abstract: Trace-compatible polynomial extensions are a recurring local ingredient in high-order finite element analysis on conforming hexahedral meshes. They are needed whenever prescribed edge and face traces must be preserved while a polynomial is extended into a neighboring cell or boundary patch. The main contribution of this paper is the construction of p-robust polynomial liftings on nonsingular conforming hexahedral boundary patches, with stable control of both the H^1 norm and the H^1-seminorm estimates needed for energy arguments. These liftings imply H^1-seminorm stable discrete harmonic extensions of polynomial Dirichlet traces. They also serve as boundary corrections for the conforming hp Clement interpolant, yielding trace-preserving interpolation operators for functions with only H^1 regularity. Under the uniform boundary-degree condition the constants are p-uniform; in the non-uniform case the stated logarithmic loss appears. We also treat meshes that may contain conforming singular boundary patches, where the loss remains polylogarithmic in the maximal local degree. Trace-preserving interpolation on reference cells and vertex-supported decompositions are developed as local tools for these patch and mesh-level constructions.

</details>


### [191] [Symmetric-Tensor Distributional Mixed Method for Fourth-Order Elliptic Singular Perturbation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02188)
*Xuehai Huang,Xinyue Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对四阶椭圆奇异摄动问题提出了一种对称张量分布混合方法，采用法向-法向连续对称张量元逼近矩变量，并结合H^1-非协调虚拟元空间与余维二子单纯形上的多项式乘子表示标量变量。推导了独立于边界层的最优参数一致误差估计。方法的混合化形式等价于无稳定弱Galerkin和H^2-非协调虚拟元方法。在二维情形下，建立了与经典Hellan-Herrmann-Johnson (HHJ)方法的紧密联系，将二维HHJ方法推广到任意空间维度。三维数值实验验证了理论收敛性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对四阶椭圆奇异摄动问题中边界层导致的参数依赖困难，以及现有方法在高维空间推广中的局限性，发展一种参数一致、维数无关的数值方法。

Method: 采用分布混合法框架：矩变量使用法向-法向连续对称张量元；标量变量使用H^1-非协调虚拟元空间，并在余维二子单纯形上引入多项式乘子。方法可混合化，等价于无稳定弱Galerkin和H^2-非协调虚拟元方法。

Result: 推导了与边界层无关的最优参数一致误差估计；在二维情形下揭示了该方法与经典HHJ方法的等价性，实现了向任意空间维度的推广；三维数值实验验证了收敛性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的对称张量分布混合方法有效解决了四阶椭圆奇异摄动问题的参数一致逼近难题，建立了与经典HHJ方法的联系并将其推广到高维，为相关问题的数值求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: A symmetric-tensor distributional mixed method for a fourth-order elliptic singular perturbation problem is developed in this paper. The moment variable is approximated by normal-normal continuous symmetric tensor elements, while the scalar variable is represented by an $H^1$-nonconforming virtual element space coupled with a polynomial multiplier on interior subsimplices of codimension two. Optimal parameter-uniform error estimates are derived, independent of the presence of boundary layers. A hybridized form of the method is also equivalent to stabilization-free weak Galerkin and $H^2$-nonconforming virtual element methods. In two dimensions, a close connection of the distributional mixed method to the classical Hellan-Herrmann-Johnson (HHJ) method is established, by naturally identifying the scalar virtual element-multiplier pair with the Lagrange finite element space. Thus the proposed method extends the two-dimensional HHJ method to arbitrary spatial dimensions. Three-dimensional numerical experiments support the theoretical convergence and robustness estimates.

</details>


### [192] [Spectral Audit of In-Context Operator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02427)
*Zhiwei Gao,Liu Yang,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于雅可比矩阵的谱审计方法，用于评估上下文算子学习中的局部动力学结构保真度，发现预测精度与局部算子保真度是神经算子的两个独立性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅依赖预测误差，但准确预测输出无法保证模型捕捉正确的局部动力学结构（如敏感性、频率响应、模式耦合、切向行为稳定性等）。

Method: 对固定提示，将网络输出对查询函数求导得到雅可比矩阵作为学习到的切向算子，再将其投影到傅里叶模态上，获得局部谱表征（频率增益、相位结构、跨模态耦合）。

Result: 审计揭示了相位传输、黏性依赖阻尼、非线性模式耦合、反应扩散稳定性结构等算子级现象；检测到预测误差指标部分隐藏的高频退化、不正确相位恢复、提示-算子不一致等失效模式。

Conclusion: 预测准确性与局部算子保真度是神经算子的不同性质，本文框架为稳定性、敏感性和算子一致性提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of neural operators and in-context operator learning rely primarily on prediction error, but accurate output prediction does not guarantee the correct local dynamical structure. A model may match solutions while exhibiting incorrect sensitivities, distorted frequency response, spurious mode coupling, or unstable tangent behavior. We introduce a Jacobian-based spectral audit for in-context operator learning. For a fixed prompt, we differentiate the network output with respect to the query function and view the resulting Jacobian as a learned tangent operator. Projecting it onto Fourier modes, we obtain a local spectral characterization of the inferred operator, including frequency-dependent gains, phase structure, and cross-mode coupling. The audit complements standard prediction metrics by testing whether the model reproduces local mechanisms of the underlying PDE operator rather than only outputs. Across benchmarks, the audit reveals distinct operator-level phenomena, including phase transport, viscosity-dependent damping, nonlinear mode coupling, and reaction--diffusion stability structure. It also detects failures partially hidden by prediction-error metrics, including high-frequency degradation, incorrect phase recovery, and prompt--operator inconsistencies. Corrupted or internally inconsistent prompts lead to degraded tangent-operator structure even when pointwise predictions remain partially accurate. Our results suggest that prediction accuracy and local operator fidelity are distinct properties of learned neural operators. Our framework also provides a diagnostic for stability, sensitivity, and operator consistency.

</details>


### [193] [Physics-Informed Residuals for Adaptive Mesh Refinement in Finite-Difference PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02475)
*Henry Kasumba,Ronald Katende*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出将PINN作为离网残差探针用于自适应网格细化（AMR），替代传统基于梯度的指示器，在有限差分求解前引导网格加密。一维Burgers方程验证表明，PINN阈值细化在60个自由度下达到0.021067的相对L²误差，优于均匀网格192个自由度的0.022617；2D/3D代理测试（非线性薛定谔方程、不可压Navier-Stokes）显示PINN残差可组织结构化细化，但未一致优于梯度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典有限差分求解器的效率高度依赖网格分辨率分布，均匀细化在解具有局部陡峭梯度、前沿、振荡或约束敏感区域时会浪费自由度。现有基于梯度的误差指示器在某些情况下不够鲁棒或缺乏物理信息，因此需要一种能利用物理信息（而非仅数值梯度）来指导网格自适应的策略。

Method: 混合策略：PINN不作为最终求解器，而是作为离网残差探针（off-grid residual probe）。具体流程为：在整个域上采样PINN残差，将其转换为基于单元的指示器，用以引导网格细化；最终逼近由经典有限差分求解器在自适应网格上完成。采用PINN阈值细化（PINN-threshold）和PINN-Dörfler细化两种策略。
