Result: 一维粘性Burgers方程：PINN阈值细化在60个自由度下相对L²误差0.021067，均匀网格192个自由度下误差0.022617；相同网格大小时误差降低约67.5%。PINN-Dörfler细化在58个自由度下误差0.021264。梯度指示器仍略优。2D非线性薛定谔方程和3D不可压Navier-Stokes代理测试：PINN残差可组织结构化细化并优于随机细化，但未一致优于梯度或均匀基线。

Conclusion: PINN引导的AMR可作为残差指示器策略，将基于物理信息的诊断信息传递到有限差分网格自适应中，同时保留经典求解器作为最终逼近引擎。该方法具有实用性，但并非普遍优于传统梯度指示器。

Abstract: Classical finite-difference solvers remain reliable tools for partial differential equations, but their efficiency depends on where mesh resolution is placed. Uniform refinement can waste degrees of freedom when solution difficulty is localised near sharp gradients, fronts, oscillations, or constraint-sensitive regions. This paper studies a hybrid strategy in which a physics-informed neural network (PINN) is used not as the final solver, but as an off-grid residual probe for adaptive mesh refinement. The PINN residual is sampled over the domain, converted into cellwise indicators, and used to guide refinement before the final approximation is computed by a finite-difference solver.
  The method is evaluated on three benchmarks. The main full-solver validation uses the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with a nonuniform finite-difference solve on the adapted meshes. PINN-threshold refinement attains final relative $L^2$ error $0.021067$ with $60$ degrees of freedom, compared with $0.022617$ for uniform refinement with $192$ degrees of freedom. At matched mesh size, PINN-threshold reduces the error by about $67.5\%$. PINN-D"orfler refinement gives similar performance, with error $0.021264$ using $58$ degrees of freedom. A gradient indicator remains slightly more accurate, so the result supports usefulness rather than universal superiority. Manufactured 2D and 3D proxy tests, based on a nonlinear Schr"odinger equation and an incompressible Navier--Stokes system, show that PINN residuals can organise structured refinement and improve over random refinement, although they do not consistently outperform gradient or uniform baselines. The results support PINN-guided AMR as a residual-indicator strategy for transferring physics-informed diagnostic information into finite-difference mesh adaptation while preserving the classical solver as the final approximation engine.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Kleshchev multipartitions, affine Mirković-Vilonen polytopes, and representations of KLR algebras in type ${\tt A}^{(1)}_1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00421)
*Samantha Allen,Jack Isaac,Corinne Moscariello,Robert Muth,Bella Deborah Uwase,Lucas Walton*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在类型 A₁⁽¹⁾ 中构造了 B(∞) 晶体的三个模型（仿射MV多面体、Kleshchev多分割和上层边缘图）之间的显式同构，并给出了相关晶体的直接构造方法与非迭代识别定理，应用于KLR代数表示论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立仿射A₁⁽¹⁾型B(∞)晶体不同模型之间的显式对应关系，填补这些模型间的空白，并为KLR代数提供组合词典。

Method: 通过构造显式同构连接三个模型；利用边界根划分直接完成仿射MV多面体；提出非迭代的识别定理刻画Kleshchev多分割；最后将结果应用于KLR代数的尖点与细胞理论框架。

Result: 建立了三种模型之间的显式同构，给出了仿射MV多面体的直接构造方法和Kleshchev多分割的非迭代识别定理，得到了KLR代数中诱导与限制函子的扩充分支规则。

Conclusion: 这些结果为KLR代数的尖点理论与细胞理论框架提供了组合词典，并给出了实根函子的扩充分支规则。

Abstract: We construct explicit isomorphisms between three models for the $B(\infty)$ crystal in type ${\tt A}_1^{(1)}$: affine Mirković--Vilonen polytopes, Kleshchev multipartitions, and a new model we call upper ledge diagrams. We also present some clarifying results on these crystals, giving a direct method for completing an affine MV polytope from the data of one of its boundary root partitions, and a non-iterative recognition theorem which characterizes Kleshchev multipartitions in type ${\tt A}_1^{(1)}$. We apply these results to the representation theory of KLR algebras, where they yield a combinatorial dictionary between cuspidal- and cellular-theoretic frameworks, along with some augmented branching rules for real root functors of induction and restriction.

</details>


### [195] [AHA! RSK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00679)
*Eugene Stern*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过对称群$S_n$和退化仿射Hecke代数$H_n$的表示论，给出了Robinson-Schensted-Knuth（RSK）对应的谱实现。将排列视为阶梯形斜表， rectification（Jeu de Taquin）过程将斜表转化为标准杨表对，该过程通过算子作用在正则表示上建模，最终建立了$H_n$权向量与$S_n$权向量之间的基变换，其标签对应关系恰好是RSK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为RSK对应提供一种表示论层面的谱实现，将组合对象（排列、标准杨表）与代数结构（对称群、退化仿射Hecke代数的表示）建立深层联系。

Method: 将排列视为阶梯形斜表，利用rectification（Jeu de Taquin）转化为标准杨表对；通过将$S_n$嵌入更大对称群并右推至无穷，使Jucys-Murphy元素能表示$H_n$的外部平移；将rectification过程中的滑动建模为作用于正则表示上的算子序列，显式写出$H_n$权向量与$S_n$权向量之间的基变换矩阵。

Result: 建立了$H_n$权向量（由排列标记）与$S_n$权向量（由左右Jucys-Murphy元素共同对角化，由标准杨表对标记）之间的明确对应，该对应恰好是RSK对应。

Conclusion: RSK对应可以在退化仿射Hecke代数和对称群的表示论框架下获得谱实现，为这一经典组合对应提供了代数和几何解释。

Abstract: We give a spectral realization of the Robinson-Schensted-Knuth (RSK) correspondence in terms of the representation theory of the symmetric group $S_n$ and the degenerate affine Hecke algebra (AHA) $H_n$. We view RSK, which builds a pair of standard Young tableaux from a permutation, as a special case of rectification, also known as Jeu de Taquin, which turns skew tableaux into straight ones. In this framing, the initial permutation corresponds to a skew tableau of staircase shape. To interpret this in terms of representation theory, take permutations to label weight vectors in a generic $H_n$-module $V(a_1, \ldots , a_n)$, which is isomorphic to $\mathbb{C}[S_n]$ as an $S_n$-module. Writing permutations as staircases amounts to placing these weight vectors inside the regular representation of a larger symmetric group containing $S_n$; more geometrically, we push $S_n$ to the right toward infinity so its Jucys-Murphy (JM) elements have enough room to represent the external translations of $H_n$. Then, rectification corresponds to squeezing out this extra room from the left, leaving only $S_n$ and its regular JM elements as the limit of the external translations. By expressing slides via sequences of exchanges of consecutive values inside the tableau, we can model rectification by an operator acting on the regular representation. This lets us explicitly write down the change of basis between $H_n$-weight vectors and $S_n$-weight vectors, where the latter are eigenvectors of the JM elements in $S_n$ acting both on the left and on the right, and hence labeled by pairs of standard tableaux. The resulting correspondence between the labels of the weight vectors is exactly RSK.

</details>


### [196] [Classifying subcategories of a Grothendieck category via its spectral category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00908)
*Reza Sazeedeh*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Krause定义的支撑概念对Grothendieck范畴中子范畴进行分类的方法，证明了该支撑与Neeman支撑一致，从而推广了交换诺特环上模范畴及其导出范畴的子范畴分类，并在局部凝聚范畴上证明了该支撑与Ziegler拓扑的开子集一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将模范畴及其导出范畴的子范畴分类结果推广到一般的Grothendieck范畴中，建立一个统一的分类框架。

Method: 利用Krause基于谱范畴定义的支撑概念，建立其与Neeman支撑的等价性，并在局部凝聚范畴情形下与Ziegler拓扑建立联系。

Result: 证明了该支撑分类与Neeman支撑分类一致，且在局部凝聚范畴中与Ziegler拓扑的开子集相对应。

Conclusion: 成功将交换诺特环上模范畴及其导出范畴的子范畴分类推广到Grothendieck范畴，并揭示了与Ziegler拓扑的内在联系。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{A}$ be a Grothendieck category. In this paper we classify subcategories of $\mathcal{A}$ via a support notion defined by Krause \cite{Kr2024} based on the spectral category of $\mathcal{A}$. We show that this aligns with Nemman's support \cite{Ne1992}, and thereby extends earlier classifications of subcategories of the category of modules over a commutative noetherian ring and subcategories of its derived category. Over a locally coherent category $\mathcal{A}$, we show that this support agrees with open subsets of the Ziegler topology on $\mathcal{A}$.

</details>


### [197] [Weyl algebras on Braverman-Kazhdan spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01206)
*Chun-Hsien Hsu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了特征零域上分裂单连通几乎简单代数群G的极大抛物子群P的派生群对G的齐性空间上的微分算子环，证明其与经典Weyl代数共享若干关键结构性质，并发展了相应的D-模理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解齐性空间上微分算子环的结构，特别是推广经典Weyl代数的性质到更一般的代数群背景中。

Method: 利用代数群和抛物子群的结构理论，分析P^der\G上的微分算子环，建立与Weyl代数的类比，并构造D-模理论。

Result: 证明了该微分算子环具有与经典Weyl代数类似的关键结构性质，并建立了相应的D-模理论框架。

Conclusion: 极大抛物子群的派生群对G的齐性空间上的微分算子环可以视为Weyl代数的推广，具有类似的结构性质，且存在相应的D-模理论。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a split, simply connected, almost simple algebraic group over a field of characteristic zero, and let $P$ be a maximal parabolic subgroup of $G$. We study the ring of differential operators on $P^{\mathrm{der}}\backslash G$, showing that it shares several key structural properties with classical Weyl algebras. We also develop a corresponding theory of $D$-modules.

</details>


### [198] [Wide Regular Subalgebras of Symmetrizable Kac-Moody Algebras and an Extension of Schur's Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01371)
*Andrew Douglas,Abid Ali*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究对称化Kac-Moody代数中的宽正则子代数，将Douglas-Repka理论从半单Lie代数推广到更广情形。主要结果是Schur引理的推广：若实根闭包条件成立，则不可约可积最高权模的限制分解是平凡的；在仿射情形下建立了宽子代数的充要条件。关键工具是仿射根系闭子集在有限根和下的封闭性结构定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lie理论中，表示在限制下的行为是核心课题。宽子代数（限制后不可约可积最高权模仍保持不可分解）的概念源于Douglas-Repka对半单Lie代数的研究。本文将其推广至对称化Kac-Moody代数，旨在建立宽子代数的判别准则并理解其结构。

Method: 通过正则子代数与根空间分解的关联，定义闭子集T。利用实根闭包条件和Schur引理的推广，证明宽子代数的充分条件。在仿射情形下，借助根系闭子集在有限根和下封闭的结构定理，建立宽子代数的等价刻画。

Result: 1) 推广Schur引理：若实根闭包条件成立，则End模的定算子代数退化到复数域。2) 该条件为宽子代数的充分条件。3) 仿射情形下，宽子代数等价于T∪(-T)的闭包覆盖整个根系。4) 关键结构定理：仿射根系闭子集在任意有限根和（仍为根）下封闭。

Conclusion: 将宽子代数理论从半单Lie代数推广到对称化Kac-Moody代数，在仿射情形下给出完整的充要刻画，揭示了宽子代数的根闭包本质。

Abstract: The behavior of representations under restriction is a central theme in Lie theory. We study wide regular subalgebras of symmetrizable Kac-Moody algebras, extending work of Douglas and Repka on semisimple Lie algebras. A subalgebra is wide if every irreducible integrable highest weight module remains indecomposable upon restriction. Let $\mathfrak{g}$ be a symmetrizable Kac-Moody algebra with Cartan subalgebra $\mathfrak{h}$, root system $Φ$, simple roots $Π$, and root space decomposition $\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{h}\oplus\bigoplus_{α\inΦ}\mathfrak{g}_α$. Denote by $Φ_{\operatorname{re}}$ the set of real roots. To a regular subalgebra $\mathfrak{s}$ normalized by $\mathfrak{h}$, we associate a closed subset $T\subseteq Φ$ by declaring $α\in T$ if $\mathfrak{s}\cap \mathfrak{g}_α\ne \{0\}$. Our main result is an extension of Schur's lemma: if $\mathfrak{h}\subseteq \mathfrak{s}$ and the real closure of $(T\cup(-T))\cap Φ_{\operatorname{re}}$ contains $Π$, then $(\operatorname{End} V)^{\mathfrak{s}}=\mathbb{C}\operatorname{Id}_V$ for every irreducible integrable highest weight module $V$. As a consequence, this real-root closure condition yields a sufficient condition for wideness. In the affine case, we establish a converse: if $\mathfrak{s}$ is wide, then the closure of $T\cup(-T)$ in $Φ$ is all of $Φ$, and this implication holds without assuming that $\mathfrak{h}\subseteq \mathfrak{s}$. A key ingredient is a structural result showing that closed subsets of affine root systems are closed under arbitrary finite root sums that remain roots.

</details>


### [199] [Perverse filtration on the cohomology of affine Springer fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01507)
*Zhiwei Yun*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明仿射Springer纤维的顶部上同调（作为Weyl群表示）包含某些Springer纤维总上同调的主要部分，关键工具是在仿射Springer纤维的纯上同调部分上构造“perverse filtration”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示仿射Springer纤维与Springer纤维上同调结构之间的深层联系，理解Weyl群表示在这些几何对象上的分布规律。

Method: 在仿射Springer纤维的纯上同调部分上构造一个“perverse filtration”（反常滤过），利用该滤过来分析上同调结构并建立与Springer纤维上同调的关联。

Result: 证明了仿射Springer纤维的顶部上同调（作为Weyl群表示）包含了某些Springer纤维总上同调的主要部分。

Conclusion: 仿射Springer纤维的上同调结构可以通过perverse filtration有效地分解，并揭示其与Springer纤维上同调之间的表示论对应关系。

Abstract: We show that the top cohomology of any affine Springer fiber, as a Weyl group representation, contains a large part of the total cohomology of certain Springer fibers. The main ingredient of the proof is the construction of a ``perverse filtration'' on the pure part of the cohomology of affine Springer fibers.

</details>


### [200] [The Unitarity of Arthur Packets for Real Reductive Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01609)
*Jeffrey Adams,Andrei Ionov,Lucas Mason-Brown,David Vogan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Arthur关于实约化群Arthur包均由酉表示构成的猜想。核心创新是Arthur包的"Jordan分解"——通过实抛物和上同调归纳将任意Arthur包约化为特定Levi子群的幂幺Arthur包，从而将酉性问题归约到已知的幂幺情形。同时证明了实约化群的Jiang猜想，给出了Arthur包成员波前集的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arthur在1980年代猜想存在满足若干非凡性质的不可约容许表示包（Arthur包），并进一步猜想所有Arthur包均由酉表示构成。该长期未解决的猜想是本文要证明的核心目标。

Method: 提出Arthur包的"Jordan分解"——一个规范的两步过程：通过实抛物和上同调归纳，将任意Arthur包从某一Levi子群的幂幺Arthur包构造出来。该分解类似于复代数群中元素分解为椭圆、双曲和幂幺部分的唯一交换积。

Result: 完整证明了Arthur猜想（所有Arthur包由酉表示构成），并将酉性问题归结为幂幺Arthur包情形（已有Adams-Arancibia-Mezo等人工作覆盖）。同时证明了实约化群的Jiang猜想。

Conclusion: Arthur包酉性猜想获证，Jiang猜想（关于波前集上界，由Barbasch-Vogan对偶给出）在实约化群情形也成立。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected reductive algebraic group defined over $\mathbb{R}$. In the 1980s, Arthur conjectured the existence of certain packets of irreducible admissible representations of $G(\mathbb{R})$ satisfying various remarkable properties. These packets were given a precise definition in the book of Adams, Barbasch, and Vogan in terms of microlocal geometry on a space of Langlands parameters. A longstanding conjecture, originally due to Arthur, is that all Arthur packets consist of $\textit{unitary}$ representations. In this paper, we prove this conjecture in general. The main new idea is a `Jordan decomposition' for Arthur packets: a canonical two-step process for realizing an arbitrary Arthur packet via real parabolic and cohomological induction from a unipotent Arthur packet for a certain Levi subgroup. This process is analogous to the decomposition of an element of a complex algebraic group as a (unique) commuting product of elliptic, hyperbolic, and unipotent parts. Using our Jordan decomposition, we reduce the question of unitarity to the case of unipotent Arthur packets, where the answer is already known (by work of Adams-Arancibia-Mezo, Adams-van Leeuwen-Miller-Vogan, Arthur, Barbasch, Barbasch-Ma-Sun-Zhu, and Davis-Mason-Brown). As an application of the same methods, we also give a proof of Jiang's conjecture for real reductive groups, which gives an upper bound on the wavefront sets of the members of an Arthur packet in terms of the Barbasch-Vogan dual of the Arthur $SL_2(\mathbb{C})$.

</details>


### [201] [Presentations for categories of crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02249)
*David He,Daniel Tubbenhauer*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了由单复李代数的基本晶体生成的晶体幺半范畴的生成元与关系，并列举了几个小秩例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体范畴是表示论中的重要工具，但对其生成元与关系的系统描述尚不完善，尤其对于基本晶体生成的幺半范畴缺乏明确的代数刻画。

Method: 使用组合表示论和晶体基理论，通过分析基本晶体的结构及它们之间的相互作用，提取出范畴层面的生成元与关系式，并在小秩情形下进行显式验证。

Result: 得到了基本晶体生成的幺半范畴的一组生成元和关系，并具体给出了若干小秩李代数的例子。

Conclusion: 该结果为理解晶体范畴的代数结构提供了统一的框架，有助于后续研究晶体范畴的表示和分类问题。

Abstract: We give generators and relations for the monoidal categories of crystals generated by the fundamental crystals of a simple complex Lie algebra. We also spell out several small-rank examples.

</details>


### [202] [On rational representations and rational group algebra of $\operatorname{GL}_2(q)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02415)
*Ram Karan Choudhary,Sunil Kumar Prajapati*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了GL₂(q)在有理数域上的不可约表示，给出了特征标公式、不等价表示计数、表示构造方法，并推导了有理群代数Q[G]的Wedderburn分解的显式组合公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为GL₂(q)在有理数域上的表示提供系统性的组合描述，包括计数、构造以及群代数的Wedderburn分解。

Method: 利用Schur指标与Galois群作用描述有理特征标结构，通过抛物诱导构造有理矩阵表示，结合组合方法推导计数与分解公式。

Result: 给出了不等价不可约有理解表示按度数的组合计数，提出了构造由抛物诱导得到的不可约复特征标对应的有理表示的方法，并得到了仅依赖于q的Wedderburn分解显式组合公式。

Conclusion: 为GL₂(q)的有理表示理论提供了完整的组合刻画，并给出了有理群代数的结构分解。

Abstract: In this article, we study rational representations of $G=\operatorname{GL}_2(q)$, where $q$ is a prime power. Let $ρ$ be an irreducible representation of $G$ over $\mathbb{Q}$. Then $ρ$ affords the character \[ Ω(χ)=m_{\mathbb{Q}}(χ)\sum_{σ\in\operatorname{Gal}(\mathbb{Q}(χ)/\mathbb{Q})}χ^σ, \] for some irreducible complex character $χ$ of $G$, where $m_{\mathbb{Q}}(χ)$ denotes the Schur index of $χ$ over $\mathbb{Q}$, with the converse also holding. We obtain a combinatorial description for the counting of inequivalent irreducible $\mathbb{Q}$-representations of $G$ of distinct degrees. Furthermore, we present a method to construct an irreducible rational matrix representation $ρ$ of $G$ affording the character $Ω(χ)$, where $χ$ is an irreducible complex character of $G$ arising from parabolic induction. Finally, using the results from the rational representations of $G$, we derive an explicit combinatorial formula, depending only on $q$, for the Wedderburn decomposition of $\mathbb{Q}G$.

</details>


### [203] [Folding shuffle algebras and twisted $q$-characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02471)
*Andrei Neguţ,Keyu Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过引入折叠混洗代数的新概念，证明了Hernandez关于量子非扭仿射代数模的某些q-字符与其扭对应物相等的猜想，并将该结果推广到带自同构的任意箭图情形，特别地定义并描述了扭量子环面代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Hernandez关于量子非扭仿射代数模的q-字符与其扭对应物相等的猜想，并将该结果推广到更一般的带自同构的箭图设定中。

Method: 引入了折叠混洗代数（folding shuffle algebras）的新概念，以此作为核心工具来建立量子非扭仿射代数与其扭版本之间的q-字符等式。

Result: 成功证明了Hernandez猜想；将结果推广到任意带自同构的箭图；定义并描述了扭量子环面代数（twisted quantum toroidal algebras）。

Conclusion: 折叠混洗代数是连接扭与非扭代数结构的有力工具，为理解量子代数模的表示论提供了新的视角和推广框架。

Abstract: Using our new notion of folding shuffle algebras, we prove a conjecture of Hernandez on the equality between certain $q$-characters of quantum untwisted affine algebra modules and their twisted counterparts. We generalize this result to the setting of arbitrary quivers with automorphisms, in particular by defining and describing twisted quantum toroidal algebras.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Perfect powers in the product of denominators of elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00466)
*Subham Bhakta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文使用筛选论证来估计椭圆曲线上有理点s-元组中分母乘积为素数ℓ次幂的频率，分别考虑带平移的标量乘点和有界规范高度点两种情形，并为无穷远点特殊情况推导了更优估计，同时给出了有理函数的类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究椭圆曲线上有理点的分母乘积为完美ℓ次幂的分布规律，这是数论中经典问题的椭圆曲线类比，旨在推广R. de la Bretèche等人（2021）关于有理函数的结果。

Method: 采用筛选论证（sieving arguments）进行估计，在特殊情形下使用带椭圆整除序列的椭圆筛法（elliptic sieve with elliptic divisibility sequences）获得更优估计。

Result: 得到了两种设置下（形如n_iP_i+Q_i的点以及有界规范高度的一般点）有理点s-元组中分母乘积为ℓ次幂的频率估计，并在所有Q_i为无穷远点时获得了改进的估计。

Conclusion: 给出了椭圆曲线上有理点分母乘积为完美幂问题的定量估计，为相关有理函数结果提供了椭圆类比。

Abstract: We use sieving arguments to estimate the frequency of $s$-tuples of rational points $$(P_1,\dots,P_s)\in E_1(\mathbb{Q})\times\cdots\times E_s(\mathbb{Q}),$$ where $E_1,\dots,E_s$ are (not necessarily distinct) elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}$, for which the product of their denominators is a perfect $\ell$th power for a fixed prime $\ell$. We consider two settings: one in which the points are of the form $n_iP_i+Q_i$ with $n_i$ ranging over an interval, and another in which we take arbitrary points of bounded canonical height. In the special case where all $Q_i$ are the points at infinity, we also obtain better estimates by using a version of the elliptic sieve with elliptic divisibility sequences. Consequently, we derive analogues of these results for various rational functions, providing elliptic analogs of R. de la Bretèche, P. Kurlberg and I. E. Shparlinski (2021).

</details>


### [205] [On $\mathbb{Z}_2$-extensions of real quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00482)
*Sosuke Sasaki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了实二次域k的Z_2扩张各层的类数2-部分指数序列(a_0,a_1,...)的规律，通过迭代扩展分圆单位群推导出相关定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解实二次域Z_2扩张中类数2-部分的变化规律是代数数论的重要问题，但目前对该序列的系统性规律认识不足。

Method: 采用迭代扩展分圆单位群的方法，分析实二次域k的Z_2扩张各层k_n的类数2-部分指数a_n。

Result: 推导出关于序列(a_0,a_1,...)的多条定律，揭示了类数2-部分在Z_2扩张各层中的变化模式。

Conclusion: 通过分圆单位群的迭代扩展，成功获得了实二次域Z_2扩张中类数2-部分指数序列的系统性规律。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a real quadratic field. Let $k_n$ be the $n$-th layer of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-extension of $k$, and $2^{a_n}$ be the $2$-part of the class number of $k_n$. In this paper, we derive many laws concerning the sequence $(a_0,a_1,\dotsc)$, by iteratively extending the group of cyclotomic units.

</details>


### [206] [Prescribed realisation of longest runs in continued fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00505)
*Ying Wai Lee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了连分数展开中固定符号的最长游程与全局最长游程之间的相互作用，证明了对于每个指定的部分商值和每个可接受的增长尺度，存在一个具有完全Hausdorff维数的无理数集合，使得指定值的最长游程具有指定的渐近增长，并且对于每个初始长度，唯一地实现了全局最大值。由此可知，负责全局最长游程的符号可以预先固定而不损失Hausdorff维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化已知的固定符号最长游程增长和全局最长游程增长的全维例外集结果，同时证明全局问题中的最大化符号是完全可预先指定的。

Method: 通过构造性方法，对每个指定的部分商值和可接受的增长尺度，构建具有完全Hausdorff维数的无理数集合，使得固定符号的最长游程恰好具有指定的渐近增长，并且唯一地成为全局最长游程。

Result: 成功构造了所需的无理数集合，证明了负责全局最长游程的符号可以预先固定而不损失Hausdorff维数。

Conclusion: 固定符号最长游程增长和全局最长游程增长的全维例外集结果得到同时强化，全局问题中的最大化符号是完全可预先指定的。

Abstract: Exceptional longest-run behaviour in continued fraction expansions is studied through the interaction between fixed-symbol runs and the overall longest run. For every prescribed partial quotient value and every admissible growth scale, a full Hausdorff dimensional set of irrational numbers is constructed on which the longest run of the prescribed value has exactly the prescribed asymptotic growth and, for every initial length, uniquely realises the overall maximum. It follows that the symbol responsible for the overall longest run can be fixed in advance without any loss of Hausdorff dimension. Thus, the known full-dimensional exceptional-set results for fixed-symbol longest-run growth and for overall longest-run growth are simultaneously strengthened, while the maximising symbol in the overall problem is shown to be fully prescribable.

</details>


### [207] [Polylogarithmic Analogues of Euler's Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00525)
*Takumi Noda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入了一族常数 \(C_m\)，作为欧拉常数的多对数类似物，研究了它们的基本性质、迭代对数积分表示、关联的多对数zeta势与多对数gamma函数，并建立了与黎曼zeta函数Dirichlet级数特殊值的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典欧拉常数 \(\gamma = \lim_{n\to\infty} (\sum_{k=1}^n 1/k - \log n)\) 是数论与解析数学中的基本常数。本文旨在将调和级数与对数之差这一结构推广至多对数函数 \(\operatorname{Li}_m(1/k)\)，引入新的常数族 \(C_m\)，并系统研究其分析性质与数论联系。

Method: 定义常数族 \(C_m = \lim_{n\to\infty} ( \sum_{k=1}^n \operatorname{Li}_m(1/k) - \log n )\)；利用迭代对数积分结构与gamma函数建立其表示；引入多对数zeta势与多对数gamma函数；建立微分关系与积分表示；分析对数分支在奇点附近的渐近行为；通过Dirichlet级数与黎曼zeta函数建立联系。

Result: 获得了常数 \(C_m\) 的基本性质与迭代对数积分表示；建立了多对数zeta势与多对数gamma函数的微分关系与积分表示；描述了奇点附近的对数分支渐近行为；将 \(C_m\) 关联到某些涉及黎曼zeta函数的Dirichlet级数的特殊值。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了欧拉常数的多对数推广族 \(C_m\)，为其建立了完整的分析理论框架（积分表示、微分关系、渐近行为），并揭示了其与黎曼zeta函数及Dirichlet级数的深刻联系，为进一步数论与解析应用奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce a family of constants \[ C_m := \lim_{n\to\infty} \left( \sum_{k=1}^n \operatorname{Li}_m\!\left(\frac1k\right) -
\log n \right), \] which may be regarded as polylogarithmic analogues of Euler's constant. We study their basic properties and derive representations in terms of iterated logarithmic integral structures associated with the gamma function. We further introduce associated polylogarithmic zeta potentials and polylogarithmic gamma functions, establish differential relations and integral representations, and describe logarithmic branch asymptotics near the singular points. As an application, we relate the constants \(C_m\) to special values of certain Dirichlet series involving the Riemann zeta function.

</details>


### [208] [On the $P(t)$-adic Littlewood conjecture in odd characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00633)
*Li Lai,Johannes Sprang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意奇特征基域上，对于任何不可约多项式 $P(t)$，$P(t)$-adic Littlewood猜想不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $P(t)$-adic Littlewood猜想是数论中著名的p-adic Littlewood猜想在函数域上的类比，其真实性一直是待解决的问题。本文旨在探究该猜想在奇特征基域上是否成立。

Method: 通过构造反例或利用函数域特有的代数几何性质，在任意奇特征基域上对任意不可约多项式$P(t)$证明该猜想不成立。

Result: 成功证明$P(t)$-adic Littlewood猜想在奇特征基域上对于任何不可约多项式$P(t)$均不成立。

Conclusion: 该猜想在奇特征函数域情形下被证伪，这有助于更深入理解丢番图逼近在不同数域中的行为差异。

Abstract: The $P(t)$-adic Littlewood conjecture is a function field analogue of the famous $p$-adic Littlewood conjecture in Diophantine approximation. In this paper, we prove that the $P(t)$-adic Littlewood conjecture fails for any irreducible polynomial $P(t)$ over any ground field of odd characteristic.

</details>


### [209] [Locating a shortest vector in certain $2$-dimensional lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00705)
*Guixian Zou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了一个二维格 Λ(a,m) 中最短向量的位置，其中 a 和 m 为正整数且互质，证实了 Wu 和 Xu 的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了证实 Han Wu 和 Guangwu Xu 关于特定二维模格中最短向量位置的猜想。

Method: 通过数学分析和格论工具，研究了二维格 Λ(a,m) 的结构，并确定了其中最短向量的具体位置。

Result: 成功找到了该二维格中最短向量的位置，证实了先前提出的猜想。

Conclusion: 该研究完整刻画了二维模格 Λ(a,m) 的最短向量，对理解此类格的结构有重要意义。

Abstract: Let $a$, $m$ be positive integers, $1<a<m$, $\gcd(a,m)=1$. We determine the location of a shortest vector in the $2$-dimensional lattices $$ Λ(a,m) = \{(x, y)\in\mathbb{Z}\times\mathbb{Z}\mid ax + y\equiv 0~(\bmod\,m)\}. $$ This confirms a conjecture of Han Wu and Guangwu Xu.

</details>


### [210] [On indecomposable elements in lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00868)
*Lenny Fukshansky,Filiana Kostopoulou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧几里得格中不可分解元素的分布，证明了二维格中每个正元素可表示为至多两个不可分解元素的正整数线性组合，并给出了实二次域分式理想对应格中不可分解元素数量的对数增长界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解格中不可分解元素（即希尔伯特基）的分布与计数特性，探究哪些格仅有有限多个不可分解元素，哪些具有无限多个。

Method: 采用分类讨论方法，区分有限/无限不可分解元素的格；在二维情形证明离散Carathéodory性质变体；对实二次域分式理想格进行显式计数估计。

Result: 二维格中每个正元素可表示为至多两个不可分解元素的正整数线性组合；实二次域分式理想对应的格中，有界范数不可分解元素数量呈对数增长。

Conclusion: 建立了格中不可分解元素的基本分类结果，揭示了二维情形下的结构性质，并为特定数论格提供了精确的渐近计数。

Abstract: We study the distribution of indecomposable elements in Euclidean lattices. A positive element in a lattice is called indecomposable if it cannot be represented as a sum of two other positive nonzero elements. The set of all indecomposables in a lattice forms the Hilbert basis for the positive lattice semigroup. We classify lattices that contain only finitely many indecomposables versus those that contain infinitely many. In the two-dimensional case, we prove that every positive element in a lattice can be represented as a positive integer linear combination of at most two indecomposables, which is a certain variation of the discrete Carathéodory's property. In the case of lattices coming from fractional ideals in real quadratic number fields, we obtain an explicit counting estimate for the number of indecomposables with bounded norm, showing logarithmic growth.

</details>


### [211] [The categorical local Langlands conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00983)
*David Hansen,Lucas Mann*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个证明Fargues-Scholze提出的范畴化局部朗兰兹猜想（CLLC）的方案，该方案适用于所有拟分裂p进群，前提是Fargues-Scholze L-参数与已知的“自守”局部朗兰兹参数化的半单化一致。关键工作假设是增强Whittaker系数函子c_ψ与Eisenstein级数的兼容性。对于GL_n，该假设可推出完整的CLLC；对于更一般的群G，本文证明了归纳原理，将CLLC归约为所有真Levi子群的CLLC加上关于G的极少信息。文中还建立了许多基础性结果，包括谱常项函子的强有限性定理、p进Hecke代数的Bernstein有限全局维数定理的谱类比、可容许ind-凝聚层与可容许对偶理论，以及谱作用的对偶定理，并在此基础上无条件地定义了一个从谱侧到自守侧的显式新函子t_ψ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Fargues-Scholze提出的范畴化局部朗兰兹猜想（CLLC），该猜想是局部朗兰兹纲领的范畴化推广，旨在建立p进群的表示范畴与Langlands参数范畴之间的深层联系。

Method: 提出基于假设（c_ψ与Eisenstein级数兼容性）的证明方案；对GL_n直接证明假设蕴含CLLC；对一般群G建立归纳原理，归约到真Levi子群；建立谱常项函子有限性定理、谱Bernstein定理、可容许ind-凝聚层与对偶理论等基础工具，并定义显式函子t_ψ。

Result: 对于GL_n，在单一假设下完整证明了CLLC；对于一般群G，证明了归纳原理，可无条件应用于许多经典群；建立了多个基础性结果，并定义了从谱侧到自守侧的新函子t_ψ，该函子定义在足够多的ind-凝聚层上以控制整个猜想。

Conclusion: 本文为CLLC提供了系统的证明方案与归纳框架，通过建立一系列基础理论并定义关键函子，将CLLC的证明归结为可验证的假设与归纳步骤，为最终解决该猜想奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We formulate a program to prove the categorical local Langlands conjecture (CLLC) of Fargues-Scholze, for all quasisplit $p$-adic groups where the Fargues-Scholze $L$-parameters agree with the semisimplification of a known "automorphic" local Langlands parametrization. A key working hypothesis - which we expect to prove elsewhere jointly with Hamann - is the compatibility of the enhanced Whittaker coefficient functor $c_ψ$ with Eisenstein series. For $\mathrm{GL}_n$, we show that this hypothesis alone implies the full CLLC. For more general groups $G$, we prove an induction principle which reduces CLLC for $G$ to CLLC for all proper Levi subgroups together with a very small amount of information about $G$. This principle applies unconditionally to many classical groups with current technology.
  Along the way, we establish many foundational results. In particular:
  - We prove a very strong finiteness theorem for spectral constant term functors.
  - We prove a spectral analogue of Bernstein's finite global dimension theorem for $p$-adic Hecke algebras.
  - We introduce and develop the theory of admissible ind-coherent sheaves and admissible duality on derived stacks.
  - We prove a duality theorem for the spectral action.
  Using all of these results, we unconditionally define a new and explicit functor $t_ψ$ from the spectral side to the automorphic side, which is defined on enough ind-coherent sheaves to control the entire conjecture.

</details>


### [212] [A further study of polynomial $g_{n,q}$ over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01037)
*Neranga Fernando,Bhitali Kousik*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上多项式 $g_{n,q}$ 的置换性质，特别是在偶特征有限域上的置换行为，引入了其多元情形，并给出了若干新恒等式和开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找有限域上新的置换多项式类别，这是密码学和编码理论中的重要研究方向。

Method: 通过分析多项式 $g_{n,q}$ 在偶特征有限域上的置换行为，将其推广到多元情形，研究多元置换多项式和局部置换多项式，并推导新的恒等式。

Result: 给出了 $g_{n,q}(X)$ 在偶特征有限域上的置换性质分析结果，建立了多元情形 $g_{n,q}(X_1,\ldots,X_k)$ 的置换多项式与局部置换多项式性质，并发现了若干新恒等式。

Conclusion: 本文推进了对 $g_{n,q}$ 类置换多项式的理解，并提出了若干有待解决的开放问题。

Abstract: Let $n\geq 0$ be an integer and $q$ a prime power. The polynomial $g_{n,q}$ was introduced in [10] with the purpose of finding new classes of permutation polynomials over finite fields. We investigate the permutation behaviour of the polynomial $g_{n,q}(X)$ over finite fields of even characteristic. We introduce the multivariate case of the polynomial $g_{n,q}$, and study the permutation polynomials in several variables and local permutation polynomials resulting from the polynomials $g_{n,q}(X_1,X_2,\ldots , X_k)$. We also present several new identities of $g_{n,q}(X)$, and present some open questions on the permutation property of $g_{n,q}(X)$.

</details>


### [213] [Topological and Diophantine properties of lattice subset projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01040)
*Wayne M Lawton*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究格点子集L的k-稠密性与其在Grassmannian上投影极限点集L_{n,lim}之间的关系，利用Baire范畴定理和Khintchine-Groshev定理刻画了L_{n,lim}的拓扑和度量性质，并构造了不同测度大小的例子，最后提出与多维晶体测度和Fourier拟晶构造相关的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立格点子集L的k-稠密性（几何填充性质）与Grassmannian上投影极限点集L_{n,lim}（丢番图逼近性质）之间的严格联系，并探索其测度论和拓扑学特征。

Method: 利用Baire范畴定理证明L为k-稠密当且仅当L_{n,lim}为G_δ集；利用Khintchine-Groshev定理将L_{n,lim}的丢番图性质与L的缺项性质联系起来；通过构造性方法得到不同σ_n测度的k-稠密集L。

Result: 给出了k-稠密性的拓扑等价刻画；证明了可以通过调控L的缺项性质来控制L_{n,lim}的σ_n测度（可构造测度为0或1的例子）。

Conclusion: 该工作为格点几何与丢番图逼近的交叉领域提供了新视角，并指出相关技术可能推广到多维晶体测度和Fourier拟晶的构造问题。

Abstract: Fix $1 \leq n < m, k = m-n.$ The Grassmannian $Gr(n,m)$ is a compact $kn$-dimensional manifold with a unique rotation invariant probability measure $σ_n.$ For $W \in Gr(n,m)$, $P_W : \mathbb R^m \mapsto W$ is orthogonal projection. A lattice subset $L \subset \mathbb Z^m \subset \mathbb R^m$ is called $k$-dense if it intersects $C(O) := \bigcup_{V \in O} V\backslash \{0\}$ for every nonempty open $O \subset Gr(k,m)$. We use Baire's category theorem [4] to prove that $L$ is $k$-dense iff $L_{n,lim} := \{W \in Gr(n,m) : 0 \mbox{ is a limit point of } P_W(L) \}$ is a $G_δ$ set. We use Khintchine-Groshev's theorem [5,13,20] to characterize Diophantine properties of $L_{n,lim}$ by lacunary properties of $L$ and construct $k$-dense $L$ with $σ_n(L_{n,lim}) = 0$ and with $σ_n(L_{n,lim}) = 1.$ We pose related questions about the construction of multidimensional crystalline measures and Fourier quasicrystals.

</details>


### [214] [Tame Galois Groups, Linking Numbers and Mildness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01083)
*Julian Feuerpfeil,Oussama Hamza,Donghyeok Lim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意有限个野素数集S₀（|S₀|≥2），存在由两个野素数构成的集合S₁，使得G_{S₀∪S₁}的上同调维数为2。该结果改进了Labute的定理，并推广到不含p次本原单位根的数域上。研究通过加权Zassenhaus滤子、图论方法和Koch型展示，回答了Labute关于温和群的一个问题，并应用于解决若干带指定分歧和分裂的上同调Galois反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Labute在其关于温和群的 seminal 论文中提出的一个问题，并改进其关于Galois群上同调维数的结果。

Method: 结合加权Zassenhaus滤子、图论方法和Koch型展示，分析最大pro-p扩张的Galois群在特定素数集上的结构性质。

Result: 对于任意|S₀|≥2的有限野素数集，存在两个野素数使得并集对应的Galois群上同调维数为2；该结果在数域不含p次本原单位根且满足适当分裂条件下也成立。

Conclusion: 回答了Labute的问题，改进了已有定理，并为带指定分歧和分裂的上同调Galois反问题提供了解法，附有数值实例和统计结果。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime and let $S$ be a set of tame primes. We denote by $G_S$ the Galois group of the maximal pro-$p$ extension of $\mathbb{Q}$ unramified outside $S$.
  We prove that for every finite set of tame primes $S_0$ with $|S_0|\geq 2$, there exists a set $S_1$ consisting of two tame primes such that $G_{S_0\cup S_1}$ has cohomological dimension $2$. This refines a result of Labute. More generally, we establish an analogous result for number fields not containing a primitive $p$-th root of unity, under a suitable splitting condition.
  Our approach answers a question of Labute, from his seminal paper on mild groups, and combines weighted Zassenhaus filtrations, graph-theoretic methods, and Koch-type presentations. As an application, we solve several cohomological Galois inverse problems with prescribed ramification and splitting. We also provide numerical examples and statistics.

</details>


### [215] [Piatetski-Shapiro Primes in short intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01115)
*Lingyu Guo,Victor Zhenyu Guo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了短区间中Piatetski-Shapiro素数的存在性，分别在θ和c的特定限制下证明了渐近公式和下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短区间中素数存在性是数论核心问题，而Piatetski-Shapiro素数$\lfloor n^c \rfloor$的研究是对著名猜想$n^2+1$型素数无穷性的近似。本文旨在扩展经典结果到Piatetski-Shapiro素数情形。

Method: 借鉴Guth-Maynard的渐近公式方法和Baker-Harman-Pintz的下界方法，将其推广到Piatetski-Shapiro素数在短区间中的分布问题，在参数θ和c的约束条件下进行分析。

Result: 在θ和c满足特定限制条件下，分别证明了Piatetski-Shapiro素数在短区间中存在的渐近公式和下界结果。

Conclusion: 将短区间中素数存在性的经典结果推广到了Piatetski-Shapiro素数情形，同时获得了渐近公式和下界两种类型的结论。

Abstract: The existence of primes in a short interval, which asks if there are prime numbers in the interval $[x, x + x^θ]$, is a core problem in number theory. Guth and Maynard proved the best known result for this problem with an asymptotic formula while Baker, Harman and Pintz proved the best lower bound result.
  In this article, we focus on Piatetski-Shapiro primes in a short interval. The study of Piatetski-Shapiro primes of the form $\lfloor n^c \rfloor$ is an approximation of the well-known conjecture that there exist infinitely many primes of the form $n^2+1$. We prove the existence of such primes under restrictions on $θ$ and $c$ with an asymptotic formula and a lower bound, respectively.

</details>


### [216] [Additive Rigidity for Images of Rational Points on Abelian Varieties II: The General Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01299)
*Seokhyun Choi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究阿贝尔簇上的群律与其到射影空间态射像上加法结构之间的相互作用，证明有限秩子群在仿射空间中的像不能具有强加法结构，即能量E(X)≪|X|²且和集|X+X|≫|X|²，去掉了作者先前结果中的简单性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究阿贝尔簇的群结构与其在仿射空间中像的加法结构之间的约束关系，特别关注有限秩子群在仿射空间中的像是否可能具有丰富的加法组合性质。此前结果需要简单性假设，本文旨在去除这一限制。

Method: 结合Gao-Ge-Kühne的一致Mordell-Lang猜想、对Ueno轨迹的精细使用、Rémond关于有界度阿贝尔子簇的有界性定理，以及对阿贝尔簇维数的归纳法。

Result: 对任意仿射图Aⁿ⊆ℙⁿ和任意有限子集X⊆f(Γ)∩Aⁿ，能量满足E(X)≪|X|²，和集满足|X+X|≫|X|²。这表明有限秩子群在仿射空间中的像不具有强加法结构。

Conclusion: 成功去除了作者先前结果中的简单性假设，建立了更一般的结论：阿贝尔簇有限秩子群在仿射空间中的像本质上是"非加法"的，其和集增长接近二次。

Abstract: We study the interaction between the group law on an abelian variety and the additive structure induced on its image under a morphism to a projective space. Let $A/F$ be an abelian variety, $f:A \rightarrow \mathbb{P}^n$ be a morphism which is finite onto its image, and $Γ\subseteq A(F)$ be a finite-rank subgroup. We show that for any affine chart $\mathbb{A}^n \subseteq \mathbb{P}^n$ and any finite subset $X \subseteq f(Γ) \cap \mathbb{A}^n$, the energy satisfies $E(X) \ll \lvert X \rvert^2$ and the sumset satisfies $\lvert X+X \rvert \gg \lvert X \rvert^2$. Thus images of finite-rank subgroups of abelian varieties cannot have strong additive structure in affine space. This removes the simplicity assumption from the author's previous result. The proof combines the uniform Mordell--Lang conjecture of Gao--Ge--Kühne with a refined use of the Ueno locus, Rémond's boundedness theorem for abelian subvarieties of bounded degree, and induction on the dimension of $A$.

</details>


### [217] [A Sharp Reverse Minkowski Inequality for the Gaussian Mass of Integral Unimodular Lattices Through Rank $32$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01347)
*Scott Duke Kominers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在n≤32维整数幺模格中，整数格ℤ^n具有最大高斯质量Θ_L(t)，并在40维偶幺模格上得到严格不等式。证明不依赖于格的分类，而是通过模函数u=Δ_8/ϑ_3^8参数化theta级数，并利用范数1分裂、ADE根系计数和影子正性来控制系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数格ℤ^n被猜想在所有n维稳定格中最大化高斯质量Θ_L(t)，该猜想是格论中的经典问题，与数论、调和分析和优化理论密切相关。

Method: 将积分幺模theta级数参数化为模函数u=Δ_8/ϑ_3^8∈(0,1/64]的多项式，利用范数1分裂、ADE根系计数和影子正性控制少数出现的系数，从而在不依赖格的分类的情况下证明不等式。

Result: 对每个n≤32的积分幺模格L，证明了Θ_L(t)≤Θ_ℤ^n(t)，等号仅当L≅ℤ^n；对每个40维偶幺模格，得到了严格不等式。

Conclusion: 该结果验证了整数格在低维幺模格类中具有最优的高斯质量，为更广泛的稳定格上的高斯质量最大化猜想提供了重要证据。

Abstract: The integer lattice $\mathbb{Z}^n$ is conjectured to maximize the Gaussian mass $Θ_L(t)=\sum_{x\in L}e^{-t\|x\|^2}$ over the set of stable lattices in $\mathbb{R}^n$, for every $t>0$. We prove this sharp inequality for every integral unimodular lattice $L$ of rank $n\leq 32$, with equality only at $L\cong\mathbb{Z}^n$, and furthermore obtain the strict inequality for every even unimodular lattice of rank $40$. The proof does not use the classification of unimodular lattices in these ranks; rather, it parametrizes integral unimodular theta series as polynomials in the modular function $u=Δ_8/\vartheta_3^8\in(0,1/64]$, with the few coefficients that arise controlled by norm-$1$ splitting, ADE root counts, and shadow positivity.

</details>


### [218] [Sums of Apostol's Möbius functions of order $k$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01384)
*Reo Terada*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对Bege关于k阶Möbius函数在算术级数上的和的猜想，完整解决了条件版本，部分解决了无条件版本，并给出了误差项的均方估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bege在2001年提出了关于k阶Möbius函数μ_k(n)在互素条件(n,q)=1下的和式的条件与非条件估计猜想，该问题尚未解决。

Method: 利用解析数论方法，对Apostol关于μ_k的渐近公式进行推广，结合算术级数上的分布性质，分别处理条件版本（依赖黎曼假设或类似假设）和无条件版本（不依赖未证明假设）的估计，并推导误差项的均方估计。

Result: 完整证明了Bege猜想的条件版本；部分证明了无条件版本；给出了误差项的均方估计结果。

Conclusion: 本文推进了k阶Möbius函数在算术级数上的和估计问题，为相关数论函数在互素条件下的渐近分析提供了更完整的理论依据。

Abstract: In 1970, T. M. Apostol introduced the Möbius function $μ_{k}$ of order $k$ for all positive integer $k$, as a generalization of the Möbius function $μ= μ_{1}$. For any integer $k \ge 2$, he proved $\sum_{n \le x} μ_{k}(n) = A_{k} x + O_{k}(x^{1/k} \log x)$ where $A_{k}$ is a positive constant. In 2001, A. Bege conjectured both the conditional and unconditional estimates for the sum $\sum_{n \le x, (n, q) = 1}μ_{k}(n)$ for any positive integer $q$. In this paper, we give affirmative solutions to the conditional version of Bege's conjecture completely and the unconditional one partially. We also give a mean square estimate for the error term.

</details>


### [219] [Distinguished defining polynomials for extensions of p-adic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01453)
*Jordi Guàrdia i Rúbies,John W. Jones,Kevin Keating,Sebastian Pauli,David P. Roberts,David Roe*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种为p-adic域扩张选择特定定义多项式的算法，该算法在L函数与模形式数据库中p-adic域数据库的最近扩展中起到了关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为p-adic域扩张选择一种规范的、可计算的定义多项式，以支持大规模数据库（如LMFDB）中p-adic域数据的扩展和规范化存储。

Method: 提出一种算法，用于从p-adic域扩张的多个可能的定义多项式中自动选择一个具有区分性或规范性的多项式。

Result: 该算法成功应用于L函数与模形式数据库（LMFDB）中p-adic域数据库的扩展，成为其中的关键组件。

Conclusion: 该算法为p-adic域数据库的构建提供了有效工具，有助于p-adic域数据的标准化和大规模集成。

Abstract: We give an algorithm for choosing a distinguished defining polynomial for a p-adic field extension. This algorithm formed an important ingredient in the recent expansion of the database of p-adic fields within the L-functions and modular forms database.

</details>


### [220] [Geometrization of summation formulae for quadrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01491)
*Chun-Hsien Hsu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对数域上偶数个变量的分裂二次型零点的Poisson求和公式进行了几何化处理，通过明确Braverman-Kazhdan空间与theta提升定义的二次型Schwartz空间之间的关系来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Poisson求和公式几何化到分裂二次型零点上，并理清两种不同定义方式下Schwartz空间的关系。

Method: 通过建立Braverman-Kazhdan空间与theta提升之间的联系，显式地关联这两种方式定义的二次型Schwartz空间。

Result: 成功几何化了数域上偶数个变量分裂二次型零点的Poisson求和公式。

Conclusion: 明确了Braverman-Kazhdan空间与theta提升定义的Schwartz空间之间的关系，为相关几何化工作奠定了基础。

Abstract: We geometrize the Poisson summation formula for the zero locus of a split quadratic form in an even number of variables over number fields. We do so by making explicit the relationship between Schwartz spaces on quadrics defined in two different ways: via Braverman-Kazhdan spaces and via theta lifts.

</details>


### [221] [High moments of random multiplicative functions twisted by Fourier coefficients of modular forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01514)
*Peng Gao,Liangyi Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在广义黎曼假设下，确定了模形式傅里叶系数与随机乘性函数混合乘积的部分和之矩的阶，精度达到 $e^{O(q^2)}$ 因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机乘性函数与模形式傅里叶系数的混合矩，探索数论中随机性与确定性结构的相互作用。

Method: 在广义黎曼假设（GRH）下，利用解析数论方法处理矩的估计。

Result: 对 $1 \leq q \leq c\log x/\log\log x$ 范围内的 $q$，确定了 $\E|\sum_{n \leq x} h(n)λ(n)|^{2q}$ 的量级，误差控制在 $e^{O(q^2)}$ 因子内。

Conclusion: 在GRH下，给出了随机乘性函数与模形式系数混合和的矩的精确阶，为进一步理解随机乘性函数的分布性质提供了新结果。

Abstract: Let $λ(n)$ denote the Fourier coefficients of a fixed modular form and $h(n)$ a Steinhaus or Rademacher random multiplicative function. In this paper, we determine, under the generalized Riemann hypothesis, the order of magnitude of $\E|\sum_{n \leq x} h(n)λ(n)|^{2q}$ up to factors of size $e^{O(q^2)}$, for all real $x, q$ with $1 \leq q \leq c\log x/\log\log x $ and $c>0$ a small constant.

</details>


### [222] [Intrinsic Subgroups and the $\ell$-adic Galois image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01571)
*Jacob Greene*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Yamazaki等人定义的Picard群挠子群上的双加性对称配对，该配对取值于k^× ⊗ ℚ/ℤ。定义了该配对的内蕴子群Pic(X)_tors^is。当X为椭圆曲线E时，将E≃Pic^0(E)进行识别，在纯代数意义上对任意域k上的许多椭圆曲线分类了E(k)_tors^is，并将Yamazaki等人的解析方法从ℚ推广到任意子域k⊂ℂ，最后针对k=ℚ描述了显式计算E(ℚ)_tors^is的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深化对Yamazaki等人在代数曲线上定义的Picard群挠子群配对及其内蕴子群的理解，特别是将其应用于椭圆曲线情形，在更一般的域上给出代数分类，并推广解析方法以拓展适用范围。

Method: 将椭圆曲线E与Pic^0(E)等同，在纯代数框架下对任意域k上的椭圆曲线分类E(k)_tors^is；将Yamazaki等人原先在ℚ上的解析方法推广到任意子域k⊂ℂ；针对k=ℚ设计显式算法计算E(ℚ)_tors^is。

Result: 在纯代数意义上完成了对许多椭圆曲线（任意域k上）的E(k)_tors^is分类；成功将解析方法从ℚ推广到ℂ的任意子域；给出了ℚ上椭圆曲线E(ℚ)_tors^is的显式计算算法。

Conclusion: 本文建立了椭圆曲线情形下Yamazaki配对内蕴子群的代数分类理论，拓展了方法的适用范围，并为具体计算提供了算法工具。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a geometrically irreducible smooth projective curve over a field $k$. Yamazaki et al. define a biadditive symmetric pairing $\langle -,-\rangle$ on the torsion subgroup of the Picard group $\mathrm{Pic}(X)$ with values in $k^\times \otimes \mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z}$. The intrinsic subgroup $\mathrm{Pic}(X)_\mathrm{tors}^\mathrm{is}$ is the kernel of this pairing. When $X$ is an elliptic curve $E$, we can identify $E \simeq \mathrm{Pic}^0(E)$. We classify $E(k)_\mathrm{tors}^\mathrm{is}$ in purely algebraic terms for many elliptic curves over an arbitrary field $k$. We give a generalization of the analytic methods of Yamazaki et al. from $\mathbb{Q}$ to an arbitrary field $k \subset \mathbb{C}$. Lastly, for $k=\mathbb{Q}$, we describe an algorithm to explicitly compute $E(\mathbb{Q})_\mathrm{tors}^\mathrm{is}$.

</details>


### [223] [On a problem on a generalization of Euler's totient function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01633)
*John M. Campbell*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Büyükaşik等人关于广义欧拉 totient 函数 φ_k(n) 的集合 D_1 = {1, 3, 15} 的猜想，证明过程基于与 GPT-5.5 Pro 的大量交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Büyükaşik等人引入了广义欧拉 totient 函数 φ_k(n)，并定义了集合 D_s，证明了其对每个 s 有限，并猜想 D_1 = {1, 3, 15}。本文旨在证明该猜想。

Method: 基于与 GPT-5.5 Pro 的大量交互进行论证。

Result: 成功证明了 D_1 = {1, 3, 15} 这一猜想。

Conclusion: 通过使用 GPT-5.5 Pro 的交互式论证，证实了 Büyükaşik 等人的猜想。

Abstract: Büyükaşik et al. [Publ. Math. Debrecen, 2024] introduced a family of generalizations of Euler's totient function $\varphi(n)$, by setting $\varphi_k(n) = \sum_{a} a^k$ for $a \in [1, n]$ such that $(a, n) = 1$, with $\varphi_0(n) = \varphi(n)$. Letting $\mathcal{D}_{s} = \{ k \geq s : \forall n \geq 1 \ \varphi_s(n) \mid \varphi_k(n) \}$, Büyükaşik et al. proved that $\mathcal{D}_{s}$ is finite for each $s \geq 0$, and conjectured that $\mathcal{D}_{1} = \{ 1, 3, 15 \}$ and provided computations to support this conjecture. We succeed in proving this conjecture, using an argument based on our extensive interactions with GPT-5.5 Pro.

</details>


### [224] [On cyclotomic matrices related to Kloosterman sums over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01759)
*Hai-Liang Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上Kloosterman和构成的循环矩阵的奇异性，证明矩阵[K_p(1,i²+j²)]当p≥11时奇异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kloosterman和是有限域上Bessel函数的模拟，具有重要的数论意义。本文旨在通过特征和的性质研究Kloosterman和构成的矩阵的代数性质。

Method: 利用有限域F_p上特征和的算术性质，分析Kloosterman和矩阵的结构和奇异性条件。

Result: 证明了矩阵[K_p(1,i²+j²)]_{1≤i,j≤(p-1)/2}在p≥11时是奇异的，而对于较小素数(p<11)则非奇异。

Conclusion: Kloosterman和矩阵的奇异性与素数p的大小密切相关，存在一个阈值(p=11)使得矩阵性质发生转变。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime and $\mathbb{F}_p$ be the finite field with $p$ elements. For any $a,b\in\mathbb{F}_p$, it is known that the Kloosterman sum
  $$K_p(a,b)=\sum_{x\in\mathbb{F}_p\setminus\{0\}}e^{\frac{2πi}{p}(ax+\frac{b}{x})}$$
  can be viewed as a finite field analogue of certain Bessel function. In this paper, using the arithmetic properties of character sums over $\mathbb{F}_p$, we study some cyclotomic matrices involving Kloosterman sums. For example, we prove that the matrix $[K_p(1,i^2+j^2)]_{1\le i,j\le (p-1)/2}$ is singular if and only if $p\ge11$.

</details>


### [225] [Infinite Moments of Class Groups for Solvable Fields with a Normal Abelian Subgroup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01831)
*Weitong Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文运用类域论和Minkowski界，结合可解Galois群的条件与数域排序，得到了限制分歧条件下解的上界估计，并证明了类群无穷矩的存在性。特别地，对于按分歧素理想乘积排序的非Galois三次域，结合二次域的Z/3Z矩与按广义判别式排序的三次域计数，证明了Z/3Z矩为无穷大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数域计数与类群矩的研究中，对于非Galois三次域在特定排序下的Z/3Z矩的无穷性缺乏严格的证明。本文旨在填补这一空白，并为更一般的可解Galois群情形提供统一的分析框架。

Method: 运用类域论与Minkowski界获得限制分歧条件下解的上界估计；结合可解群的结构条件与数域的特定排序方式（按分歧素理想乘积及广义判别式），推导出计数问题的上界，进而证明类群无穷矩的存在性。

Result: 建立了限制分歧条件下解的上界估计；证明了非Galois三次域按分歧素理想乘积排序时，其Z/3Z矩为无穷大；同时给出了二次域Z/3Z矩的相应结果以及按广义判别式排序的三次域计数结果。

Conclusion: 本文的方法可推广至可解Galois群下的更一般数域族，为研究类群矩的无穷行为提供了有效的分析工具。

Abstract: We apply the class field theory and Minkowski bound to obtain an upper bound estimate for the number of solutions to the restricted ramifications when the Galois group is solvable. Together with suitable conditions on the solvable group and the ordering of number fields, we could prove an upper bound on specific field-counting problems, hence the infinite moment of the class groups. In particular, for non-Galois cubic fields ordered by the product of ramified primes, we could show that the $\mathbb{Z}/3\mathbb{Z}$-moment is infinite with the results on the $\mathbb{Z}/3\mathbb{Z}$-moment of quadratic number fields and the field-counting on cubic fields ordered by the generalized discriminant.

</details>


### [226] [Applications of a formula of Maesaka-Seki-Watanabe type for multiple harmonic $q$-sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02123)
*Yoshihiro Takeyama,Yuto Tsuruta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出q-模拟公式的两个应用：一是提供q-模拟多重zeta值对偶性的替代证明，二是证明q-模拟川岛函数的一个恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Maesaka-Seki-Watanabe的多重调和和公式及Yamamoto的Schur型推广至q-模拟，并探索该q-模拟公式的应用价值。

Method: 基于先前建立的q-模拟公式，分别应用于q-模拟多重zeta值的对偶性和q-模拟川岛函数的恒等式证明。

Result: 成功给出了q-模拟多重zeta值对偶性的替代证明，并证明了q-模拟川岛函数的一个恒等式。

Conclusion: 该q-模拟公式具有广泛的应用前景，可用于证明多重zeta值及其q-模拟中的多种重要恒等式和对偶性质。

Abstract: Maesaka, Seki and Watanabe proved a formula for multiple harmonic sums. Yamamoto generalized it to Schur-type multiple harmonic sums, and the second author proved a $q$-analogue of this generalization. In this paper, we give two applications of the $q$-analogue formula. The first is an alternative proof of the duality of a $q$-analogue of multiple zeta values. The second is a proof of an identity for a $q$-analogue of the Kawashima function.

</details>


### [227] [Universality of the cokernels of random $p$-adic matrices with inhomogeneously balanced columns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02180)
*Jungin Lee,Sungjin Park*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了当随机p进矩阵的列满足非均匀平衡条件时，其余核的分布具有普适性，收敛于Haar随机矩阵的极限分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Nguyen和Wood关于均匀平衡列的普适性定理推广到非均匀平衡列的情形。

Method: 考虑一个n×(n+u)的随机p进矩阵A(n)，其第i列满足α_n(i)-平衡条件，并且要求对任意ε>0，当n→∞时∑exp(-εα_n(i)n)→0。

Result: 在上述条件下，A(n)的余核分布当n→∞时收敛于Haar随机n×(n+u)矩阵的余核极限分布。

Conclusion: 证明了随机p进矩阵余核分布在非均匀平衡列条件下的普适性，推广了已有结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove universality of the distribution of the cokernels of a random $p$-adic matrix with inhomogeneously balanced columns. More precisely, let $u \ge 0$ be an integer and $A(n)$ be a random $n \times (n+u)$ matrix over $\mathbb{Z}_p$ whose $i$-th column is $α_n(i)$-balanced. We prove that if $\sum_{i=1}^{n+u} \exp(-εα_n(i)n) \to 0$ as $n \to \infty$ for every $ε>0$, then the cokernels of $A(n)$ converge in distribution, as $n \to \infty$, to the same limiting law as the cokernels of Haar-random $n \times (n+u)$ matrices over $\mathbb{Z}_p$. This extends a universality theorem of Nguyen and Wood to random $p$-adic matrices with inhomogeneously balanced columns.

</details>


### [228] [On the unit equation $\varepsilon+δ=n$ in cubic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02244)
*Maleeha Khawaja,Samir Siksek*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了三次域中的单位方程 ε + δ = n（n为非零整数且n≠2），并证明当按判别式排序时，该方程对100%的三次域无解。这一结果与作者们近期的一个猜想一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探究三次域中单位方程 ε + δ = n 的解的存在性问题，并验证其近期提出的相关猜想。

Method: 作者通过对三次域按判别式进行排序，采用密度分析方法来研究单位方程的解的存在性。

Result: 证明了对于100%的三次域（按判别式排序），方程 ε + δ = n 无解。

Conclusion: 该结果支持了作者们近期提出的关于单位方程解的猜想。

Abstract: Let n be a non-zero integer not equal to 2. We consider the unit equation $\varepsilon + δ= n$ in units $\varepsilon, δ$ of cubic fields. We show that this equation has no solutions for 100% of cubic fields, when ordered by discriminant. This is consistent with a recent conjecture of the authors.

</details>


### [229] [On periods of Elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02254)
*Daniel Barrera Salaza,Juan-Pablo Llerena-Córdova*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在椭圆曲线具有分裂乘性约化的素数p处，将E的L-不变量的驯顺部分与Darmon提出的自守p-adic周期联系起来，通过使用扭曲版本的精细化例外零猜想证明了两者的相等性。当椭圆曲线的导子恰好为p且自守周期附属于导子1的最优嵌入时，利用de-Shalit的工作无条件证明了该等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立椭圆曲线L-不变量的驯顺部分与Darmon自守p-adic周期之间的显式联系，从而深化对例外零现象的理解，并为BSD猜想等相关问题提供新的算术视角。

Method: 采用扭曲版本的精细化例外零猜想作为桥梁，通过比较两种不同来源的p-adic不变量来证明等式。在特殊情形（导子恰好为p且嵌入为最优）下，借助de-Shalit的经典工作给出无条件证明。

Result: 证明了椭圆曲线在分裂乘性约化素数p处的L-不变量的驯顺部分与Darmon自守p-adic周期之间的相等性；在导子为p且嵌入为最优的条件下达成了无条件证明。

Conclusion: L-不变量的驯顺部分具有自守解释，这进一步支持了例外零猜想与p-adic Langlands纲领之间的深层联系，并为计算L-不变量提供了新的自守途径。

Abstract: Let $E$ be an elliptic curve over $\mathbb{Q}$ having split multiplicative reduction at a prime number $p$. We describe the tame part of the $\mathcal{L}$-invariant of $E$ at $p$ in terms of automorphic $p$-adic periods introduced in the work of Darmon. More precisely, we prove an equality of refined $\mathcal{L}$-invariants using twisted versions of refined exceptional zero conjectures. When the conductor of the elliptic curve is exactly $p$ and the automorphic period is attached to an optimal embedding of conductor $1$ then we prove this equality unconditionally by using the work of de-Shalit.

</details>


### [230] [Arithmetic regularity as an alternative to transference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02312)
*Sam Chow,Sean Prendiville,Santiago Vazquez*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算术正则性的新方法，替代了自Green(2005)以来主导稀疏算术集上组合定理的傅里叶分析传递原理。该方法更通用，能处理缺乏"稠密模型"的情形，并将圆法推广为包含"组合"因子的框架，从而直接得到稠密集构型数的正确下界。作者以线性方程与高次方程组成的系统为例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Green(2005)以来，傅里叶分析传递原理在稀疏算术集的组合定理研究中占据主导地位，但该方法在缺乏明显"稠密模型"的情形下失效。作者希望找到一种更通用的替代方法，能够处理更广泛的算术问题，并得到构型计数下界。

Method: 采用算术正则性方法替代传统的傅里叶分析传递原理。该方法将传统圆法中"实数部分+p-adic部分"的分解推广为"实数部分+p-adic部分+组合部分"的三元分解，从而直接得到稠密集构型数的下界。

Result: 新方法更通用，能够在缺乏稠密模型时成功应用；它将圆法推广为包含组合因子的框架；直接给出了稠密集构型数的正确下界。以线性方程与高次方程组成的系统为例进行了验证。

Conclusion: 算术正则性方法是对傅里叶分析传递原理的有力替代，具有更广泛的适用性，并将经典的圆法思想推广到了新的维度。

Abstract: Since Green (2005), the Fourier-analytic transference principle has dominated the landscape of combinatorial theorems relative to sparse arithmetic sets. We demonstrate a different approach using arithmetic regularity. This is more versatile and has the potential to succeed when no obvious `dense model' is forthcoming. Moreover, we contend that, just as the traditional circle method disassembles an arithmetic problem into real and $p$-adic parts which can be solved individually, the arithmetic regularity method generalises this to yield an additional `combinatorial' factor. This framework leads directly to a correct lower bound on the number of configurations in a dense set. We illustrate this using a system comprising a linear equation together with a higher-degree equation.

</details>


### [231] [Cyclotomic factors of rational necklace functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02324)
*Nguyen Cao Minh,Nguyen Vu Hoang Minh,Dung Nguyen,Tung T. Nguyen,Nguyen Duy Tan,Duong Tran*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入一类新的有理项链函数，统一了项链多项式和费克特多项式，揭示了分圆多项式作为其因子的多种出现方式，并发现了伽罗瓦群在生成新分圆因子中的隐藏作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 项链多项式在组合数学、李理论和有限域伽罗瓦理论等多个数学领域中涌现，其算术性质近年被广泛研究。本文旨在统一两个经典多项式族——项链多项式和费克特多项式，并探究分圆多项式在其中作为因子的出现机制。

Method: 通过引入一类新的有理项链函数，统一定义了项链多项式和费克特多项式；利用伽罗瓦理论分析分圆多项式因子的出现条件，特别关注先前工作未覆盖的新分圆因子的生成机制。

Result: 描述了分圆多项式作为有理项链函数因子的多种出现方式；统一并推广了此前关于项链多项式和费克特多项式的多项成果；发现某些伽罗瓦群在新分圆因子的出现中扮演了隐藏角色。

Conclusion: 本文提出的有理项链函数框架有效地统一了两类经典多项式族，揭示了分圆因子出现的新机制，特别是伽罗瓦群的隐藏作用，为后续研究提供了更一般的理论基础。

Abstract: Necklace polynomials arise in various fields of mathematics, including combinatorics, Lie theory, and Galois theory over finite fields. Their arithmetic properties have been extensively studied in recent years. In this article, we introduce a new class of rational necklace functions that unifies two well-studied families of polynomials: necklace polynomials and Fekete polynomials. We describe several ways in which cyclotomic polynomials appear as factors of these rational necklace functions. Our results unify and generalize various earlier work on necklace polynomials and on Fekete polynomials. In particular, we describe a surprising phenomenon in which certain Galois groups play a hidden role in the appearance of new cyclotomic factors that are not covered by these previous works.

</details>


### [232] [Analogues of the Lindelöf Hypothesis for the Barnes multiple zeta function and related problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02407)
*Takashi Miyagawa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Barnes多重zeta函数的Lindelöf猜想类比，建立了其与积分均值估计的等价条件，并发现关键依赖于参数$w_1,\dots,w_r$的$\mathbb{Q}$-秩，出现了该函数特有的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lindelöf猜想是解析数论中关于Riemann zeta函数的重要开放问题，已知等价于某些均值估计。本文将其推广到Barnes多重zeta函数，探索该函数框架下的类比结果。

Method: 通过建立Barnes多重zeta函数在虚部趋于无穷时的点态上界与积分均值之间的等价关系，并系统分析参数组的$\mathbb{Q}$-秩对这一等价关系的影响。

Result: 获得了Barnes多重zeta函数的Lindelöf猜想类比的等价条件，揭示了其本质上依赖于$\langle w_1,\dots,w_r\rangle$的$\mathbb{Q}$-秩，并发现了该秩导致的独特现象。

Conclusion: Barnes多重zeta函数的Lindelöf型猜想与均值估计的等价性成立，且呈现出依赖于参数有理线性相关秩的新颖特性。

Abstract: For the Lindelöf Hypothesis concerning the Riemann zeta function $ζ(s)$, upper bounds as $\Im(s)\to\infty$ have been extensively studied for many years. In particular, the Lindelöf Hypothesis is one of the most important open problems in analytic number theory. It is also known to be equivalent to certain mean value estimates, which provide a fundamental connection between pointwise upper bounds and integral mean values of zeta-functions.
  In this paper, we consider an analogue of the Lindelöf Hypothesis for the Barnes multiple zeta function $ζ_r (s,a,(w_1,\dots,w_r)) = \sum_{m_1=0}^\infty \cdots \sum_{m_r=0}^\infty (a+m_1 w_1+\cdots+m_r w_r)^{-s} $, and establish equivalent conditions in terms of integral mean values. In particular, the situation depends essentially on the $\Q$-rank of $\langle w_1,\dots,w_r\rangle$, and it is especially interesting that phenomena peculiar to the Barnes multiple zeta function appear according to this rank.

</details>


### [233] [Finding the complement of an elliptic curve inside a Jacobian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02429)
*Andrea Gallese,Davide Lombardo,Francesco Naccarato,Umberto Zannier*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了一个简单算法，用于解决亏格2曲线X到椭圆曲线的有效性问题：给定一个非平凡映射π: X→E，计算另一个独立于π的椭圆曲线E'及映射π': X→E'，等价于计算Jac(X)同源分解中的互补椭圆因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题已被广泛研究，更一般的问题也已通过深刻且强大的技术得到解决，但作者注意到现有文献中缺乏本文所描述的简单显式过程。

Method: 提出一个简单算法，通过给定的亏格2曲线X到椭圆曲线E的映射π，计算互补的椭圆曲线E'及映射π'，从而实现Jac(X)的同源分解。

Result: 给出了一个简单显式的算法过程，能够有效计算互补椭圆因子。

Conclusion: 提供了一个简洁的算法，填补了该问题在简单显式方法上的空白。

Abstract: This note gives a simple algorithm for the following effectivity problem: given a genus $2$ curve $X$ together with a nonconstant map $π:X\to E$ to an elliptic curve, determine an elliptic curve $E'$ and a map $π':X\to E'$ independent of $π$. Equivalently, we compute the complementary elliptic factor in the decomposition of $\operatorname{Jac}(X)$ up to isogeny. While the problem has been studied extensively, and more general ones have been solved by deep and powerful techniques, we are not aware of a reference for the simple explicit procedure described here.

</details>

### [234] [Mechanics of Pandemics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00192)
*Seba Contreras,Philipp Dönges,Laura Müller,Piklu Mallick,Sydney Paltra,Ulrik Hvid,Robyn J. N. Kettlitz,Andreas Reitenbach,Rodrigo Amaral Lind,Maíra Aguiar,Philip Bechtle,André Calero Valdez,Ronja Gronemeyer,Manuela Harries,Veronika K. Jaeger,André Karch,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Peter Klimek,Mirjam E. Kretzschmar,Kai Nagel,Bjarke Frost Nielsen,Barbara Prainsack,Isabella M. Radhuber,Lone Simonsen,Kim Sneppen,Janik Suer,Viola Priesemann*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文由多学科团队总结出10条影响大流行发生与控制的基本机制，强调理解这些机制对提升未来大流行防范与应对能力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COVID-19及以往大流行表明疾病能深刻扰乱、威胁和改变日常生活。由于病原体和社会持续演变，每场大流行各不相同，但疾病传播的某些基本原则在不同疫情中仍然成立，理解这些机制有助于更好地应对未来大流行。

Method: 由公共卫生、医学、流行病学、政治学、数学、物理学和心理学等多学科团队协作，梳理并总结了10条影响大流行传播与控制的基本机制。

Result: 归纳出10条基于自然规律或社会结构的基本机制，这些机制虽然可能未被充分重视，但实质上深刻影响了大流行的演化过程与防控效果。

Conclusion: 越成功理解这些机制并将相关知识融入社会，就越能为未来大流行做好准备并在发生时做出恰当应对。

Abstract: COVID-19 and previous pandemics have shown how diseases can disrupt, threaten, and transform daily life. Since pathogens and societies are continuously evolving, every pandemic is different. However, certain fundamental principles of disease transmission appear to hold true across different outbreaks. These ``mechanisms'' are grounded in natural laws or the very structure of our biology and societies. This paper compiles ten fundamental mechanisms, curated by a multidisciplinary team with backgrounds spanning public health, medicine, epidemiology, political science, mathematics, physics, and psychology. These mechanisms, although perhaps underappreciated, substantially shape how pandemics unfold and are controlled. The better we succeed in understanding these mechanisms and establishing this knowledge in our societies, the better we will be able to prepare for future pandemics and respond appropriately when they occur.

</details>


### [235] [Evolution of cooperation in the multiplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00196)
*Zijie Chen,Xingru Chen,Feng Fu*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多层网络中基于多表型同质性（multi-phenotype homophily）的合作演化通用框架，推导出自然选择偏好合作的解析条件，发现表型多样性通过将种群分割为选型生态位来促进合作，并且囚徒困境的强度变化会改变合作对策略突变的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物和社会系统中，合作往往依赖于表型线索而非随机相遇。现有研究未能充分考虑现实世界中互动在多个同时维度上展开的情况，因此需要建立一个能够处理多层网络（multiplex networks）中多表型同质性驱动的合作演化通用框架。

Method: 构建了一个多层网络上的合作演化通用框架，考虑了独立或存在上位效应（epistasis）的表型特征以及不同的突变耦合模式。通过数学推导获得了自然选择偏好合作的解析条件，并分析了有限种群中的动态行为。

Result: 合作的条件可分解为各层特定的σ规则（σ-rules），仅依赖于局部收益结构、有效表型数量和突变率。表型多样性通过将种群分割为选型生态位（assortative niches）促进合作。在有限种群中，囚徒困境强度增加会使合作对策略突变的依赖从单调递减→U形→单调递增转变。

Conclusion: 该工作为多表型同质性如何支撑异质种群中合作的演化动力学提供了统一的理论解释。

Abstract: Across biological and social systems, cooperation often depends on phenotypic cues rather than random encounters. To account for real-world interactions unfolding across multiple, simultaneous dimensions, here we develop a general framework for the evolution of cooperation in multiplex networks governed by multi-phenotype homophily. We derive analytical conditions for natural selection to favor cooperation across phenotypic traits that are independent or exhibit epistasis and under different modes of mutation coupling. Despite the integration of fitness across layers, the conditions for cooperation resolve into layer-specific $σ$-rules, depending only on the local payoff structure, the effective number of phenotypes, and the mutation rates. We show that phenotypic diversity fosters cooperation by partitioning populations into assortative niches. Furthermore, in finite populations, intensifying the prisoner's dilemma shifts the dependence of cooperation on strategy mutation from monotonically decreasing, through U-shaped, to monotonically increasing. Our work provides a unified account of how multi-phenotype homophily underpins the evolutionary dynamics of cooperation in heterogeneous populations.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Towards fast computation of higher discrete homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00245)
*Jacob Ender,Chris Kapulkin*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算图第二离散同调群的新算法，通过识别五种基本形状（3-立方体的商图）作为2-边界的检测器，显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法计算图的第二离散同调群速度较慢，需要更高效的替代方案。

Method: 识别五种基本形状（3-立方体的商图），利用从这些形状到目标图的单射映射来检测奇异链复形中所有可能的2-边界，从而加速计算。

Result: 新算法相比现有算法在计算图第二离散同调群时速度显著更快。

Conclusion: 通过五种基本形状的识别，能够高效检测所有2-边界，从而实现更快的同调群计算。

Abstract: We develop a new algorithm for computing the second discrete homology group of a graph which is much faster when compared to existing algorithms. To do so, we identify five basic shapes, which are quotient graphs of the 3-cube with the property that the injective maps from them detect all possible 2-boundaries in the singular chain complex computing discrete homology.

</details>


### [237] [On Fréchet Traveling Salesmen Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01147)
*Omrit Filtser,Tzalik Maimon,Michal Moiseev*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Fréchet距离约束下构建两条曲线的TSP变体问题，提出了离散Fréchet距离下的近线性算法，并证明了连续Fréchet距离下问题的NP难性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fréchet距离通常用于曲线相似性度量，本文旨在展示其在多智能体协同路径规划中的新应用价值，即两个智能体需在保持近距离的同时访问给定站点集合。

Method: 将问题建模为Fréchet距离约束下的TSP变体；针对离散Fréchet距离设计了近线性算法；研究了曲线长度最小化和站点分配平衡等变体；通过归约证明了连续Fréchet距离下的NP难性。

Result: 在离散Fréchet距离下提出了近线性时间算法；证明了连续Fréchet距离下该问题是NP难的。

Conclusion: Fréchet距离除了用于曲线相似性度量，还能有效约束多智能体协同路径规划问题；离散与连续情形下问题的计算复杂度存在本质差异。

Abstract: The Fréchet distance is a well-studied distance measure between two curves. In this work, we demonstrate that the merit of Fréchet distance extends beyond evaluating similarity, and introduce a new setting in which it proves useful. Consider a situation where two agents are required to visit a given set of sites, while staying close to each other throughout their traversal. In this paper, we study problems where the goal is to construct two curves whose vertices are from a given set of points, under the constraint that the Fréchet distance between the curves is kept as small as possible. This problem can be viewed as a variant of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP), and thus may be of interest in routing, network planning and more. We present a near-linear algorithm for this problem under the discrete Fréchet distance, and explore several variants of the problem, including minimizing the lengths of the curves and balancing the number of sites assigned to each agent. Lastly, we prove that the problem is NP-hard under the continuous Fréchet Distance.

</details>
