### [238] [BBH-Genesis: Disentangling Binary Black Hole Formation Channels with GWTC-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00234)
*Shaunak Padhyegurjar,Suvodip Mukherjee*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于GWTC-4引力波数据，开发了BBH-Genesis推理管道，发现双黑洞事件最有力地支持双通道形成模型（其中包含非孤立双星形成通道），第三通道（AGN形成）仅有微弱支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理建模复杂，难以从理论预测区分不同形成渠道，因此需要数据驱动的方法从已探测的引力波事件中识别不同形成通道的存在。

Method: 开发了新的推理管道BBH-Genesis，应用于第四版引力波目录（GWTC-4），通过数据驱动方式识别多个潜在的不同子群体。

Result: GWTC-4中所有双黑洞事件最有力地支持双通道场景，暗示存在非孤立双星形成通道；该子群体可进一步划分为第三通道（AGN形成），但在有效自旋和质量比关联上仅有微弱支持。

Conclusion: 当前数据下双通道模型最具证据支持，未来更多事件探测将有助于判断是否需要至少三个通道来解释引力波观测到的双黑洞事件。

Abstract: The detected population of binary black holes (BBHs) from the gravitational wave (GW) data has made it possible to decipher their formation and evolution history over cosmic time. The complexity of astrophysical modeling of binary mergers makes it challenging to predict key signatures for different formation channels. As a result, one of the major avenues to discover the presence of different channels from detected GW events is through a data-driven way which can isolate different scenarios. In this spirit, we developed a new inference pipeline BBH-Genesis and applied it on the fourth GW catalog (GWTC-4) to identify the presence of multiple underlying distinct populations. We find that the current population of all the binary events in GWTC-4 can be explained with the strongest evidence for only a two-channel scenario, hinting at the presence of a non-isolated binary formation channel. This sub-population can be further divided into a third channel with mild support towards formation in AGN exhibiting a slightly different effective spin and mass ratio correlation. In the future, with the detection of more events, it will be clearer whether it is necessary to consider at least three channels to explain the BBH events detected using GW observations.

</details>


### [239] [The Delta Resonance in the Neutrino Sky](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00249)
*Arifa Khatee Zathul,Ke Fang,Francis Halzen,Dan Hooper*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出IceCube探测到的宇宙中微子能谱在~30 TeV处的断裂特征可能源于pγ相互作用中的Δ-重子共振，并论证该机制可自然解释观测谱形，同时缓解中微子源对各向同性伽马射线背景的贡献矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IceCube观测到的扩散宇宙中微子通量在~30 TeV处存在谱断裂，现有模型难以完美解释这一特征，需要提出新的物理机制来自然描述该观测现象。

Method: 采用质子谱dN_p/dE_p ∝ E_p^{-3.1}与典型能量~0.3 keV的X射线相互作用，通过Δ-重子共振产生中微子，计算能谱并与观测数据对比。

Result: 该模型能自然拟合观测到的中微子能谱及断裂特征；显著降低中微子源对各向同性伽马射线背景的贡献，缓解了长期存在的理论-观测矛盾；伴随产生的伽马射线级联至MeV-GeV能量，在~3 GeV处贡献约10%的各向同性伽马射线背景。

Conclusion: 若该方案成立，可能意味着我们已识别出产生河外宇宙射线的占主导地位的源。

Abstract: Recent measurements of the diffuse cosmic neutrino flux by IceCube show evidence for a spectral break at an energy near $E_ν\sim 30$ TeV. In this letter, we suggest that this feature may be due to the $Δ$-baryon resonance in $pγ$ interactions. We show that the measured spectrum, including the observed break, can be naturally accommodated by a flux of protons accelerated with a spectrum $dN_p /dE_p \propto E_p^{-3.1}$ interacting with X-rays of typical energy $E_γ \sim 0.3\,{\rm keV}$. We also point out that the presence of this spectral break significantly reduces the contribution of neutrino sources to the isotropic gamma-ray background, alleviating the longstanding tension between these measurements. In the $Δ$-resonance scenario, the gamma rays accompanying neutrino production cascade down to MeV-GeV energies and contribute at the $\sim 10\%$ level to the isotropic gamma-ray background at $\sim 3$~GeV. If our proposal is realized, it may imply that we have identified the dominant sources that produce the extragalactic cosmic rays.

</details>


### [240] [Hard X-ray Sources in Fermi UFOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00331)
*Alessandro Paggi,Ioannis Liodakis,John Antoniadis,Chien-Ting Chen,Steven R. Ehlert,Daniel Gruen,Philip Kaaret,Ignacio De La Calle Perez,Elena Jimenez Bailon,Mykhailo Ilin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过将未标识的费米γ射线源（UFOs）与硬X射线源位置匹配，并结合多波段软X射线数据，为17个UFOs中的15个找到了可能的低能对应体，包括耀变体、塞弗特星系、X射线双星、激变变星等，并对其他类型来源的关联性进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: γ射线探测器提供的位置不确定性较大，导致未标识γ射线源与低能对应体的识别和关联是现代天文学中的一个关键挑战，亟需通过多波段交叉匹配来鉴定这些UFOs的性质。

Method: 从Palermo Swift-BAT硬X射线星表和SRG/ART-XC全天X射线巡天星表中选取与未标识费米天体（UFOs）位置兼容的硬X射线源，进而收集Swift-XRT、Chandra-ACIS、XMM-Newton-EPIC和eROSITA的软X射线数据，识别软X射线对应体，并基于多波段特征进行分类和关联分析。

Result: 发现17个UFOs有重叠的硬X射线源；识别出16个软X射线对应体；将15个源与已知天体关联，分类包括：耀变体/耀变体候选体（2个）、塞弗特星系（5个）、X射线双星（2个）、普通X射线源（1个）、激变变星（2个）、变星（3个）。

Conclusion: 耀变体和塞弗特星系很可能是UFOs的低能对应体；X射线双星是潜在的对应体；一个普通X射线源被建议为脉冲星候选体；若确认，2个激变变星将成为首例该类γ射线发射源；3个变星与UFOs的关联被认为不太可能。

Abstract: Identification and/or association of unidentified $γ$-ray sources with lower-energy counterparts represents a key challenge in modern astronomy, due to the relatively large positional uncertainty provided by $γ$-ray detectors. We selected Unidentified Fermi Objects (UFOs) positionally compatible with hard X-ray sources in the latest Palermo Swift-BAT hard X-ray Catalog and in the SRG/ART-XC all-sky X-ray survey Catalog, to identify lower-energy sources and possibly associate them to the UFOs. We found 17 UFOs with overlapping hard X-ray sources. We then collected soft X-ray data from Swift-XRT, Chandra-ACIS, XMM-Newton-EPIC, and eROSITA, identified 16 soft X-ray counterparts to the hard X-ray sources, and associate 15 with known astronomical objects, classified as: blazars/blazars candidates (2 sources), Seyfert galaxies (5 sources), X-ray binaries (2 sources), generic X-ray sources (1 source), cataclysmic variables (2 sources), and variable stars (3 sources). Blazars and Seyfert galaxies are likely lower-energy counterparts to the UFOs, since their mid-IR colors and broad-band spectral energy distributions suggest significant jetted, non-thermal emission. X-ray binaries can be potential lower-energy counterparts to the UFOs, since this class of sources has been already observed to emit $γ$-rays. The generic X-ray source has been proposed as a pulsar candidate, and we therefore suggest that it can be the lower-energy counterpart to the UFO. Cataclysmic variables have been suggested as potential $γ$-ray emitters so, if confirmed, the 2 sources classified as cataclysmic variables would represent the first $γ$-ray emitting sources of this kind. Finally, we consider the association of the 3 variable stars with the UFOs unlikely.

</details>


### [241] [Suppression of boosted relic neutrinos by photon backgrounds during ultra-high-energy cosmic ray propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00498)
*Gabriel Azeredo,Vitor de Souza*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛传播框架重新评估了被超高能宇宙射线增强的宇宙中微子背景（CνB）的可观测性，发现与漫射光子背景的相互作用会强烈抑制增强的中微子通量，且要使CνB产生可观测效应，其过密度需达到极端值（η≳10⁸）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束宇宙中微子背景是宇宙学中的重大实验挑战，先前研究未有效考虑初级宇宙射线的能量损失以及与漫射光子背景的竞争性相互作用。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛传播框架，一致地纳入宇宙射线能量损失、原子核光致解离以及次级中微子的产生过程。

Result: 漫射光子背景的相互作用强烈抑制了简化传播场景中预测的增强中微子通量；要使CνB对UHECR能谱产生可观测抑制或与宇宙成因中微子通量相当，其过密度需达η≳10⁸的极端量级。

Conclusion: 先前对增强CνB通量的估计过于乐观，实际观测约束难度极大，需要极端过密度条件才能产生可观测信号。

Abstract: Constraining the cosmic neutrino background (C$ν$B) represents a major experimental challenge in cosmology. Recent studies have suggested that relic neutrinos boosted by ultra-high-energy cosmic rays (UHECRs) may generate observable diffuse neutrino fluxes. Previous estimates have not effectively propagated the primary cosmic rays, often neglecting crucial energy losses and the unavoidable, competing interactions with diffuse photon backgrounds. Here we revisit these expectations using a realistic Monte Carlo propagation framework. This approach allows us to consistently incorporate cosmic ray energy losses, nuclear photodisintegration, and production of secondary neutrinos. We show that interactions with diffuse photon backgrounds strongly suppress the boosted relic neutrino flux predicted in simplified propagation scenarios. Furthermore, we demonstrate that to produce any observable suppression on the UHECR energy spectrum at Earth, or for the boosted C$ν$B component to become comparable to the cosmogenic neutrino flux, the C$ν$B density must be enhanced by a factor, the so-called overdensity, of extreme magnitude ($η\gtrsim 10^{8}$).

</details>


### [242] [The Radio--X-ray Correlation of High-Redshift AGN: A Numerical Study of Inverse-Compton Scattering of the CMB Photons in Relativistic Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00721)
*Aditya Sharma,Bhargav Vaidya,Silvia Belladitta,Christian Fendt,Dharam V. Lal,Eduardo Bañados,Biman B. Nath,Harshita Bhuyan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过三维相对论磁流体力学模拟结合混合欧拉-拉格朗日粒子框架，研究了活动星系核喷流中逆康普顿散射宇宙微波背景光子的红移演化效应，成功再现了射电光度弱红移依赖和X射线辐射在高红移下的(1+z)^4增强，并统一解释了α-z关系等多波段观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核的相对论性喷流预期会因宇宙微波背景能量密度随红移增加而表现出显著的辐射红移演化，但此前缺乏系统的理论模拟研究来隔离CMB演化效应并定量解释观测到的射电与X射线红移依赖关系。

Method: 采用三维相对论磁流体力学模拟结合混合欧拉-拉格朗日粒子框架，固定喷流动力学和周围介质性质不变，仅改变CMB背景以隔离其演化效应；构建合成能谱分布和强度图，同时考虑同步辐射、IC/CMB损失和激波粒子加速。

Result: 成功再现了射电光度随红移的弱依赖性和X射线辐射在高红移下的(1+z)^4增强行为；X射线与射电流量比随红移系统增加并与观测一致；较慢喷流展现出更强的X射线红移演化；自然再现了射电谱指数随红移变陡的α-z关系。

Conclusion: IC/CMB是高红移下X射线增强的主导机制；喷流速度通过影响传播尺度与粒子能量演化调节红移演化强度；该模拟框架为统一解释高红移射电源的多波段性质提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Relativistic jets from active galactic nuclei are expected to exhibit strong redshift evolution in their radiative output due to the increasing energy density of the cosmic microwave background (CMB). We investigate the role of inverse Compton (IC) scattering of CMB photons in regulating the radio and X-ray emission from large-scale jets using three-dimensional relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations coupled with a hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian particle framework. By keeping the jet dynamics and ambient medium properties fixed across redshifts, we are able to isolate the impact of the cosmological evolution of the CMB on the jet radiation. From our simulations, we construct synthetic spectral energy distributions and intensity maps considering synchrotron and IC/CMB losses along with particle acceleration from shocks. We are able to reproduce the weak redshift dependence of radio luminosity and the strong enhancement of X-ray emission toward high redshift that is observed in radio-loud quasars. At high redshift, the X-ray luminosity follows the expected $(1+z)^4$ scaling, confirming IC/CMB as the dominant mechanism driving the X-ray enhancement. The resulting X-ray-to-radio flux ratio increases systematically with redshift and is consistent with observational constraints. Finally, we show that slower jets exhibit a stronger redshift evolution of the X-ray enhancement than faster jets, highlighting the critical role of jet propagation length scales and particle energy evolution. The simulations also naturally reproduce the steepening of the radio spectral index with redshift - the $α$-$z$ relation - thus providing a unified framework that allows to interpret the multiwavelength properties of high-redshift radio sources.

</details>


### [243] [TeV gamma-ray spectral spikes produced by magnetic reconnection in blazar jets: the case of the 2014 high state of Markarian 501](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00790)
*João G. Giesbrecht Formiga Paiva,Elisabete M. de Gouveia Dal Pino,Juan C. Rodríguez-Ramírez,Ulisses Barres de Almeida,Gabriela B. Díaz-Cortés*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了2014年7月耀变体Markarian 501的多波段监测数据，发现了一个TeV伽马射线尖峰特征（3-4σ显著性）与X射线通量增强同时出现。作者提出双轻子辐射区模型（磁重联驱动）来解释这一现象：稳定区域产生常规谱成分，上游更小、更磁化的第二区域产生耀发，导致X射线通量增加和TeV尖峰。该模型与磁条纹喷流模型一致，并成功拟合了2014年高态的多波段SED数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Markarian 501在2014年7月多波段监测中展现出TeV伽马射线尖峰特征（3-4σ）与X射线通量增强同时出现的异常行为，传统单区SSC模型无法解释这一特征，因此需要探索新的物理机制来解释这种额外发射成分的起源。

Method: 提出双轻子辐射区模型：在喷流磁重联能量耗散框架下，稳定谱成分由最大磁耗散区域产生；第二个耀发区域位于喷流上游，更小、磁化更强、运动更慢，产生X射线通量增加和TeV尖峰。采用磁条纹喷流模型中的湍流诱导磁重联机制驱动喷流加速和非热辐射，对MJD 56855.91、56857.98（含TeV尖峰）、56858.98、56859.97四个时期的SED数据进行建模。

Result: 双区模型成功再现了TeV尖峰与X射线增强同时出现的关键特征，验证了磁重联驱动的双辐射区方案可以解释Markarian 501在2014年高态期间观测到的异常谱特征。

Conclusion: 该研究支持磁重联驱动的双区发射模型作为耀变体非热辐射多样性起源的有效机制，为理解耀变体喷流中的粒子加速和能量耗散过程提供了新视角。

Abstract: Multi-wavelength monitoring of the flaring blazar Markarian 501 during July 2014 revealed a TeV gamma-ray spike feature with 3-4$σ$ significance and coincident with a prominent enhancement in its X-ray flux. The appearance of this spectral feature strongly suggests the presence of an extra emission component in addition to the usual one-zone SSC scenario. Several possible explanations for the origin of this novel behavior have been discussed, including stochastic particle acceleration, magnetospheric vacuum gap, and pion decay. In this paper, we show that the TeV narrow feature, simultaneous with an increase in the X-ray flux, can be produced with two leptonic emission regions in a jet undergoing magnetic reconnection energy dissipation along its propagation axis. In this scenario, the stable spectral components are produced in the region of maximum magnetic dissipation. A second region produces a flare upstream in the jet in a slower, more magnetized, and much smaller region compared to the stable one, which is responsible for increasing the X-ray flux and producing the TeV spike. The macroscopic properties of these two emission regions are consistent with a magnetically striped jet model discussed in previous works, where the acceleration of the jet flow and its non-thermal emission is driven by turbulent-induced magnetic reconnection. We employ this jet-reconnection scenario to model the 2014 high state of the blazar Markarian 501, considering the sequence of SED datasets corresponding to MJD 56855.91, 56857.98, 56858.98, and 56859.97, with the second dataset being the one that exhibits the TeV spike.

</details>


### [244] [High-Synchrotron-Peaked BL Lacs as Multi-Messenger Sources: Connecting Ultra-High-Energy Cosmic Rays and Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00816)
*Luiz Augusto Stuani Pereira,Rita C. Anjos*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了高同步峰（HSP）BL Lac天体作为超高能宇宙射线（UHECR）和中微子候选源的研究现状。这类极端粒子加速器具有高同步峰频率（>10^15 Hz）、相对近的距离（z≥0.5）和干净的辐射环境，是加速能量>10^19 eV UHECR和产生>100 TeV中微子的理想候选体。然而，极端重子载荷（L_p/L_e~10^3-10^5）给能量预算带来压力，Auger成分测量更倾向于重核而非质子主导情景，UHECR到达方向近各向同性也难以与罕见束源协调。下一代设施将探测关键可观测量以确认或否定这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究HSP BL Lac天体是否确实是UHECR和中微子的主要源，评估现有观测证据的矛盾之处，并为下一代实验提供理论指导。

Method: 综合评述了HSP的物理特性、WISE红外选择方法（2WHSP和3HSP星表，约2000个源）、轻子同步自康普顿模型与强子模型的对比，以及多信使观测证据（包括IceCube-170922A与TXS 0506+056的关联、2014-2015年中微子 flare等）。

Result: 发现：（1）极端重子载荷（L_p/L_e~10^3-10^5）使能量预算紧张；（2）Auger成分测量不支持质子主导情景，更倾向于重核；（3）UHECR到达方向近各向同性难以与罕见束源协调。讨论了磁重联、结构喷流和占空比效应等可能解决方案。

Conclusion: 下一代设施（IceCube-Gen2、KM3NeT、CTAO、IXPE、AugerPrime/TA×4）将提供关键观测数据，以确定HSP BL Lac天体是否为最高能宇宙线粒子的来源，或将搜索方向转向其他加速器类别。

Abstract: High-synchrotron-peaked (HSP) BL Lac objects are extreme particle accelerators whose synchrotron emission peaks at high frequencies, typically in the UV-to-X-ray band ($ν_{\rm peak} > 10^{15}$ Hz; $ν_{\rm peak} \geq 10^{17}$ for EHSPs), implying electron Lorentz factors of order $10^5-10^6$. Their relative proximity ($z \geq 0.5$), clean radiation environments, and favorable Hillas parameters make them prime candidates for ultra-high-energy cosmic ray (UHECR) acceleration beyond $10^{19}$ eV and for neutrino production above 100 TeV. The 2017 association of IceCube-170922A with the flaring blazar TXS 0506+056 provided compelling evidence for blazars as neutrino sources, while an archival neutrino flare from 2014-2015 with no clear electromagnetic counterpart (13 events) revealed additional complexity in the emission mechanism. This review examines HSP physical properties, identifies them through WISE-based infrared selection (the 2WHSP and 3HSP catalogs, approximately 2000 sources), and contrasts leptonic synchrotron self-Compton models with hadronic alternatives. We assess the observational evidence linking HSPs to high-energy neutrinos and UHECRs, finding that extreme baryonic loading ($L_p/L_e \sim 10^3-10^5$) strains energetic budgets, Auger composition measurements favor heavy nuclei over proton-dominated scenarios, and the near-isotropy of UHECR arrival directions is difficult to reconcile with rare beamed sources. Potential resolutions involving magnetic reconnection, structured jets, and duty cycle effects are discussed. Next-generation facilities, including IceCube-Gen2, KM3NeT, CTAO, IXPE, and AugerPrime/TA x 4, will probe key observables to either establish HSP BL Lacs as sources of the highest-energy cosmic particles or redirect the search toward alternative accelerator classes.

</details>


### [245] [Enhancing the Angular Resolution of Large Array of imaging atmospheric Cherenkov Telescope (LACT) at Ultra-High Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01059)
*Zhipeng Zhang,Ruizhi Yang,Shoushan Zhang,Jiali Liu,Liqiao Yin,Wedong Wang,Lingling Ma,Zhen Cao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文针对LACT阵列的立体方向重建问题展开研究，发现标准Hillas参数化在严重图像泄漏下存在显著重建偏差；提出2D高斯拟合法在100 TeV中心区域实现了优于0.06°的角分辨率，并利用LightGBM分位数回归模型实现基于质量加权的一致改进（0.02°–0.03°），最后通过神经比率估计的像素级似然重建探索了15%–40%的性能上限潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LACT阵列致力于PeVatron的高分辨率形态学研究，但在超高能观测中，标准Hillas参数化方法因图像泄漏导致严重的重建偏差，亟需发展新的方向重建方法来提升角分辨率，以解析PeVatron的复杂辐射结构。

Method: 1) 提出2D高斯拟合替代标准Hillas参数化以缓解图像泄漏偏差；2) 利用LightGBM分位数回归模型独立估计单图像质量，并基于质量权重应用HillasWeightedSum和HillasWeightedDisp方法；3) 探索基于神经比率估计的像素级似然重建方法以建立理论性能上限。

Result: 2D高斯拟合法在100 TeV中心偏移区间（0°–1°）实现优于0.06°的角分辨率，在偏移至4°时仍保持优于0.12°；质量加权方案在高能大偏移事件中带来0.02°–0.03°的一致改善；神经比率估计方法在100 TeV全视场内可望实现15%–40%的整体提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的方向重建方法显著优于标准Hillas方法，实现了超高能伽马天文所需的高角分辨率，为解构PeVatron复杂发射区及内部结构测绘提供了关键技术支撑；像素级似然方法虽依赖模拟与观测数据的高质量对齐，但展现了可观的理论性能潜力。

Abstract: The Large Array of Imaging Atmospheric Cherenkov Telescopes (LACT) is dedicated to high-resolution morphological studies of PeVatrons. In this work, we present a fundamental investigation into stereoscopic direction reconstruction for the LACT array, specifically addressing the challenges of ultra-high-energy observations. We demonstrate that the standard Hillas parameterization introduces a significant reconstruction bias under severe image leakage. To mitigate this, we introduce an approach utilizing a 2D Gaussian fit, achieving an exceptional angular resolution of better than $0.06^\circ$ at $100\text{ TeV}$ within the central $0^\circ\text{--}1^\circ$ offset bin, and maintaining better than $0.12^\circ$ across offsets up to $4^{\circ}$. Building on this robust baseline, we evaluate advanced weighting schemes by utilizing a LightGBM-based quantile regression model to independently estimate single-image quality. Applying these quality-based weights yields a consistent improvement of $0.02^\circ$ to $0.03^\circ$ for high-energy, large-offset events using both the \textit{HillasWeightedSum} and \textit{HillasWeightedDisp} methods. Finally, to establish a theoretical performance ceiling, we explore a pixel-wise likelihood reconstruction technique utilizing Neural Ratio Estimation. While its practical realization depends heavily on minimizing the gap between Monte Carlo simulations and observational data, this exploratory approach demonstrates the potential to yield an overall improvement of approximately 15\% to 40\% at $100~\rm TeV$ across the entire field of view. Such high angular resolution is critical for disentangling complex emission regions and mapping the internal structures of PeVatrons.

</details>


### [246] [Hunting for Compact Object Binaries from eRASS1 Optical Counterparts through ZTF Time-domain Photometry and Multi-wavelength Census](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01085)
*Xin-Yu Fang,Hao-Bin Liu,Wei-Min Gu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用eRASS1光学对应体星表，结合ZTF时域测光和多波段观测，系统普查致密天体双星候选体（重点X射线双星），通过两条互补管道分别识别出151个周期变源（含43个高优先级候选体）和1958个距离约束源，最终通过射电交叉匹配发现7个射电发射源和4个有希望的XRB候选体，证明eROSITA结合宽场时域测光和多波段巡天是发现隐藏COB群体的高效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统普查致密天体双星候选体，特别是X射线双星，以发现隐藏的COB群体。

Method: 利用eRASS1光学对应体星表，整合ZTF时域测光和多波段观测，建立两条互补管道：（1）基于周期变源筛选出151个源，进一步提炼43个高优先级候选体；（2）基于高X射线光度或高log(F_X/F_opt)选出1958个距离约束源。最后与射电星表交叉匹配。

Result: 两条管道分别得到151个周期变源（含43个高优先级COB候选体）和1958个距离约束源；射电交叉匹配发现7个射电发射源，其中4个为有希望的XRB候选体。

Conclusion: eROSITA结合宽场时域测光和多波段巡天是高效发现隐藏致密天体双星群体的策略。

Abstract: Capitalizing on the eRASS1 optical counterpart catalog, we conduct a systematic census of compact object binary (COB) candidates, with a primary focus on X-ray binaries (XRBs), by integrating ZTF time-domain photometry with multi-wavelength observations. This framework establishes two complementary pipelines, yielding two distinct source samples. The first sample consists of 151 periodically variable sources, from which a highly refined subset of 43 high-priority COB candidates is identified. The second sample comprises 1958 distance-constrained sources selected based on elevated X-ray luminosities or high $\log (F_{\mathrm{X}}/F_{\mathrm{opt}})$. Crucially, cross-matching both samples with radio catalogs reveals seven radio-emitting sources, highlighting four promising XRB candidates. Our results underscore that coupling eROSITA with wide-field time-domain photometric and multi-wavelength surveys offers a highly efficient strategy for uncovering the hidden population of COBs.

</details>


### [247] [Photon Escape from Slab Thomson Media: A Scattering-order-resolved Recursive Formalism for Comptonization Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01087)
*Haichao Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文针对平板介质中光子的散射历史，提出了一种递归形式体系，用于计算各散射阶数的边界与角度分辨逃逸概率，并验证了其与蒙特卡洛模拟的一致性。该体系为半解析康普顿化光谱、康普顿放大因子及向下散射光度分数提供了光子数守恒的计算路径，并导出了不同注入条件下的平均散射次数精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立高效的半解析框架，用于建模平板介质中光子散射历史，从而改进康普顿化光谱与盘-冕辐射反馈的计算效率与物理理解。

Method: 发展递归形式体系，演化平板汤姆孙介质中散射后的深度-方向分布，得到各阶边界与角度分辨逃逸概率；利用两分量基闭合角依赖，将传输问题约化为深度核递归；对垂直入射束、朗伯律边界注入及均匀各向同性内源三种情形求解，并与蒙特卡洛模拟对比验证。

Result: 得到光子数守恒的半解析康普顿化光谱、康普顿放大因子与向下散射光度分数；导出朗伯注入下平均散射次数⟨N⟩=2τ的精确结果；均匀内源从光学薄τ ln(1/τ)行为过渡到光学厚τ²/4标度；高散射阶由主特征模控制，归一化角分布趋于极限。

Conclusion: 该递归框架为未来能量依赖的平板康普顿化模型提供了关键的输运要素（特征值与本征函数），高阶散射的视角与逃逸边界主要影响X射线分量归一化，光谱形状差异集中于低阶与未散射成分。

Abstract: The scattering history of photons in slab media plays an important role in modelling Comptonized spectra and disc-corona radiative feedback. We develop a recursive formalism that evolves the post-scattering depth--direction distribution in slab Thomson media and yields boundary- and angle-resolved escape probabilities at each scattering order. For azimuth-integrated problems, the angular dependence closes within a two-component basis, reducing the transport problem to an efficient depth-kernel recursion. We apply the method to normally incident beam injection, Lambert-law boundary injection, and a vertically uniform isotropic internal source, and verify the results with Monte Carlo radiative-transfer simulations. The resulting distributions provide a photon-number-conserving route to semi-analytic Comptonized spectra and estimates of the Compton amplification factor and the fraction of downwardly scattered luminosity. We also derive the mean scattering number within this framework, obtaining the exact result $\langle N\rangle=2τ$ for Lambert-law injection, while the uniform internal source changes from an optically thin $τ\ln(1/τ)$ behaviour to an optically thick $τ^2/4$ scaling. At high scattering orders, the recursion is controlled by a dominant eigenmode: $P_n/P_{n-1}\rightarrowλ(τ)$, where $λ(τ)$ is the spectral radius of the slab recursion operator. This eigenmode also determines a limiting normalized angular distribution, so that viewing angle and escape boundary primarily affect the normalization of the high-order X-ray component, while spectral-shape differences are mainly confined to the unscattered and low-order components. These eigenvalue and eigenfunction results provide transport ingredients for future energy-dependent slab Comptonization models.

</details>


### [248] [Deep Optical Follow-up Observations to IceCube Cosmic Neutrinos: a case for IC230724A with Subaru/HSC and prospects with Rubin/LSST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01158)
*Shigeo S. Kimura,Masaomi Tanaka,Seiji Toshikage,Tomoki Morokuma,Nobuhiro Shimizu,Nozomu Tominaga,Naoki Yasuda,Yousuke Utsumi,Michitoshi Yoshida,Yasushi Fukazawa,Koji S. Kawabata*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用Subaru/HSC对冰立方中微子事件IceCube 230724A进行了深度光学后随观测（极限星等25.5等），在盲分析框架下未发现潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）候选体，并预测Rubin/LSST对10个中微子事件的观测将能约束TDE对宇宙高能中微子背景的贡献比例（非探测时≤60%，探测时≥30%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰立方探测宇宙高能中微子已超十年，但其主要来源仍存争议，亟需通过中微子警报进行电磁后随观测以鉴定源。

Method: 对IceCube 230724A事件进行Subaru/HSC深度光学观测（极限星等25.5等），采用盲分析策略，系统评估背景率和真阳性率，在感兴趣区域搜索TDE候选体。

Result: 未在感兴趣区域内发现TDE候选体。

Conclusion: Rubin/LSST若覆盖10个中微子事件的误差区域，非探测时可约束TDE贡献≤60%，探测时可约束其贡献≥30%。

Abstract: IceCube has been detecting cosmic high-energy neutrinos for more than 10 years, but their major sources are still under debate. To identify them, IceCube is issuing neutrino alerts, which enable us to perform electromagnetic follow-up observations. In this paper, we present our Subaru/HSC deep optical follow-up observations down to 25.5 mag to a well-localized neutrino event, IceCube 230724A. We conduct a dedicated analysis with extensive evaluation of background rates and true positive rates adopting the blind analysis policy to identify or disfavor tidal disruption events (TDEs) as cosmic neutrino sources. Our analysis found no TDE candidate in the region of interest. Rubin/LSST survey will enable us to constrain their fractional contribution to the cosmic high-energy neutrino background, either $\lesssim 60\%$ or $\gtrsim30\%$ for non-detection and detection, respectively, if Rubin covers the error regions of 10 neutrino events.

</details>


### [249] [Rapid quasi-periodic reconfiguration of the accretion column in pulsar 1A 0535+262](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01625)
*Lingda Kong,Xiaohang Dai,Andrea Santangelo,Long Ji,Valery F. Suleimanov,Alexander A. Mushtukov,Lorenzo Ducci,Shu Zhang,Qingcang Shui,Shuang-Nan Zhang,Hua Feng,Sergey S. Tsygankov,Honghui Liu,Pengju Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对X射线脉冲星1A 0535+262巨爆发的观测，发现准周期性硬X射线通量变化伴随着回旋吸收线能量的同步振荡，且振幅超过简单吸积率波动的预期。回旋能量与视通量之间的反相关提供了线形成区域快速变化的直接谱-时证据，这被解释为吸积柱的几何重构。这种变化出现在辐射压变得动力学重要的光度区间，揭示了准稳态模型在该源中的局限性，表明辐射支持的吸积柱在高光度吸积脉冲星中可进入内在动力学状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁化中子星的吸积过程通常使用准稳态模型解释，但该模型在短动力学时标上的有效性一直不确定。本文旨在利用回旋吸收线能量作为磁场强度的诊断工具，检验准稳态描述是否在快速变化的时间尺度上仍然成立。

Method: 观测X射线脉冲星1A 0535+262的巨爆发事件，分析准周期性硬X射线通量变化与回旋吸收线能量振荡之间的同步关系，并通过谱-时分析研究两者的反相关性。

Result: 发现准周期性硬X射线通量变化与回旋线能量振荡同步，振幅超出简单吸积率波动的预期；回旋能量与视通量呈现反相关关系；该现象出现在辐射压变得动力学重要的高光度区间。

Conclusion: 简单的准稳态吸积模型不能完全解释该源在短时标上的行为，高光度下辐射支持的吸积柱可进入内在动力学状态，导致吸积柱几何结构的快速重构。

Abstract: Accretion onto strongly magnetized neutron stars is commonly interpreted using quasi-steady models, in which the accretion-column structure adjusts smoothly to the mass inflow rate. The cyclotron line in the X-ray spectrum, whose centroid energy traces the magnetic field strength and thus the height of the line-forming region, provides a key diagnostic of this structure. Whether this simple quasi-steady description remains valid on short dynamical timescales has remained uncertain. Here we show that, during a giant outburst of the X-ray pulsar 1A~0535+262, quasi-periodic hard X-ray flux variations are accompanied by synchronized oscillations of the cyclotron line energy, with amplitudes exceeding those expected from simple accretion-rate fluctuations. The anti-correlation between cyclotron energy and apparent flux provides direct spectral-timing evidence for rapid changes in the line-forming region, which we interpret as geometric reconfiguration of the accretion column. The variability emerges in the luminosity regime where radiation pressure becomes dynamically important. These results reveal limitations of a simple quasi-steady interpretation for this source and suggest that radiation-supported columns can enter intrinsically dynamical states in high-luminosity accreting pulsars.

</details>


### [250] [Reconnection-Driven Injection and Stochastic Reacceleration during Cosmological Magnetogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01775)
*Ji-Hoon Ha*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究宇宙磁化过程中磁重联能否提供超热质子种子粒子。研究发现磁重联可作为快速注入通道产生超热尾，但非热能量分数极小（约10⁻⁷），对星系际介质非热压贡献可忽略；不过重联产生的尾部可将后续激波加速的种子质子种群提升约一个数量级，但相关伽玛射线辐射仍远低于可探测阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非线性结构形成前的宇宙磁化过程中，磁重联是否为超热质子种子粒子提供来源，以及其对宇宙射线能量预算的贡献潜力。

Method: 在原有各向异性压力驱动随机加速框架（Fokker-Planck方程）基础上，添加唯象的重联驱动源项，注入功率与磁化过程中磁能增长率耦合，并通过测试粒子激波再加速估计评估后续影响。

Result: 磁重联可产生可见超热尾，但非热能量分数仅约10⁻⁷；重联尾部可将激波种子质子种群提升约一个数量级；相关强子伽玛射线辐射远低于当前可探测性阈值。

Conclusion: 宇宙磁化过程中的磁重联不太可能直接主导宇宙射线能量预算，但可为后续激波加速提供低水平种子粒子种群。

Abstract: We investigate whether magnetic reconnection can provide suprathermal proton seed particles during cosmological magnetogenesis prior to nonlinear structure formation. Previous work showed that pressure-anisotropy-driven stochastic acceleration alone is strongly limited by cosmological expansion and Coulomb cooling. Here, we extend this framework by adding a phenomenological reconnection-driven source term to the Fokker--Planck equation for the isotropic ion distribution, with the injection power tied to the magnetic-energy growth rate during magnetogenesis. We find that reconnection can act as a fast injection channel and can produce a visible suprathermal tail. However, the resulting nonthermal energy fraction remains very small, of order $10^{-7}$ in the fiducial model, implying a negligible nonthermal pressure contribution to the pre-structure intergalactic medium. This limitation arises because the extremely high-beta plasma contains only a small magnetic-energy reservoir, even when reconnection itself is locally fast. Using a test-particle shock reacceleration estimate, we further show that the reconnection-produced tail can enhance the suprathermal proton population available for later structure-formation shocks by about an order of magnitude. Nevertheless, the associated hadronic gamma-ray emission from low-density cluster outskirts is expected to remain far below current detectability. We therefore conclude that reconnection during cosmological magnetogenesis is unlikely to dominate the cosmic-ray energy budget directly, but may provide a low-level seed population for subsequent shock acceleration.

</details>


### [251] [A new Chandra look at the globular cluster NGC 6540 and its peculiar X-ray flaring source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01792)
*A. Sacchi,S. Mereghetti,R. Di Stefano,J. A. Irwin,M. Rigoselli,A. De Luca,N. Sims*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用深度的 Chandra 观测（≈65 ks）对球状星团 NGC 6540 中的奇特 X 射线源 3XMM J180608.9−274553 进行研究，发现该源附近有三个微弱 X 射线源，并通过光学对应体和亚角秒定位讨论了它们的性质及与 X 射线耀发的可能关联，同时对微引力透镜放大、黑洞耀发等解释场景进行了分析，最终为理解这一罕见暂现源提供了新约束，展示了高分辨率 X 射线观测在致密恒星环境中区分稀有现象的重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该 X 射线源曾由 XMM-Newton 观测到一次短时（≈300 秒）且强烈的 X 射线耀发，其光度和持续时间与典型的低质量 X 射线双星的 I 型 X 射线暴及恒星耀发均不符，因此其本质不明，需要深入探究。

Method: 采用深度 Chandra 观测（≈65 ks）获取高分辨率的 X 射线图像，结合亚角秒精度的定位和光学对应体分析，并与之前较短的 Chandra 观测及 XMM-Newton 数据进行对比。

Result: 在 XMM-Newton 探测到的耀发位置附近发现三个微弱 X 射线源，其中仅有一个在之前的短时 Chandra 观测中被探测到；基于这些源的性质和光学对应体，讨论了它们与 3XMM J180608.9−274553 的可能关联。

Conclusion: 该研究对解释 X 射线耀发的可能机制（如微引力透镜放大、类似于 Sgr A* 的黑洞耀发活动等）进行了讨论，为这一不寻常暂现源的本质提供了新的约束，并强调了高分辨率 X 射线观测在球状星团致密环境中的辨别力。

Abstract: We report the results of a deep ($\approx65$ ks) Chandra observation of the globular cluster NGC 6540, obtained to investigate the nature of the peculiar X-ray source 3XMM J180608.9$-$274553. This source was previously observed with XMM$-$Newton to exhibit a short ($\approx300$ s) and intense X-ray flare whose luminosity and duration are inconsistent with both typical type I X-ray bursts from low mass X-ray binaries and stellar flares. Our new data show the presence of three faint X-ray sources near the position of the flare seen by XMM$-$Newton, only one of which was detected in a previous, much shorter Chandra observation. Based on the properties of these sources, localized at sub-arcsecond precision, and of their optical counterparts, we discuss their possible nature and association with 3XMM J180608.9$-$274553. We also discuss some scenarios to explain the X-ray flare, such as microlensing-induced amplification, black hole flaring activity analogous to that observed from Sgr A$^\ast$. Our results place new constraints on the nature of this unusual transient and highlight the power of high-resolution X-ray observations for disentangling rare phenomena in the dense stellar environments of globular clusters.

</details>


### [252] [A 0.03 Hz Radio Quasi-periodic Oscillation During the 2025 Flare of GRS 1915+105](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01823)
*Ya Xing Li,Lei Liu,Wu Jiang,Zhen Yan,Bo Xia,Zhi Qiang Shen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了微类星体GRS 1915+105在2025年发生的一次明亮射电耀发，通过上海两台射电望远镜组成的短基线在S波段（2.25 GHz）和X波段（8.42 GHz）同时观测。高时间分辨率分析在两个连续观测日（MJD 60765：>5.9σ；MJD 60772：2.8σ）的两个射电波段中均探测到显著的短寿命准周期振荡（QPO），频率约0.03 Hz及其谐波约0.06 Hz。两个波段的QPO频率相同，且与往年探测到的振荡频率一致。该QPO频率的复现性和波长独立性表明其源于吸积-喷流系统的固有特征时标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微类星体GRS 1915+105已知表现出多种时变现象，但其射电波段的准周期振荡特性及其与吸积-喷流耦合的关系尚不完全清楚。本文旨在通过高时间分辨率的多波段同时观测，探测并确认该源射电QPO的存在及其物理起源。

Method: 利用上海两台射电望远镜组成的短基线，以每周为周期的射电监测，在S波段（2.25 GHz）和X波段（8.42 GHz）同时对GRS 1915+105进行观测，并对数据进行高时间分辨率分析以检测QPO信号。

Result: 在2025年的一次明亮耀发中，于MJD 60765（>5.9σ）和MJD 60772（2.8σ）两个连续观测日，在两个射电波段同时探测到约0.03 Hz的QPO及其约0.06 Hz的谐波。QPO频率在两个波段中完全相同，且与历史观测结果一致。

Conclusion: 该QPO频率的复现性和波长独立性表明其反映了吸积-喷流系统的固有特征时标，为理解微类星体的内部物理过程提供了重要约束。

Abstract: Our weekly-cadence radio monitoring campaign captured a bright flare in 2025 from the microquasar GRS 1915+105, observed simultaneously in the S- and X-bands (2.25 GHz and 8.42 GHz) with a short single baseline of two radio telescopes in Shanghai. Through high time resolution analysis, we detected a significant and short-lived quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) at $\sim$0.03 Hz and its harmonic ($\sim$0.06 Hz) in both radio bands of two consecutive observations on MJD 60765 ($>5.9 σ$) and MJD 60772 (2.8$σ$). Crucially, the QPO frequency is identical in both radio bands and matches oscillations detected in previous years. The recurrence and wavelength independence of the QPO frequency suggest an intrinsic characteristic timescale of the accretion-jet system.

</details>


### [253] [Possible High-Energy Neutrino Emission from Dark Matter Annihilation in the Disrupting Dwarf Galaxy Boötes~III](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01853)
*Shunhao Ji,Zhongxiang Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次利用IceCube十年中微子数据搜寻恒星流核中暗物质湮灭产生的高能中微子信号，在Boötes~III（Styx流的核）位置发现3.1σ的超出（最佳暗物质质量26.5 TeV），并给出了J因子下限，这是首个可能与暗物质湮灭相关的高能中微子候选目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期伽马射线研究将恒星流核提出为一类新型间接暗物质探测目标的启发，本文旨在探索这些天体是否可产生可探测的高能中微子信号，以拓展暗物质搜寻的渠道。

Method: 利用IceCube公开的十年有迹中微子数据，分析北半球三个恒星流核（以Boötes~III为代表）的暗物质湮灭信号，采用$\chi\chi\to\nu\bar\nu$湮灭通道假设，通过似然比方法进行统计推断。

Result: 在Boötes~III位置发现3.1σ后验显著性超出，最佳拟合暗物质质量为26.5 TeV；给出J因子下限$\log_{10}(J_{\rm ann}/{\rm GeV^2\,cm^{-5}})\gtrsim 19.1^{+0.3}_{-0.5}$，与经验估计一致。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了首个可能与暗物质湮灭相关的高能中微子候选目标，但需未来大型中微子探测器（如IceCube升级版）进一步确认，以探测暗物质粒子性质。

Abstract: We report the first search for high-energy neutrino emissions from dark matter (DM) annihilation in stellar-stream cores. Motivated by a recent gamma-ray study that proposed these cores as a new class of indirect DM targets, we analyze three stream cores in the Northern Hemisphere using the public ten-year track-like neutrino data released by IceCube. Under the $χχ\toν\barν$ annihilation hypothesis, the most significant excess among the three targets is found at the position of the nearby dwarf galaxy Boötes~III, the core of the Styx stream, with a best-fit DM mass of 26.5\,TeV. The excess has a post-trial significance of $3.1σ$. Considering the existing IceCube dwarf-galaxy limit for the same channel, we obtain a limit on the J-factor $J_{\rm ann}$, $\log_{10}(J_{\rm ann}/{\rm GeV^2\,cm^{-5}})\gtrsim 19.1^{+0.3}_{-0.5}$. This limit is broadly consistent with empirical estimates of $J_{\rm ann}$ for Boötes~III. The results provide the first candidate target with a possible HE neutrino signal associated with DM annihilation. This neutrino excess and the general existence of DM-induced neutrino signals from other similar sources will be confirmed with the near-future large high-energy neutrino detectors, thus enabling us to probe the nature of DM particles.

</details>


### [254] [Fast radio bursts, magnetars and earthquakes: their "family feud"?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01855)
*Si-Lu Xu,Yong-Kun Zhang,Pei Wang,Di Li,Jun-Shuo Zhang,Tian-Cheng Lv,Yong-Feng Huang,Tian-Cong Wang,Long-Xuan Zhang,Pei-Xin Zhu,Jin-Huang Cao,Yi Feng,He Gao,Jian Li,Wan-Jin Lu,Chen-Chen Miao,Chen-Hui Niu,Qing-Yue Qu,Chao-Wei Tsai,Yi-Dan Wang,Wen-Ting Wang,Su-Ming Weng,Jia-Fu Wu,Ru-Shuang Zhao,Yuan-Chuan Zou,Yu-Hao Zhu,Ya-Biao Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究提出Pincus-Lyapunov图（PLD）作为新诊断工具，将重复快速射电暴（FRB）与磁星耀斑、脉冲星自转突变、太阳耀斑和地震的爆发序列映射到随机-混沌相空间。结果显示FRB占据独特区域，统计检验表明其与磁星耀斑、脉冲星自转突变分布存在显著差异（p≈0.05），且最活跃FRB 20240114A的时序特征在八个月观测期内保持稳定，表明重复FRB的触发机制可能不同于其他天体物理瞬变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速射电暴（FRB）的起源仍不清楚，存在类地震过程与类耀斑机制两种竞争模型。为区分这两种场景，需要开发新的诊断方法对FRB与已知瞬变现象（磁星耀斑、脉冲星自转突变、太阳耀斑、地震）进行系统比较。

Method: 开发Pincus-Lyapunov图（PLD），将五个代表性重复FRB（FRB 20121102A、FRB 20190520B、FRB 20201124A、FRB 20220912A、FRB 20240114A）及磁星耀斑、脉冲星自转突变、太阳耀斑、地震的爆发序列映射到随机-混沌相空间。采用置换检验评估分布差异，使用增广迪基-富勒检验分析最活跃FRB的时间稳定性。

Result: FRB在PLD相空间中占据独特区域；置换检验显示FRB与磁星耀斑、脉冲星自转突变分布存在统计显著差异（p≈0.05）；FRB 20240114A的Pincus指数和Lyapunov指数在八个月内统计稳定（ADF检验p值分别为1.78×10⁻³和9.91×10⁻³）。

Conclusion: 重复快速射电暴的触发机制很可能不同于磁星耀斑、脉冲星自转突变、太阳耀斑和地震的驱动机制。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are millisecond-duration cosmic transients whose origin remains elusive. Competing models invoke either earthquake-like processes or flare-like mechanisms. To discriminate between these scenarios, we develop a novel diagnostic, the Pincus-Lyapunov diagram (PLD), to characterize the energetic transients in the stochasticity-chaos phase space. We compile burst sequences from five representative FRBs (FRB 20121102A, FRB 20190520B, FRB 20201124A, FRB 20220912A, and FRB 20240114A), together with those from magnetar flares (SGR J1550$-$5418, SGR J0501+4516, SGR 1806$-$20, SGR 1900+14, and SGR J1935+2154), pulsar glitches, solar flares, and earthquakes, and map them onto the PLD for comparative analysis. The resulting diagram shows that FRBs occupy a distinct region of the phase space. Specifically, a permutation test reveals a statistically significant difference in the distributions of magnetar flares and pulsar glitches compared to those of repeating FRBs ($p$-value $\simeq 0.05$). To examine whether temporal variations in source activity can shift a repeater's position in this phase space, we analyze the time evolution of the most prolific repeater, FRB~20240114A. For this repeating FRB, both Pincus Index and Lyapunov Exponent demonstrate statistically stable behaviour over the eight-month observation session, with Augmented Dickey--Fuller tests yielding $p \simeq 1.78\times10^{-3}$ and $9.91\times10^{-3}$, respectively. By assembling the most comprehensive dataset to date, our work indicates that the trigger mechanisms of repeating FRBs are likely to be distinct from those driving magnetar flares, pulsar glitches, solar flares, and earthquakes.

</details>


### [255] [Mass and Spin Growth of Very Massive Stars in Star Clusters Potentially Associated with Little Red Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01870)
*Ataru Tanikawa,Masaru Shibata,Kunihito Ioka*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过N体模拟研究致密星团中极高质量恒星（VMS）的质量与自旋演化，发现VMS质量可达10³–10⁴ M☉，且考虑Hayashi轨道的膨胀状态后质量可增加三倍；所有VMS的自旋均远超典型黑洞自旋参数（>10）。这类高速自旋VMS预计坍缩为中等质量黑洞+大质量吸积盘系统，可能产生GW190521和GW231123类型的爆发性引力波事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索致密星团中极高质量恒星（VMS）的质量增长极限与自旋演化，并评估其与“小红点”（LRDs）及中等质量黑洞形成的潜在关联，为解释GW190521等引力波事件提供可能的前身天体模型。

Method: 采用引力N体数值模拟，追踪致密星团中VMS通过恒星碰撞与吸积的质量累积过程，并对比考虑与不考虑Hayashi轨道膨胀状态（临界吸积率>3×10⁻² M☉ yr⁻¹）两种情况下的质量增长差异；同时计算VMS归一化自旋参数。

Result: VMS最终质量可达10³–10⁴ M☉；考虑Hayashi轨道膨胀后质量增幅可达三倍；所有模拟中VMS归一化自旋参数均>10；模型可能因未包含主序后演化和碰撞中的质量/角动量损失而高估数值。

Conclusion: 致密星团中形成的VMS可以拥有极高的自旋速率，其坍缩后形成的中等质量黑洞-吸积盘系统能够产生类似于GW190521和GW231123的爆发性引力波信号，为理解≥100 M☉黑洞的形成机制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Using gravitational $N$-body simulations, we investigate the evolution of mass and spin for very massive stars (VMSs) in dense star clusters, which may be potentially associated with Little Red Dots (LRDs). Our results show that VMS masses can reach $10^3$--$10^4\,M_\odot$, depending on the initial conditions of the host clusters. Notably, the VMS mass increases by up to a factor of three when accounting for the bloated state at the Hayashi track induced by stellar collisions, provided that this state is maintained at accretion rates exceeding $3 \times 10^{-2}\,M_\odot\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$. In all cases, the spin of the VMS, when normalized to the dimensionless black hole (BH) spin parameter, exceeds $10$. While our model may overestimate VMS masses and spins due to the omission of post-main-sequence evolution and the loss of mass and angular momentum during collisions, we nonetheless demonstrate that VMSs formed in dense star clusters can be highly spinning. Such a rapidly spinning VMS is expected to collapse into an intermediate-mass BH surrounded by a massive accretion disk. This BH-disk system could trigger powerful explosions and emit burst gravitational waves, similar to those observed in GW190521 and GW231123, for which the remnant BH masses are estimated to be $\gtrsim 100\,M_\odot$.

</details>


### [256] [Locating the Production Sites of High-Energy Neutrinos in Blazar Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02024)
*Rui Xue,Yoshiyuki Inoue,Ze-Rui Wang,Neng-Hui Liao,Dingrong Xiong*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合射电约束的喷流特性与多区发射模型，揭示了耀变体中微子有效产生需要喷流参照系中外部辐射场强于磁场，且中微子发射区需与主导电磁辐射区物理分离，解释了耀变体-中微子关联罕见的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耀变体喷流中高能中微子的产生位点一直难以确定，现有观测无法有效约束中微子产生的具体物理环境。

Method: 结合射电观测约束的喷流物理参数与多区辐射区发射模型，分析中微子产生所需的物理条件，并对比观测数据。

Result: 中微子高效产生需要外部辐射场强于喷流参照系中的磁场；单区发射模型与观测的双峰流量比不一致，表明中微子发射区需与电磁辐射主导区物理分离；这种分离可通过喷流在亚秒差距尺度内完成加速或具有极大体洛伦兹因子实现，但这两种情形在观测中均不常见。

Conclusion: 研究结果自然解释了耀变体-中微子关联的罕见性，强调约束小尺度喷流结构对于识别有希望的中微子发射耀变体的重要性。

Abstract: The production sites of high-energy neutrinos in blazar jets remain poorly constrained. In this work, we investigate the physical conditions required for efficient neutrino production by combining radio-constrained jet properties with multi-zone emission modeling. We show that efficient neutrino production requires an external radiation field stronger than the magnetic field in the jet frame. This environment not only enhances the efficiency of photohadronic interactions but also suppresses synchrotron radiation from secondary pairs, thereby avoiding overshooting the hard X-ray data. Such conditions can be achieved in regions near or within the broad-line region. However, assuming a single emission zone, these conditions are generally inconsistent with the double-bump flux ratio of the observed broadband emission. This implies that the neutrino-emitting region should be physically separated from the dominant electromagnetic emission zone. We further show that such a scenario can be realized either if the jet completes its acceleration within sub-parsec scales or if the bulk Lorentz factor is intrinsically large, both of which appear uncommon based on current observations. These results provide a natural explanation for the rarity of blazar-neutrino associations and highlight the importance of constraining jet structure at small scales to identify promising neutrino-emitting blazars.

</details>


### [257] [Revealing the high redshift host galaxy of the short GRB 061201 with JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02032)
*Yuhan Mao,Hanrui He,Jia Ren,Yun Wang,Hao Zhou,Qiuli Wang,Yiming Zhu,Zhiping Jin,Daming Wei*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用JWST和HST的深度近红外/光学图像，本文为GRB 061201识别了一个新的候选宿主星系（红移z~1.2），通过三条独立证据（能量关系、千新星限制、余辉建模）排除了之前提出的z=0.111低红移宿主，认为该伽马暴起源于中等高红移宿主，并显著缓解了并合率与引力波约束之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决GRB 061201长期存在的宿主星系争议——此前提出的低红移（z=0.111）宿主存在物理偏移过大、能量关系异常、并合率过高等问题，亟需利用JWST深度观测重新识别其真实宿主，以正确理解短伽马暴的物理性质和宇宙并合率。

Method: 利用JWST和HST深度近红外/光学图像识别距光学余辉~2角秒的新候选宿主；通过测光红移拟合确定z~1.2；计算随机重合概率评估可靠性；从能量关系（Ghirlanda/Amati关系）、千新星约束（与AT2017gfo对比）、余辉建模（AIC准则）三条独立线索检验高低红移方案；计算物理偏移、恒星年龄和并合率进行一致性验证。

Result: 新候选宿主随机重合概率P_cc=0.18（略高于经典阈值但可接受），原低红移宿主P_cc=0.11（由亮尾统计驱动）；z=1.2方案与Amati关系一致，z=0.111方案在Ghirlanda关系中为异常值；深度近红外观测排除了类似AT2017gfo的千新星；余辉建模ΔAIC=16.35强支持高红移；物理偏移16.4-16.9 kpc，恒星年龄~2 Gyr，与短暴宿主群体一致；低红移方案会导致~1400 Gpc⁻³ yr⁻¹的极高并合率，与引力波约束矛盾。

Conclusion: GRB 061201应起源于z~1.2的中等高红移宿主，该推论显著缓解了历史并合率矛盾；该工作展示了JWST深度曝光在揭示历史"无宿主"伽马暴宿主星系方面的独特能力。

Abstract: Using deep near-infrared and optical images from JWST and HST, we identify a new host galaxy candidate for GRB 061201. It lies ~2" from the optical afterglow position. Photometric redshift fitting yields z~1.2. We compare the previously proposed host at z=0.111 with the new candidate. The chance-coincidence probability is $P_{cc}=0.18$, above the classical threshold of 0.1 but consistent with a physical association given the extreme depth of JWST imaging. In contrast, evaluated with corresponding JWST observations, the previously claimed host has a lower $P_{cc}=0.11$, which is driven primarily by bright-tail statistics rather than a more plausible association. A high-z origin is favored by three independent lines of evidence. First, for the z=0.111 scenario, the beaming-corrected energy shows GRB 061201 is an outlier of the Ghirlanda ($E_{p,i}-E_γ$) relation for short GRBs, while for the z=1.2 scenario, it is well consistent with the Amati relation. Second, deep near-infrared observations rule out a kilonova similar to AT2017gfo at z=0.111. Third, afterglow modeling yields an AIC criterion of $Δ$AIC=16.35, providing strong evidence for the high-redshift scenario. Assuming the host candidate is the actual host galaxy of GRB 061201, the physical offset is 16.4-16.9 kpc (substantially reduced from ~42 kpc) and the host stellar age is ~2 Gyr, which are consistent with the host population of short GRBs. A low-redshift origin would lead to a very high binary neutron star merger rate of ~1400 Gpc$^{-3}$ yr$^{-1}$, which is contradictory to the gravitational-wave constraint. We suggest that GRB 061201 originates from a moderately high-redshift (z~1.2) host, significantly alleviating this apparent merger rate discrepancy. This case demonstrates the power of deep JWST exposures in revealing the host galaxies of historically hostless GRBs.

</details>


### [258] [Statistical properties of type-II bursts in Rapid Burster observed with Insight-HXMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02190)
*Shi-peng Wang,Long Ji*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用Insight-HXMT卫星观测数据，在中子星低质量X射线双星MXB 1730-335（快速暴源）中探测到II型X射线暴，研究了2017和2020年爆发衰减阶段的暴发特性变化（包括暴发模式切换），确认了弛豫振荡行为（E∝Δt^α），其中模式-2暴发的α≈1，模式-1暴发的E-Δt关系可用分段幂律描述，且峰值流量F_peak在临界值以下与E相关但与Δt无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测和研究MXB 1730-335中II型X射线暴的特性，特别是不同暴发模式及其在爆发衰减阶段的变化规律，以深入理解中子星X射线双星的吸积不稳定性机制。

Method: 利用Insight-HXMT卫星对MXB 1730-335的2017和2020年爆发进行观测，统计分析了暴发参数（峰值流量F_peak、流量E、暴发持续时间t_dur、复发时间Δt）的分布及其相关性，并采用分段幂律模型拟合E-Δt关系。

Result: 确认了弛豫振荡行为（E∝Δt^α），模式-2暴发的α≈1；模式-1暴发的E-Δt关系可用分段幂律模型描述，α随Δt变化；F_peak在临界值以下与E相关，但与Δt无关。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了弛豫振荡模型对II型X射线暴的适用性，揭示了不同暴发模式间的转换规律及其与吸积状态演化的关联，为进一步理解中子星X射线双星的物理过程提供了观测依据。

Abstract: We report the detection of type-II X-ray bursts in a neutron star low mass X-ray binary MXB 1730-335 (also known as Rapid Burster) observed with Insight- HXMT satellite. We found significant variations in the burst properties along with the decay phases of its 2017 and 2020 outbursts, such as switches between different burst modes.We investigated the statistical distributions of burst parameters (peak flux $F_{\mathrm{peak}}$, fluence $E$, burst duration $t_{\mathrm{dur}}$, recurrence time $Δt$) and studied their correlations.We confirm the relaxation oscillator behavior ($E \propto Δt^α$), and find that for mode-2 bursts the index $α$ is around 1. For mode-1 bursts, $α$ varies with $Δt$ and the $E-Δt^α$ relation can be described by a broken powerlaw model.We also confirm that $F_{\mathrm{peak}}$ is correlated with $E$ below a critical value and is independent of $Δt$.

</details>


### [259] [The First Detection of Sub-Populations in the Delay-Time Distribution of Binary Black Holes in GWTC-4 of LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02318)
*Shaunak Padhyegurjar,Suvodip Mukherjee*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA第四次引力波目录（GWTC-4）数据，测量了双黑洞（BBH）并合时间延迟分布与源性质（质量、质量比、自旋）之间的相关性，识别出三个具有不同并合率行为的子群，并排除了所有BBH具有统一并合率的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同形成通道的双黑洞在并合时间延迟分布及源性质（质量、质量比、自旋等）上留下不同印记，可能存在延迟时间分布与致密天体源性质之间的潜在关联，但目前尚无对此特征的首次测量。

Method: 利用GWTC-4引力波目录数据，分析BBH并合事件的时间延迟分布与源性质（质量、质量比、自旋）之间的相关性，识别不同的子群及其并合率行为。

Result: 识别出三个具有不同并合率行为的子群；质量超过45 M☉的源其延迟时间分布显著不同于较低质量源，且与质量比和自旋强相关；接近相等质量和接近零有效自旋的GW源延迟更长；三个子群在z=0处的并合率从~0.6到12 Gpc⁻³ yr⁻¹不等。

Conclusion: 排除了所有BBH具有统一并合率的假设，证实了延迟时间分布与源性质之间存在依赖关系，为区分BBH不同形成通道提供了观测依据。

Abstract: The imprint of different formation channels of binary black holes (BBHs) is encoded in the distribution of time delays between BBH mergers and the formation of their progenitor stars, along with their source properties such as component mass, mass-ratio, spin, and more. This makes it possible for the presence of a potential correlation between the delay-time distribution and compact-object source properties. We report the first measurement of this inevitable signature from the fourth gravitational wave (GW) catalog (GWTC-4) of LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA and identified three sub-populations that show distinct merger rate behavior as a consequence of this. We find that the delay-time distribution of the sources above a mass of $45$ M$_\odot$ is significantly different from the ones below and exhibits strong dependence on the mass-ratio and spin, indicating that GW sources close to equal masses and close to zero effective spin are more delayed in comparison to the values otherwise. Our analysis identifies the presence of at least three source property dependent sub-population of merger rates with the merger rate at redshift $z=0$ varying from $\sim 0.6- 12$ Gpc$^{-3}$ yr$^{-1}$ for the three different sub-populations and hence rule out a Universal merger rate for all the BBHs detected using GW.

</details>


### [260] [Regression on Regression: Mapping Data-Driven Binary Black Hole Merger Rate Fits to Progenitor Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02431)
*Emma Blanchet,Aryanna Schiebelbein-Zwack,Maya Fishbach*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出“回归上回归”框架，将物理驱动的双黑洞并合率模型映射到数据驱动的B-Spline拟合结果上，避免重新分析引力波事件数据。通过拟合最小延迟时间、延迟时间幂律指数和前身形成率参数，发现物理模型无法同时匹配四个锚点，揭示了模型设定错误。前身形成率在低红移处比全局恒星形成率陡峭约5.3倍，支持低金属丰度环境偏好，但最终需要更复杂的唯象模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞并合率由前身形成率和延迟时间分布共同决定，但这些函数约束很差。现有方法要么计算负担重，要么无法揭示模型与数据之间的潜在矛盾。作者希望建立一种既能进行物理解释、又能暴露模型设定错误的高效框架。

Method: 提出“回归上回归”（regression on regression）方法：将物理驱动模型的参数直接映射到现有数据驱动的B-Spline并合率后验分布上，而不重新分析原始引力波事件数据。使用四个锚点（红移点）来拟合最小延迟时间τ_min、延迟时间幂律指数α，以及前身形成率的归一化A、早期增长γ和晚期衰减δ参数。

Result: 锚点从两个增加到四个时，中位平方和误差减少约4.5倍。但残差显示物理模型无法通过所有四个锚点，暴露了模型设定错误。前身形成率在低红移处形状稳健，其对数空间斜率在z=0.1到1.0之间比全局恒星形成率陡峭约5.3倍。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效暴露标准推断方法所掩盖的模型与数据之间的张力；前身形成率支持低金属丰度环境的偏好；当前物理模型不足以匹配B-Spline并合率，需要更复杂的唯象模型。

Abstract: The binary black hole (BBH) merger rate is governed by the progenitor formation rate and the distribution of delay-times between formation and merger, but these functions remain poorly constrained. We introduce a framework that maps the parameters of physics-driven models directly onto existing data-driven fits of the BBH merger rate. This ``regression on regression'' approach enables physical interpretation of flexible population models without the computational burden of reanalyzing the underlying gravitational-wave event data. Applying this method to the \textsc{B-Spline} merger-rate posteriors from the Fourth Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog, we fit the minimum delay time ($τ_{\text{min}}$), delay-time power-law index ($α$), and progenitor formation parameters controlling the normalization ($\mathcal{A}$), early-time growth ($γ$), and late-time decay ($δ$). Increasing the number of anchoring redshift points from two to four reduces the median sum-squared error (SSE) by a factor of $\approx 4.5$. However, residuals reveal that the physical model does not pass through all four anchors, exposing model misspecification and demonstrating a key strength of the framework: unlike standard inference methods, which preferentially weight compatible curves and mask underlying tensions, our approach exposes BBH posteriors irreconcilable with the model. Despite uncertainties at $z\gtrsim1$, the shape of the progenitor formation rate at low-$z$ is robust and evolves more steeply than the global star formation rate (SFR), supporting a preference for low metallicity environments. Specifically, the log-space slope of the progenitor rate is $\approx 5.3$ times steeper than the SFR between $z=0.1$ and $z=1.0$. Ultimately, a more complex phenomenological model is required to match the \textsc{B-Spline} merger rates.

</details>
