### [261] [Photostationary Lifshitz transition in High Tc superconductor Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+δ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00185)
*Ji Dai,Lukas Hellbrück,Michele Puppin,Alberto Crepaldi,Francesco Barantani,Thomas LaGrange,Arnaud Magrez,Edoardo Martino,Neven Barisic,László Forró,J. Hugo Dil,Marco Grioni,Henrik M. Rønnow,Siham Benhabib,Fabrizio Carbone*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发现超快光激发可以驱动Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+δ高温超导体的电子能带结构进入光稳态的长寿命激发态，在高泵浦通量下发生Fermi面的Lifshitz相变（空穴型→电子型拓扑转变），该过程与化学掺杂效应高度类似，揭示了光掺杂机制涉及电荷转移、电子关联重整化和缺陷辅助电荷捕获等协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上主要通过化学掺杂或磁场调控高温铜氧化物超导体的稳态电子能带结构，本文旨在探索利用超快光激发作为替代手段，实现对电子能带结构的非平衡态调控，并研究其与化学掺杂的类比性。

Method: 采用时间分辨和角度分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES），结合单能带紧束缚模型计算，分析1.6 eV光激发下Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+δ的能带结构演化过程。

Result: 高泵浦通量下Fermi面发生Lifshitz相变，拓扑结构由空穴型转变为电子型；光激发导致的能带结构演化与化学掺杂效应高度类似。

Conclusion: 光掺杂机制涉及电荷转移、有效电子关联重整化及缺陷辅助电荷捕获等协同效应；超快光控手段可通过调节泵浦通量进入相图中原本不可及的参数区域，同时对铜氧化物中与激光重复周期可比拟的热化过程提出了基础性问题。

Abstract: To date, controlling the steady-state electronic band structure in high-Tc cuprate superconductors has been achieved primarily through chemical doping or magnetic fields. Here, we present that ultrafast optical excitation can instead drive the electronic band structure of Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+δ into a photostationary, long-lived excited state. At sufficiently high pump fluences, this state undergoes a Lifshitz transition of the Fermi surface, characterized by a change in topology from hole-like to electron-like. Time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, supported by single-band tight-binding calculations, reveals that 1.6 eV photoexcitation induces band-structure evolutions closely analogous to those produced by chemical doping. These results point to an efficient photodoping mechanism involving cooperative effects, including charge transfer, renormalization of effective electronic correlations, and defect-assisted charge trapping. Our findings raise fundamental questions regarding thermalization processes occurring on timescales comparable to the laser repetition period in cuprates. More broadly, ultrafast optical control enables access to otherwise inaccessible regions of the phase diagram by tuning the pump fluence.

</details>


### [262] [Vestigial Nematic Order at Zero Temperature in Two-Dimensional Frustrated Quantum Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00211)
*Matthew C. O'Brien,Eduardo Fradkin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用基于连续非线性sigma模型的大N技术，半经典地研究了二维量子J1-J3海森堡反铁磁体的相图，发现此前被忽略的相互作用对稳定残留向列相（有限温度和零温下）至关重要，并揭示了全局对称性自发破缺受红外量子涨落强度控制，该涨落因靠近经典Lifshitz点而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子J1-J3海森堡反铁磁体在方格子上的相图是一个长期悬而未决的问题，尽管数值技术有所进步，但缺乏详细的解析理论。

Method: 采用基于连续非线性sigma模型有效场论的半经典方法，运用非微扰大N技术来绘制相图并确定磁关联。

Result: 发现此前被忽略的相互作用对稳定残留向列相（有限温度和零温下）至关重要；全局对称性自发破缺受红外量子涨落强度控制，该涨落因靠近经典Lifshitz点而增强。

Conclusion: 揭示了J1-J3模型中对称性破缺的机制，为理解该长期悬而未决的相图问题提供了解析理论框架。

Abstract: The phase diagram of the two-dimensional quantum $J_1$-$J_3$ Heisenberg antiferromagnet on a square lattice is a long-standing open problem. Despite recent advances in numerical techniques for quantum spin models, a detailed analytical theory is still lacking. We address this problem using a semiclassical approach based on a continuum nonlinear sigma model effective field theory, applying the nonperturbative large-$N$ technique to map out the phase diagram and determine the magnetic correlations. We show that previously-overlooked interactions are crucial for stabilizing a vestigial nematic phase, both at finite and zero temperature. Our results reveal that the spontaneous breaking of global symmetries in the $J_1$-$J_3$ model is controlled by the strength of infrared quantum fluctuations which are enhanced by proximity to the classical Lifshitz point.

</details>


### [263] [Extracting central charge from ground-state overlaps of spatially deformed Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00214)
*Chen Bai,Xinyu Sun,Liang-Hong Mo,Hong-Hao Tu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接从基态波函数重叠中提取(1+1)维共形场论中心荷的方法，通过q-Möbius变形得到仅依赖于中心荷的精确重叠公式，并在临界量子链和Chern绝缘体边缘模中验证了该方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法提取共形场论的共形反常（中心荷）通常需要复杂的分析手段，本文旨在发展一种仅基于基态波函数重叠的简单、鲁棒的探针，以便在微观模型中直接提取共形数据。

Method: 利用q-Möbius变形构造空间共形变形，推导变形与未变形基态之间的精确重叠公式，证明其指数仅依赖于中心荷。基于此构建仅依赖于基态重叠的晶格估计器，应用于临界量子链和二维Chern绝缘体的边缘模。

Result: 数值结果表明，该方法能够简单且鲁棒地探测微观模型中的中心荷。变形基态在纠缠谱和纠缠熵中保留了普遍的几何结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为从波函数角度探测临界系统和拓扑边缘模中的共形数据提供了一条简洁有效的途径。

Abstract: We show that the conformal anomaly of a $(1+1)$-dimensional conformal field theory can be extracted directly from a ground-state wave-function overlap associated with a spatial conformal deformation. Focusing on the $q$-Möbius deformation, we derive an exact overlap formula between the deformed and undeformed ground states, whose exponent depends only on the central charge. Motivated by this result, we construct a lattice estimator based solely on ground-state overlaps and apply it to representative critical quantum chains and the gapless edge modes of a two-dimensional Chern insulator. Numerical results demonstrate that the resulting overlaps provide a simple and robust probe of the central charge in microscopic models. We further demonstrate that the deformed ground states retain universal geometric structures in their entanglement spectra and entanglement entropies. These results provide a simple wave-function-based route to probing conformal data in critical systems and topological edge modes.

</details>


### [264] [Electron vs. hole doping in infinite-layer nickelates: electronic structure, magnetism and correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00223)
*Ezra Day-Roberts,Fabio Bernardini,Harrison LaBollita,Yi-Feng Zhao,Andres Cano,Antia S. Botana*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过DFT+DMFT方法研究无限层镍氧化物RNiO₂在空穴掺杂和电子掺杂两种区域下的电子结构不对称性：空穴掺杂时R(5d)态对Ni-d_{x^2-y^2}带的自掺杂效应被强烈抑制，而电子掺杂则增加R(5d)电子口袋但无法有效对Ni-d_{x^2-y^2}带进行空穴掺杂；这导致反铁磁序在空穴掺杂时迅速被抑制，而在电子掺杂时仍保持基态；但两种掺杂区域下电子关联均由Ni d_{x^2-y^2}轨道主导，表明单带描述可能适用于两种掺杂区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未掺杂无限层镍酸盐RNiO₂中超导现象的发现挑战了现有认知，有必要重新审视该材料家族的电子结构，特别是从已广泛研究的空穴掺杂区域扩展到几乎未探索的电子掺杂区域。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论（DFT）和动力学平均场理论（DMFT）研究空穴掺杂和电子掺杂两种区域下电子结构的演化。

Result: 发现了由于R(5d)态导致的Ni-d_{x^2-y^2}带自掺杂效应的显著不对称性：空穴掺杂下自掺杂被强烈抑制，而电子掺杂则增大R(5d)电子口袋但不有效空穴掺杂Ni-d_{x^2-y^2}带；反铁磁序在空穴掺杂下快速被抑制，而在电子掺杂下保持为基态；两种掺杂区域下电子关联均由Ni d_{x^2-y^2}轨道主导。

Conclusion: 尽管空穴掺杂和电子掺杂表现出显著的不对称性，但单带描述可能对两种掺杂区域的无限层镍酸盐都适用。

Abstract: The observation of superconductivity in undoped infinite-layer nickelates $R$NiO$_2$ ($R$ = rare earth) challenges our current understanding and calls for a re-examination of the underlying electronic structure of this family of materials. In this context, it is particularly important to extend the investigation of $R$NiO$_2$ compounds from the intensively studied hole-doped regime to the almost unexplored electron-doped one. Here, we use a combination of density-functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory to study the evolution of the electronic structure of infinite-layer nickelates in these two doping regimes. We find a striking asymmetry in the self-doping of the Ni-$d_{x^2-y^2}$ band due to the $R(5d)$ states: while this effect is strongly suppressed upon hole doping, electron doping instead leads to an increase in the size of the $R(5d)$ electron pockets, but without effectively hole-doping the Ni-$d_{x^2-y^2}$ band. This asymmetry has an important impact on the magnetic response as antiferromagnetism is rapidly suppressed upon hole doping, whereas it remains the ground state upon electron doping. Despite these differences, electronic correlations on both sides of the phase diagram are dominated by the Ni $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital, suggesting that a single-band description may be appropriate for infinite-layer nickelates in both the electron- and hole-doped regimes.

</details>


### [265] [Imaging the Magnetically Driven Reconstruction of the Electronic States in the Antiferromagnetic Topological Insulator EuSn$_2$As$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00337)
*Luka Khizanishvili,Erekle Jmukhadze,Anika Raisa,Divyanshi Sar,Mingda Gong,Tetiana Romanova,Dariusz Kaczorowski,Wei-Cheng Lee,Pegor Aynajian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过变温STM/STS和DFT计算，研究了反铁磁拓扑绝缘体EuSn$_2$As$_2$在Néel转变前后的局域电子结构，发现了两种磁驱动能隙（~100 meV费米能级附近能隙和~50 meV狄拉克点能隙），前者源于反铁磁布里渊区折叠与杂化，后者与Sn空位破坏镜面对称性导致狄拉克点质量打开有关，为实现轴子绝缘体器件提供了实验依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁拓扑绝缘体中实现轴子绝缘体相常受到保护无能隙表面态的晶体对称性的阻碍，因此需要在实空间层面理解反铁磁序与拓扑电子态的耦合机制。

Method: 采用变温扫描隧道显微镜(STM)和扫描隧道谱(STS)，结合密度泛函理论(DFT)计算，研究EuSn$_2$As$_2$的(001)表面在Néel温度(24 K)上下的局域电子结构变化。

Result: 观察到大量本征Sn空位引起的纳米级电子不均匀性和p型掺杂；在冷却至T_N以下时，出现两种磁驱动能隙：费米能级附近~100 meV能隙和ARPES观测到的狄拉克点处~50 meV能隙。

Conclusion: ~100 meV能隙归因于反铁磁布里渊区折叠杂化；~50 meV能隙的特征指向Sn空位破坏镜面对称性从而导致狄拉克点质量打开，但不能完全排除反铁磁折叠杂化的贡献。结果揭示了局域磁矩与巡游拓扑态之间的强耦合，表明EuSn$_2$As$_2$是制造轴子绝缘体器件的潜在候选材料。

Abstract: The realization of the axion insulator phase in magnetic topological insulators is often hindered by crystalline symmetries that protect gapless surface states, even when time-reversal symmetry is broken. Here, we use variable-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) and spectroscopy (STS), complemented with density functional theory (DFT), to investigate the local electronic structure of the antiferromagnetic (AFM) topological insulator EuSn$_2$As$_2$ across its Néel transition at $T_N = 24$ K. On the (001) surface, we observe a substantial density of intrinsic Sn vacancies that introduce nanoscale electronic inhomogeneity and p-type doping. Upon cooling below $T_N$, we resolve the emergence of two distinct magnetically driven gaps: a $\sim$100 meV gap near the Fermi level and a $\sim$50 meV gap at the ARPES-resolved Dirac point. We attribute the former gap to AFM Brillouin-zone folding and hybridization. The characteristics of the 50 meV gap point toward the lifting of mirror-symmetry protection by Sn vacancies and the consequent mass gapping of the Dirac point, although contributions from AFM-induced folding hybridization cannot be entirely ruled out. Our findings provide real-space evidence for strong coupling between localized moments and itinerant topological states, highlighting exfoliable EuSn$_2$As$_2$ as a potential candidate for realizing axion-insulator-based devices.

</details>


### [266] [Magnetic self-frustration from spontaneous structural distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00339)
*T. Vignau Costa,G. L. Rossini,D. C. Cabra,S. A. Grigera,R. A. Borzi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出"磁自阻挫"概念，通过蒙特卡洛模拟发现，具有最近邻伊辛铁磁相互作用的kagomé晶格在中等磁弹耦合强度下会发生磁结构相变，进入一种呼吸相，其中等边三角形自发畸变为等腰三角形。该相可映射为反铁磁三角格子，保持无序和残余熵，大小约为Wannier残余熵的三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示与常见磁弹耦合导致有序化相反的现象——磁自阻挫，即原本简单的铁磁相互作用在磁弹耦合下反而产生阻挫和无序态。

Method: 建立最小经典模型，采用蒙特卡洛模拟，并对磁弹耦合常数α进行一阶解析分析。

Result: kagomé晶格在中等α值下出现呼吸相：等边三角形自发畸变为等腰三角形，且形状具有鲁棒性；磁学部分映射为反铁磁三角格子，保持无序，残余熵为Wannier熵的三分之一。

Conclusion: 磁弹耦合可以导致"磁自阻挫"，产生无序相而非有序相，该现象在弱耦合（铁磁有序）和强耦合（有序二聚化反铁磁相）之间出现。

Abstract: In frustrated magnetism, lattice distortions mediated by magnetoelastic coupling are commonly invoked as an escape route from extensive degeneracy toward an ordered ground state and, in some cases, the onset of multiferroicity. Here we present a minimal classical model that illustrates the converse phenomenon, which we term ``magnetic self-frustration''. Monte Carlo simulations reveal that a kagomé lattice with trivial magnetic interactions -- namely, nearest-neighbor Ising ferromagnetism -- undergoes a magnetostructural transition into a breathing-like phase, characterized by irregular bond dimerization along the three kagomé directions. Structurally, the equilateral triangles belonging to one of the two kagomé sublattices spontaneously distort, expanding into isosceles triangles. An analysis to first order in the magnetoelastic coupling constant $α$ shows that the shape of these triangles is remarkably robust. Acting like rigid building blocks in a puzzle, their vertices determine the geometry of the second sublattice, giving rise to contracted ferromagnetic triangles with a variety of shapes. The magnetic sector can be mapped onto an effective antiferromagnetic triangular lattice, which remains disordered down to low temperatures and retains a finite residual entropy of one third of that of Wannier. This self-frustrated phase takes place at intermediate values of $α$, separating the conventional undistorted ferromagnetic phase at weak coupling from a strongly coupled ordered phase characterized by a regular dimerized up-up-down-down antiferromagnetic pattern along the three kagomé directions, built from ferromagnetic triangles and hexagons.

</details>


### [267] [Surface Reconstruction in Low-Temperature ARPES Spectra of Charge-Ordered EuAl$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00453)
*Hao Liu,Bo Chen,Chen Zhang,Qi-Yi Wu,Sheng-Tao Cui,Zhe Sun,Zhong-Tuo Fu,Ying Zhou,Yang Luo,Jun Liu,Yu-Xia Duan,Jian-Qiao Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过光子能量、温度和解理历史依赖的ARPES实验与第一性原理计算相结合，发现EuAl₄低温ARPES谱中额外的电子型能带和费米面轮廓主要源于热敏性表面重构，而非体相电荷密度波引起的能带折叠，澄清了该材料中关于电荷有序起源的关键争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EuAl₄中电荷有序与能带重构、磁性及拓扑电子态的关联已被广泛讨论，但低温ARPES谱的微观起源尚不明确，需要区分体相本征带与表面衍生谱权重。

Method: 采用光子能量依赖、温度依赖和解理历史依赖的ARPES实验，结合第一性原理计算，对比高温解理（160 K冷却至10 K）与低温解理表面的电子结构，分析表面衍生特征与体相能带的差异。

Result: 高温解理表面的谱与计算的三维体相电子结构吻合；低温解理表面出现额外的电子型能带、类复制的费米面轮廓及近kz无关的表面衍生特征，这些特征在升温时逐渐被抑制，而体相能带在电荷密度波转变前后基本不变。

Conclusion: 低温ARPES谱中的额外特征主要源于热敏性表面重构，而非体相CDW引起的能带折叠，为BaAl₄家族关联半金属中区分表面重构与体相电荷有序效应提供了光谱学判据。

Abstract: Charge ordering in EuAl$_4$ has been widely discussed in connection with band reconstruction, magnetism,and topological electronic states, yet the microscopic origin of the complex low-temperature ARPES spectra remains unresolved. Here we combine photon-energy-, temperature-, and cleavage-history-dependent ARPES with firstprinciples calculations to separate intrinsic bulk bands from surface-derived spectral weight. Spectra measured on high-temperature-cleaved surfaces, both at 160 K and after cooling to 10 K, are well described by the calculated three-dimensional bulk electronic structure, whereas low-temperature-cleaved surfaces exhibit additional electron-like bands, replica-like Fermi-surface contours, and nearly kz-independent surface-derived features that are absent from the bulk calculations. These extra features are progressively suppressed upon warming, whereas the bulk-derived bands remain largely unchanged across the charge-density-wave transition within the experimental resolution. The combined analysis of photon-energy dependence, temperature evolution, and cleavage history identifies the reconstructed low-temperature spectral weight as arising predominantly from a thermally fragile surface reconstruction rather than from intrinsic bulk CDW-induced band folding. These results resolve an important ambiguity in EuAl$_4$ and provide spectroscopic criteria for distinguishing surface reconstruction from bulk charge-order effects in BaAl$_4$-family correlated semimetals.

</details>


### [268] [Complexity of tensor network simulation for noisy quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00474)
*Yuguo Shao,Zishuo Zhao,Song Cheng,Zhengwei Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了局部噪声如何影响基于张量网络方法的量子动力学的经典可模拟性。通过算子纠缠熵(OEE)证明：(1)单比特去极化噪声下，任意电路中poly(n)键维度的张量网络可在固定绝对误差下模拟O(1)深度，相对误差需O(log n)深度且最优；(2)1D局域电路中存在全轨迹误差有界的poly(n)键维MPO；(3)1D砖墙电路中特定收缩系数的随机双设计门以高概率产生O(1)OEE平台，任意门在最坏情况下产生O(log n)OEE；(4)高维中这些界给出均匀深度的poly(n)平均边界键维PEPO。建立了噪声模型、电路类型与经典可模拟性之间的严格联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立局部噪声如何影响基于张量网络方法的量子动力学经典可模拟性的严格理论界限，填补该领域缺乏严格证明的空白。

Method: 利用算子纠缠熵(OEE)作为核心分析工具，针对不同噪声模型(单比特去极化噪声、一般单比特噪声)、不同电路结构(任意电路、1D局域/砖墙电路、高维电路)和不同精度要求(绝对误差、相对误差)进行严格的数学证明。

Result: (1)任意电路中poly(n)键维张量网络在固定绝对误差下可模拟O(1)深度，相对误差需O(log n)深度且最优；(2)1D局域电路中存在全轨迹误差有界的poly(n)键维MPO；(3)1D砖墙电路中特定条件下OEE以高概率或最坏情况下有界；(4)高维中给出均匀深度的poly(n)平均边界键维PEPO。

Conclusion: 建立了噪声模型、电路类型与经典可模拟性之间的严格数学联系，为量子优势的边界提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We aim to rigorously address how local noise affects classical simulability of quantum dynamics benchmarked by tensor-network methods. Using operator entanglement entropy (OEE), we prove the following: (1) For single-qubit depolarizing noise on arbitrary circuits, tensor networks with $\mathrm{poly}(n)$ bond dimension suffice for fixed absolute Hilbert-Schmidt error after $\order{1}$ depth, while relative error demands $\order{\log n}$ depth; and this bound is optimal. (2) For single-qubit depolarizing noise on 1D local circuits, the existence of whole-trajectory error-bounded matrix product operator (MPO) of $\mathrm{poly}(n)$ bond dimension at all depths. (3) For general single-qubit noise in 1D brickwall circuits, random two-design gates with contraction coefficient $c<1/3$ yield an $\order{1}$ OEE plateau with probability $1-Te^{-Ω(n)}$, while arbitrary gates with $c<1/48$ give $\order{\log n}$ OEE in the worst case. (4) In higher dimensions, these bounds yield uniform-in-depth $\mathrm{poly}(n)$ average boundary-bond dimensions for projected entangled pair operators~(PEPO) across every cut -- under depolarizing noise at either absolute or relative accuracy, and under general noise with strong contraction at absolute accuracy. Our results establish a rigorous connection between certain noise models, circuit types, and their classical simulability.

</details>


### [269] [Supersymmetric quantum criticality with discrete symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00657)
*Teng-Yue Wang,Shuai Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了(2+1)维Gross-Neveu-Yukawa理论中，离散Z_n各向异性对涌现超对称性的影响。通过泛函重整化群分析发现，当n>3时各向异性扰动在不动点处是无关的，产生N=2 Wess-Zumino超对称临界点。在有序相中，这种危险无关各向异性产生了第二个特征长度尺度ξ'，并提取了其临界指数，支持标度关系ν'/ν = 1+|y_n|/p (p=2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态系统中涌现的超对称性通常在具有连续对称性的模型中讨论，但真实材料受限于微观离散对称性。因此，作者希望在具有离散Z_n各向异性的系统中研究涌现超对称性是否仍然存在。

Method: 采用泛函重整化群方法，分析(2+1)维Gross-Neveu-Yukawa理论中耦合Dirac费米子与具有离散Z_n各向异性的复序参量的临界行为。

Result: 当n>3时各向异性扰动在不动点处是无关的，系统呈现N=2 Wess-Zumino超对称临界点；在有序相中，危险无关各向异性产生第二个特征长度尺度ξ'；对于Z_4、Z_5、Z_6模型，结果支持标度关系ν'/ν=1+|y_n|/p (p=2)。

Conclusion: 离散Z_n对称性不会破坏n>3时临界点处的超对称性，但会在有序相中引入额外的标度行为，其标度关系与理论预期一致。

Abstract: Supersymmetry, originally proposed in high-energy physics, can emerge as a remarkable low-energy structure in condensed matter systems. While emergent supersymmetry at quantum critical points is widely discussed in models with continuous symmetries, real materials are constrained by microscopic discrete symmetries. To address this, we investigate (2+1)-dimensional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa theories coupling Dirac fermions to a complex order parameter with discrete $Z_n$ anisotropy. Using the functional renormalization group, we find that for $n>3$, the anisotropic perturbations are irrelevant at the fixed point, yielding a $\mathcal{N}=2$ Wess-Zumino supersymmetric critical point. In the ordered phase, this dangerously irrelevant anisotropy gives rise to a second characteristic length scale, $ξ'$, alongside the usual correlation length, $ξ$. By tracking mass thresholds along symmetry-broken renormalization group trajectories, we extract the exponents $ν'$ and $ν$ without imposing prior scaling assumptions. For the $Z_4$, $Z_5$, and $Z_6$ models, our results support the scaling relation $ν'/ν= 1+|y_n|/p$ with $p=2$ in the isotropic framework used here.

</details>


### [270] [Magnon bandstructure and topology in a periodically deformed Kagome lattice with DM interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00696)
*Mohammed Abdullah Hammadi,Mohammed Salman Alsadah,Husam Abdulmajeed Noorwli,Hocine Bahlouli,Michael Vogl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有DM相互作用的Heisenberg模型在变形Kagome晶格上的磁振子能带结构与拓扑性质。通过周期性变形获得六方对称性和扩大的原胞，相比于未变形情况产生了更丰富的能带结构。反铁磁耦合情况下，自旋空间对称性导致拓扑平庸态；铁磁耦合情况下，自发对称性破缺导致丰富拓扑特征，观察到从+2到-2的陈数，证明了变形磁体可产生新奇的拓扑物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索变形Kagome晶格中磁振子的拓扑性质，研究晶格变形对磁振子能带结构和拓扑相变的影响，寻找新的拓扑磁子系统。

Method: 基于具有DM相互作用的Heisenberg模型，在周期性变形的六方对称Kagome晶格（扩大的原胞）上，分别研究反铁磁和铁磁耦合情况下的磁振子能带结构，通过计算陈数分析拓扑性质。

Result: 反铁磁耦合下经典基态具有自旋空间对称性，导致拓扑平庸态；铁磁耦合下该对称性被自发破缺，观察到多次拓扑相变和陈数从+2到-2的丰富拓扑能带。

Conclusion: 变形Kagome晶格中的铁磁耦合系统能产生比未变形情况更丰富的磁振子拓扑结构，变形磁体是探索新奇拓扑物理的有效平台。

Abstract: We study the band structure and topology of magnons for a Heisenberg model with DM interaction on a deformed Kagome lattice. For simplicity, we focus on a periodically deformed lattice with hexagonal symmetry and an enlarged unit cell. This enlarged unit cell gives rise to a richer band structure than in the undeformed case. Analyzing band topology, there is a distinction between the topologically trivial case with anti-ferromagnetic coupling and the topologically rich case with ferromagnetic coupling. In the anti-ferromagnetic case, a spin-space symmetry, also present in the classical ground state, enforces these topologically trivial states. In the ferromagnetic case, this symmetry is spontaneously broken by the classical ground state. Consequently, the band structure also hosts rich topological features. Specifically, we observe many topological transitions and bands with Chern numbers ranging from $+2$ to $-2$, which makes the system richer than in the undeformed case. This emergent rich topological structure demonstrates that deformed magnets can host new and exciting physics.

</details>


### [271] [Low-Temperature Suppression of Intertwined Orders in La$_{1/3}$Sr$_{2/3}$FeO$_{3}$ Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00769)
*Mayia A. Vranas,Robin Glefke,Katherine Matthews,Xiaoke Li,Tianxing Wang,Henry Navarro,I-Ching Lin,Sarmistha Das,Biswajit Sahoo,Rourav Basak,Wei He,Holden Bauer,Ella Di Liberti,Jacob Butler,Elliot Kisiel,Erik Lamb,Jun-Sik Lee,Cheng-Tai Kuo,Heemin Lee,Christie Nelson,Sophie Morley,Sujoy Roy,Christoph Klewe,Fanny Rodolakis,Jessica McChesney,Oleg Shpyrko,Yimei Zhu,Eric Fullerton,Ivan Schuller,Alex Frano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了La$_{1/3}$Sr$_{2/3}$FeO$_{3}$ (LSFO)薄膜在低温(~25 K以下)的行为，发现其已知的反铁磁序和电荷序被完全抑制，并通过X射线散射和光谱技术分析了这一新低温相的电子结构和磁畴动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSFO薄膜在~100 K以下的低温行为尚未被深入研究，而该材料在~190 K附近表现出耦合的顺磁-反铁磁相变和电荷有序相变，低温区域探索的缺失限制了对耦合序体系中竞争相互作用的理解。

Method: 采用共振X射线散射、光谱学以及相干散射测量技术，直接探测LSFO薄膜在低温下的磁序和电荷序，分析电子结构和畴动力学。

Result: 在~25 K以下观察到LSFO已知的反铁磁序和电荷序被完全抑制，并获得了该低温相中电子结构和畴动力学的新信息。

Conclusion: 基于薄膜中畴的维度降低提出了解释序抑制的可能机制，揭示了强关联材料中耦合序体系内竞争相互作用的影响。

Abstract: The strong coupling between spin, charge, and lattice degrees of freedom in perovskite oxides leads to an array of exotic phenomena, giving these materials rich phase diagrams that can include coupled orders. This is exemplified by the A-site doped ferrite La$_{1/3}$Sr$_{2/3}$FeO$_{3}$ (LSFO), which exhibits a coupled paramagnetic-antiferromagnetic and charge ordering phase transition at $\sim$190 K that has been well studied in thin films, bulk, and polycrystalline samples. However, the low temperature behavior of LSFO thin films below $\sim$100 K has not been thoroughly explored. This work uses several X-ray scattering and spectroscopy techniques to directly probe LSFO's magnetic and charge order down to low temperature. Using resonant X-ray scattering, we observe a complete suppression of LSFO's known antiferromagnetic and charge order below $\sim$25 K. Further spectroscopy and coherent scattering measurements provide insight into LSFO's electronic structure and domain dynamics in this new low temperature phase, and we propose possible explanations for the observed order suppression based on reduced dimensionality of domains in our thin films. Our findings provide insight into the effects of competing interactions in strongly correlated materials, particularly those with coupled orders.

</details>


### [272] [Locality-Induced Hierarchical Backflow Wavefunctions for Correlated Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00924)
*Yu-Tong Zhou,Zheng-Wei Zhou,Wen-Yuan Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出层级反流（HB）波函数，利用局域性原则对反流波函数进行层次化组织，通过路径深度K系统提升表达能力。在费米子系统中，HB在$4\times4$至$10\times10$半填充系统中达到约0.5%的精度，在$n_h=0.125$空穴掺杂下可高效扩展至$12\times16$和$16\times16$系统，并清晰呈现条幅相。HB还支持局域-非局域分解，可自然衔接神经量子态，兼具紧凑表示与高效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索局域性作为反流波函数层次化组织的自然原则，以解决现有反流波函数在可解释性、系统可改进性和大规模模拟效率方面的不足。

Method: 提出层级反流（HB）波函数族，通过路径深度K控制反流关联范围，实现表达能力的系统可改进性；引入局域-非局域分解策略，连接神经量子态表示。

Result: 半填充时K=1的HB在$4\times4$至$10\times10$系统中精度约0.5%；$n_h=0.125$时空穴掺杂下可扩展至$12\times16$和$16\times16$，随K增加能量精度系统提升，清晰呈现条幅相。

Conclusion: 局域性是反流波函数的自然组织原则，HB框架为关联费米子系统的可解释大规模模拟提供了系统可改进的新途径。

Abstract: We show that locality provides a natural principle to hierarchically organize backflow wavefunctions. This leads us to propose a family of variational fermionic states, termed hierarchical backflow (HB) wavefunctions. The expressive power of HB is systematically improvable, controlled by a path depth $K$ which reflects the range of backflow correlations. At half-filling, the HB with $K=1$ already achieves high energy precision, with an accuracy around $0.5\%$ for system sizes from $4\times 4$ to $10\times 10$. At hole doping $n_h=0.125$, the method scales efficiently to $12\times16$ and $16\times16$ systems, and the energy systematically achieves higher accuracy with $K$ increasing, yielding a clear stripe phase. The HB further enables a local-nonlocal decomposition, naturally bridging to neural quantum states, while featuring compact representations and efficient optimization. Our work reveals locality as a natural organizing principle of backflow wavefunctions, opening a new framework with systematic improvability and interpretability for large-scale simulations of correlated fermion systems.

</details>


### [273] [Doping- and temperature-dependent electronic structure and spin dynamics in the Hubbard model on a square lattice within cluster perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01716)
*V. I. Kuz'min,S. G. Ovchinnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用团簇微扰理论（CPT）研究方形晶格Hubbard模型的电子结构和动态自旋磁化率随掺杂和温度的变化，结果与铜氧化物高温超导体的实验数据定性一致。通过CPT得到的静态短程磁关联进行广义平均场计算，并与CPT结果比较，探讨了短程反铁磁序在赝能隙形成中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解铜氧化物高温超导体中电子结构和自旋动力学随掺杂和温度的变化规律，以及短程反铁磁关联对赝能隙形成的影响。

Method: 采用团簇微扰理论（CPT）研究Hubbard模型；利用CPT得到的静态短程磁关联进行广义平均场计算，并与CPT结果进行对比分析。

Result: 电子和自旋谱的总体特征与铜氧化物实验数据定性一致；通过比较广义平均场计算与CPT结果，揭示了短程反铁磁序在赝能隙形成中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 短程反铁磁关联是形成赝能隙的重要机制，CPT方法能有效描述铜氧化物中电子结构和自旋动力学的主要特征。

Abstract: The doping and temperature dependencies of the electronic structure and the dynamic spin susceptibility of the Hubbard model on a square lattice are studied within cluster perturbation theory (CPT). The most general features of both electronic and spin spectra qualitatively agree with the experimental data on cuprates. Generalized mean-field calculations of the electronic structure using static short-range magnetic correlations from CPT are also implemented and compared with the results of CPT, allowing us to discuss the role of short-range antiferromagnetism in the formation of the pseudogap.

</details>


### [274] [Microscopic Theory of the Phonon Thermal Hall Effect in Chiral Mott Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01876)
*Junha Kang,Taekoo Oh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了手性莫特绝缘体中声子热霍尔效应的微观理论，推导了有效拉曼相互作用的精确解析形式，揭示了其强度正比于标量自旋手性，并在Kagome晶格上证明了内禀声子热霍尔效应，建立了通过同位素替换分离声子贡献的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热霍尔效应是探测绝缘体中电荷中性激发的重要工具，但声子贡献与自旋贡献在量级上相当，现有理论无法有效区分二者。本文旨在发展声子热霍尔效应的第一性微观理论，并建立实验上分离不同载热子贡献的标准方法。

Method: 首先在半填充莫特绝缘体中推导出有效拉曼相互作用的精确解析形式；然后在Kagome晶格上显式证明内禀声子热霍尔效应；最终通过同位素替换揭示温度依赖的输运行为 crossover，并建立分离声子贡献的标度律。

Result: 得到了有效拉曼相互作用强度与标量自旋手性成正比的精确形式；在Kagome晶格上验证了内禀声子热霍尔效应；发现了同位素替换下输运行为的温度依赖交叉；建立了定量分离声子热霍尔贡献的标度律。

Conclusion: 本文不仅提供了声子热霍尔效应的首个完全微观推导，还为在手性莫特绝缘体中隔离微观载热子贡献建立了明确的实验标准。

Abstract: The thermal Hall effect (THE) probes charge-neutral excitations in insulators, where the charge gap blocks electronic transport. Recently, phonons have been shown to induce a THE comparable in magnitude to the spin contribution, underscoring their critical role in thermal transport. Here, we develop a microscopic theory of the phonon thermal Hall effect (PTHE) in chiral Mott insulators. First, we derive the exact analytic form of the effective Raman interaction in half-filled Mott insulators, showing that its strength is directly proportional to the scalar spin chirality. Next, we demonstrate the intrinsic PTHE explicitly on the kagome lattice. Crucially, our formulation reveals a temperature-dependent crossover in the transport behavior under isotopic substitution. Using this result, we establish a scaling law that quantitatively separates the phonon contribution to the THE from other background signals. Our results not only provide the first fully microscopic derivation of the PTHE, but also establish a definitive experimental standard for isolating microscopic heat carriers in chiral Mott insulators.

</details>


### [275] [Spin Dynamics from Niu-Kleinman Adiabatic Approach and Slave Boson Mean Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01984)
*Xuan Yang,Tianyang Xie,Shaohang Shi,Kun Jiang,Jiangping Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Niu-Kleinman形式与Kotliar-Ruckenstein奴隶玻色子理论（NK+KRSB）的绝热自旋动力学理论，通过约束奴隶玻色子鞍点求解，从Berry曲率矩阵和能量Hessian中提取线性化绝热运动方程，用于计算强关联材料中的自旋波激发。在单轨道半满Hubbard模型上，该方法得到的自旋波色散与行列式量子蒙特卡洛基准相比显著优于随机相位近似，接近含时Gutzwiller近似结果，并成功推广至两轨道La₂NiO₄模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波激发是探测强关联材料中磁有序和电子关联的核心手段，但传统弱耦合方法（如随机相位近似）在强关联区域精度不足，而更精确的量子蒙特卡洛等方法计算成本高昂。因此需要发展一种既能有效纳入强关联效应、又保持计算高效性的自旋动力学理论框架。

Method: 将Niu-Kleinman绝热几何形式与Kotliar-Ruckenstein奴隶玻色子理论相结合（NK+KRSB）。对每个冻结的自旋构型，自洽求解约束奴隶玻色子鞍点方程，从中提取Berry曲率矩阵和能量Hessian矩阵，代入线性化的绝热运动方程得到自旋波色散。

Result: 在半满单轨道Hubbard模型中，该方法的自旋波色散与DQMC基准的吻合度显著优于RPA，并接近含时Gutzwiller近似的结果。在La₂NiO₄两轨道模型中成功应用，证明了其对实际多轨道关联体系的适用性。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需磁基态附近的鞍点解，计算效率高，同时能纳入超越传统弱耦合描述的强关联效应，为研究关联量子材料中的低能自旋激发提供了一个实用框架。

Abstract: Spin-wave excitations provide a central probe of magnetic order and electronic correlations in strongly correlated materials. In this work, we develop an adiabatic theory of spin dynamics by combining the Niu-Kleinman formalism with Kotliar-Ruckenstein slave-boson theory (NK+KRSB). For each frozen spin configuration, the constrained slave-boson saddle point is solved self-consistently, allowing the Berry-curvature matrix and energy Hessian entering the linearized adiabatic equations of motion to be extracted directly. Applied to the half-filled single-orbital Hubbard model, the resulting spin-wave dispersion shows substantially improved agreement with determinant quantum Monte Carlo benchmarks compared with the random phase approximation and closely approaches results from the time-dependent Gutzwiller approximation. We further extend the method to a two-orbital model of $\mathrm{La}_2\mathrm{NiO}_4$, demonstrating its applicability to realistic multi-orbital correlated systems. Because the approach only requires saddle-point solutions near the magnetic ground state, it remains computationally efficient while incorporating strong-correlation effects beyond conventional weak-coupling descriptions, providing a practical framework for studying low-energy spin excitations in correlated quantum materials.

</details>


### [276] [Anisotropic Moiré Fractional Chern Insulators and Their Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02094)
*Bo Peng,Jin-Xin Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究moiré异质结构中分数陈绝缘体(FCI)相在各向异性下的稳定性，发现中等各向异性下FCI相保持鲁棒，而强各向异性会驱动从拓扑相到电荷密度波(CDW)和费米液体(FL)相的量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索moiré异质结构中分数陈绝缘体(FCI)相在各向异性下的稳定性，以及各向异性如何调控和诱导竞争电子相。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究了扭转过渡金属二硫化物(TMD)中异质应变导致的层间动量偏移所产生的各向异性效应，并分析了理想陈带模型中的晶格拉伸情况。

Result: 发现中等各向异性下FCI相保持稳定；强各向异性导致拓扑能隙抑制并引发到CDW和FL相的量子相变；各向异性可作为调控FCI态中竞争关联相的直接途径。

Conclusion: 各向异性为工程化和探索moiré材料中FCI态的竞争关联相提供了直接手段，类似于Landau能级中有效质量各向异性对分数量子霍尔态的调控作用。

Abstract: Recently, moiré heterostructures have been observed to host fractional Chern insulator (FCI) phases at zero magnetic field. In this work, we show that the FCI phases are robust against moderate lattice anisotropy, while sufficiently strong anisotropy drives quantum phase transitions from incompressible topological phases to competing charge-density-wave (CDW) and Fermi-liquid (FL) phases. Specifically, in the case of twisted transition metal dichalcogenides (such as MoTe$_2$), the anisotropy arises from an interlayer momentum shift induced by heterostrain. We find that increasing anisotropy suppresses the many-body topological gap, eventually destabilizing the fractional topological phase. Beyond a critical anisotropy value, the system gradually transitions to a symmetry-broken state characterized by stripe-like CDW order. It has a Landau Level counterpart, where effective mass anisotropy provides an additional tuning knob for the fractional quantum Hall state; sufficiently strong anisotropy drives a transition to a charge-ordered phase. Moreover, in the anisotropic ideal Chern band model with lattice stretching, a Fermi liquid phase emerges as anisotropy increases. Our results establish that anisotropy provides a direct route for engineering and exploring competing correlated phases in FCI states based on moiré materials.

</details>


### [277] [Quenching of Nonrelativistic p-Wave Spin Splitting by c-f Decoupling in CeNiAsO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02420)
*Xinnuo Zhang,Zhicheng Jiang,Shibo Shen,Jian Yuan,Junseo Yoo,Changyoung Kim,Mao Ye,Jishan Liu,Zhengtai Liu,Yanfeng Guo,Yilin Wang,Dawei Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用超高分辨率共振角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）对单晶CeNiAsO的电子结构进行了系统研究。实验发现，在反铁磁有序转变后，既未观察到自旋密度波预期能带折叠，也未观察到理论预测的p波能带分裂，导带保持完全的Kramers简并。共振ARPES显示Ce 4f电子在磁有序态下处于局域极限，不存在相干c-f杂化。结果表明，几何对称性分类是必要但不充分条件，强电子关联会阻碍实空间磁对称性向动量空间电子能带的投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋空间群对称性扩展到共面反铁磁体后，预言了奇宇称非相对论自旋劈裂的存在，使得寻找实用的p波磁体成为自旋电子学的核心目标。层状重费米子氧磷族化合物CeNiAsO因其共面共线磁构型被理论预期会产生稳健的p波能带劈裂，被认为是验证这一范式的最具代表性的平台。

Method: 采用超低温、高分辨率、共振角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）技术，对单晶CeNiAsO的电子结构进行测量。通过连续跟踪磁有序转变过程中的能带演化，并利用共振ARPES测量Ce 4f谱权重的温度依赖关系。

Result: 在磁有序相中，未观察到自旋密度波导致的能带折叠，也未检测到任何可观测的p波能带劈裂，导带保持完全的Kramers简并。共振ARPES显示Ce 4f电子在磁有序态下处于局域极限，费米能级附近不存在相干c-f杂化。

Conclusion: 实空间磁对称性向动量空间电子能带的投影受到强电子关联效应的强烈制约。几何对称性分类是理解非相对论自旋劈裂的必要框架，但在强关联体系中并非充分条件。这一发现为实际p波磁体的设计提供了重要的多体约束。

Abstract: The extending of spin-space group symmetries to coplanar antiferromagnets has predicted the emergence of odd-parity nonrelativistic spin splittings, making the identification of a practical $p$-wave magnet a central pursuit in spintronics. The layered heavy-fermion oxypnictide CeNiAsO has been widely regarded as the prototypical platform to verify this paradigm, as its commensurate coplanar magnetic configuration is theoretically expected to induce a robust $p$-wave band splitting. Here, we investigate the electronic structure of single-crystal CeNiAsO using ultra-low-temperature, high-resolution, and resonant angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). Across the consecutive magnetic transitions into the ordered phases, our spectroscopic data reveal neither the expected band folding associated with a spin density wave nor any observable $p$-wave band splitting, demonstrating that the conduction bands retain full Kramers degeneracy. By tracking the temperature dependence of the Ce 4$f$ spectral weight via resonant ARPES, we find no evidence of coherent $c-f$ hybridization near the Fermi level within the magnetically ordered states, confirming that the Ce 4$f$ electrons operate in the localized limit. Our findings establish a clear many-body constraint on projecting real-space magnetic symmetries onto momentum-space electronic bands, demonstrating that geometric symmetry classifications constitute a necessary framework but are not a sufficient condition for nonrelativistic spin splittings in the presence of strong electronic correlations.

</details>


### [278] [Suppression of p-Wave Altermagnetism by Localized 4f Electrons in CeNiAsO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02422)
*Jiuxiang Zhang,Yueyang Sun,Honglin Zhou,Jumin Shi,Di Wu,Hongze Gu,Wenjin Mao,Hengrui Dong,Yu Xu,Yinghao Li,Ziling Cao,Taimin Miao,Bo Liang,Neng Cai,Wenpei Zhu,Mingkai Xu,Jiaqi Chen,Chunhong Deng,Bo Liu,Xun Ma,Zhengtai Liu,Mao Ye,Shenjin Zhang,Zhimin Wang,Fengfeng Zhang,Feng Yang,Qinjun Peng,Zuyan Xu,Guodong Liu,Xintong Li,Hanqing Mao,Shiliang Li,Hongming Weng,Lin Zhao,X. J. Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用高分辨率角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）研究重费米子候选材料CeNiAsO中的p波交变磁性，发现尽管宏观特征与p波磁序一致，但Ni 3d导带在近费米能级处并未观测到可分辨的p波交换劈裂。结合共振光电子能谱和第一性原理计算，证明局域化的Ce 4f电子抑制了可观测的单粒子能带劈裂信号，将CeNiAsO识别为强关联f电子极限下的p波磁性体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性此前主要在弱到中等关联的d电子体系中研究，其在重费米子材料（磁交换与近藤效应相互竞争）中的表现尚不明确，因此探索交变磁性在强关联f电子体系中的体现具有重要的物理意义。

Method: 采用高分辨率ARPES测量能带结构，结合共振光电子能谱（ResPES）探测Ce 4f态性质，并通过DFT+U第一性原理计算进行理论与实验对比。

Result: 实验未观测到Ni 3d导带的p波交换劈裂；Ce 4f态保持高度局域化且c-f杂化残余；DFT+U引入局域化Ce 4f特征后能正确复现实验费米面拓扑，并将p波劈裂降至仅几meV，低于实验分辨率。

Conclusion: CeNiAsO是强关联f电子极限下的p波磁性体系，局域化4f电子抑制了弱关联图像中预期的单粒子能带劈裂信号，揭示了交变磁性在重费米子材料中的独特表现。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, characterized by momentum-dependent spin splitting and zero net magnetization, has so far been explored mainly in weakly or moderately correlated d-electron systems. How such symmetry-allowed band splitting manifests in heavy-fermion materials, where magnetic exchange competes with Kondo correlations, remains unclear. Here we use high-resolution angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy to investigate CeNiAsO, a heavy-fermion candidate for p-wave altermagnetism. Despite macroscopic signatures consistent with the proposed p-wave magnetic order, we find no resolvable near-Fermi-level p-wave exchange splitting on the Ni 3d-derived conduction bands across the Neel transitions. Fermi-surface mapping, orbital-resolved ARPES identify that the low-energy electronic structure is dominated by Ni 3d bands, while resonant photoemission reveals that the Ce 4f states remain predominantly localized with residual c-f hybridization. First-principles calculations further show that an uncorrected itinerant-4f description produces dispersive Ce 4f bands and additional Fermi-surface pockets that are absent in experiment, thereby overestimating both the low-energy c-f hybridization and the exchange splitting transferred to the Ni 3d bands. When the localized Ce 4f character is incorporated through DFT+U , the experimental Fermi-surface topology is recovered and the residual p-wave splitting on the Ni 3d-derived bands is reduced to only a few meV, below the effective experimental resolution. These results identify CeNiAsO as a strongly correlated f-electron limit of p-wave magnetism, in which localized 4f electrons suppress the observable single-particle band-splitting signature expected from a weak-correlation picture.

</details>


### [279] [Electrical observation via spin Seebeck effect of fractionalized excitations in a magnetic insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02501)
*Nan Tang,Josef Willsher,Stephan Glamsch,Aisha Aqeel,Ludwig Scheuchenpflug,Michael Schulze,Christoph Liebald,Daniel Rytz,Christo Guguschev,Manfred Albrecht,Roderich Moessner,Philipp Gegenwart*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用自旋塞贝克效应(SSE)探测非共线Ising磁体Dy₂Ti₂O₇中的涌现磁单极子，在单极子增殖处观察到显著的SSE信号峰，并呈现特征性的频率和角度依赖性，拓展了自旋电子学方法在检测奇异激发方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数化激发是量子物质中涌现现象的标志性特征，但在阻挫磁体中难以检测和表征，需要探索新的探测方法。

Method: 采用自旋塞贝克效应(SSE)作为探测工具，将热驱动的磁激发转化为邻近金属层中的电压信号，从而电学方式获取磁动力学和输运性质。

Result: 在Dy₂Ti₂O₇中观察到SSE信号在磁单极子增殖温度处出现显著峰值，并表现出特征性的频率依赖性和角度依赖性。

Conclusion: 拓展了自旋电子学方法在检测奇异激发方面的应用范围，为磁性绝缘体研究和磁单极子物理提供了新见解，并展示了量子材料作为功能界面的潜力。

Abstract: Fractionalized excitations are among the most striking signatures of emergence in quantum matter. While widely sought in frustrated magnets, their detection and characterization remain challenging, motivating the exploration of new probes. Meanwhile, Spintronics offers versatile tools for probing spin-related phenomena. In particular, the spin Seebeck effect (SSE) converts thermally driven magnetic excitations into a voltage in an adjacent metal, providing electrical access to the underlying dynamics and transport properties. Here we employ the SSE to probe emergent magnetic monopoles in the non-collinear Ising magnet Dy$_2$Ti$_2$O$_7$, a rare instance of a three-dimensional fractionalized magnet. We observe an SSE signal featuring a pronounced peak at monopole proliferation, accompanied by characteristic frequency and angular dependence. Our results broaden the scope of spintronic methods for detecting exotic excitations, provide new insights into magnetic insulators generally and monopole physics specifically, and suggest the potential of quantum materials as functional interfaces.

</details>


### [280] [Reduce dimensional quantum criticality for Non-Fermi liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02534)
*Phumudzo T. Rabambi,Mario Solís*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种降维理论框架，将玻色子和费米子置于不同空间维度（玻色子在d+1维体，费米子在d维边界），用于研究有限密度下的量子场论和非费米液体行为。该方案简化了重整化群分析，使树级玻色子交换贡献变为有限，并将1-loop四费米子相互作用的log²和log³发散降低为对数发散，显著改善了微扰展开的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限密度量子场论中的非费米液体行为，特别是量子相变附近的理论分析，传统方法中存在严重的对数发散问题，需要一种更可控的理论框架。

Method: 将玻色子和费米子置于不同空间维度：玻色子场在(d+1)维体，费米子场限制在d维边界上，利用这种维度分离来简化无隙玻色子-费米子耦合的重整化群分析。

Result: 树级玻色子交换贡献（通常具有对数发散）变得有限；1-loop四费米子相互作用中的log²和log³发散被降低为简单的对数发散，微扰展开收敛性显著改善。

Conclusion: 该降维框架为非费米液体物理提供了可控的理论分析工具，改善了微扰理论的收敛性质。

Abstract: We present a reduced dimension theoretical framework for studying quantum field theories at finite density, providing a tractable model for investigating non-Fermi liquid (NFL) behavior near quantum phase transitions. Our approach departs from the standard paradigm by placing bosons and fermions in different spatial dimensions: bosonic fields reside in a $(d+1)$-dimensional bulk, while fermionic fields are confined on a $d$-dimensional boundary. This dimensional separation significantly simplifies the renormalization group (RG) analysis of gapless boson-fermion coupling. We demonstrate that the tree-level boson exchange contributions, which typically exhibit logarithmic divergences, become finite in our reduced-dimension scheme. Furthermore, the $\log^2$ and $\log^3$ divergences that characterize 1-loop four-fermion interactions in conventional treatments are reduced to logarithmic divergences within this framework, substantially improving the convergence properties of the perturbative expansion and allowing controlled theoretical analysis of NFL physics.

</details>
